<!--
  PLACEMENT360 — CODE GUIDE
  ------------------------------------------------------------
  1. HTML  : page structure and the #app mount point.
  2. CSS   : visual design, layout and responsive/mobile rules.
  3. Firebase: authentication and Firestore connection.
  4. State : single JavaScript object that stores the current app state.
  5. Data  : branch roles, diagnostic questions, study notes and lab banks.
  6. Views : functions that build each screen from the current state.
  7. Actions: click/input handlers that update state and save data.
  8. render(): redraws the visible page after state changes.

  Main learning flow:
  Login → AI Conversation → Diagnosis → Dashboard → Study Notes → Lab → Assessment

  Study Notes behavior:
  Clicking a topic opens the real notes first. It is NOT marked as reviewed merely
  because the student opened the page. Completion/evidence is handled separately.
-->

                        'force per unit area', 'change in temperature', 'force × distance', 'mass per unit volume'
                    ], ans: 0, skill: 'Strength of Materials'
                },
                {
                    q: 'Which law relates pressure, volume and temperature for an ideal gas?', opts: [
                        'Ideal gas equation', 'Ohm’s law', 'Hooke’s law', 'Faraday’s law'
                    ], ans: 0, skill: 'Thermodynamics'
                },
                {
                    q: 'Which manufacturing process removes material using a cutting tool to produce a desired shape?', opts: [
                        'Machining', 'Casting', 'Forging only', 'Annealing'
                    ], ans: 0, skill: 'Manufacturing'
                }
            ],
            practical: 'Write a small program that accepts force F and cross-sectional area A, validates the inputs, and calculates normal stress using stress = F/A. Display a clear result and unit.'
        },
        'Robotics / Mechatronics': {
            core: [
                {
                    q: 'Which component measures a physical quantity such as distance, temperature or position?', opts: [
                        'Sensor', 'Actuator', 'Gearbox', 'Frame'
                    ], ans: 0, skill: 'Sensors'
                },
                {
                    q: 'What is the main purpose of a PID controller?', opts: [
                        'Reduce control error and improve system response', 'Store program files', 'Increase battery voltage automatically', 'Replace all sensors'
                    ], ans: 0, skill: 'Control Systems'
                },
                {
                    q: 'Which component converts a control signal into physical motion?', opts: [
                        'Actuator', 'Sensor', 'Compiler', 'Resistor'
                    ], ans: 0, skill: 'Actuation'
                }
            ],
            practical: 'Write control logic or pseudocode for a robot that reads a distance sensor, stops the motor when an obstacle is closer than a threshold, and otherwise continues forward.'
        },
        'Other': {
            core: [
                {
                    q: 'Which approach is best when solving a new technical problem?', opts: [
                        'Define the problem, test a simple solution, then improve it', 'Guess once and stop', 'Ignore the input constraints', 'Change many things at the same time'
                    ], ans: 0, skill: 'Problem Solving'
                },
                {
                    q: 'Which data structure stores values as key-value pairs?', opts: [
                        'Dictionary/Map', 'Stack', 'Queue', 'Array only'
                    ], ans: 0, skill: 'Programming Fundamentals'
                },
                {
                    q: 'Which practice most improves reliability of a small program?', opts: [
                        'Testing edge cases', 'Removing all validation', 'Avoiding functions', 'Ignoring errors'
                    ], ans: 0, skill: 'Software Fundamentals'
                }
            ],
            practical: 'Write a small program or clear algorithm for a simple real-world task of your choice. Include input validation, the main processing steps, and a clear output.'
        }
    };
    const DIAGNOSTIC_COMMON = [
        {
            type: 'voice', section: 'Communication', level: 'Communication', skill: 'Communication', q: 'Communication Question 1 — Speak for 30–45 seconds: introduce yourself, mention your branch, and explain why you want to build a career in your target role.'
        },
        {
            type: 'voice', section: 'Communication', level: 'Communication', skill: 'Communication', q: 'Communication Question 2 — Speak for 30–45 seconds: describe a time you worked with a teammate to solve a problem or complete a task. Explain your role and the result.'
        },
        {
            type: 'mcq', section: 'Aptitude', level: 'Aptitude', skill: 'Aptitude', q: 'A product costs ₹800 and is sold at a 15% discount. What is the selling price?', opts: [
                '₹620', '₹680', '₹720', '₹740'
            ], ans: 1
        },
        {
            type: 'mcq', section: 'Aptitude', level: 'Aptitude', skill: 'Aptitude', q: 'A can complete a job in 12 days and B can complete it in 18 days. Working together, how many days will they take?', opts: [
                '6.2 days', '7.2 days', '8 days', '9 days'
            ], ans: 1
        }
    ];
    function buildDiagnosticBank(branch) {
        const key = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(DIAGNOSTIC_BANKS, branch) ? branch :
            (/CSM|AI|ML/i.test(branch || '') ? 'CSM / AI & ML' :
                /ECE/i.test(branch || '') ? 'ECE' :
                    /EEE|Electrical/i.test(branch || '') ? 'EEE' :
                        /Civil/i.test(branch || '') ? 'Civil' :
                            /Mechanical/i.test(branch || '') ? 'Mechanical' :
                                /Robotics|Mechatronics/i.test(branch || '') ? 'Robotics / Mechatronics' :
                                    /CSE|Computer Science/i.test(branch || '') ? 'CSE' : 'Other');
        const b = DIAGNOSTIC_BANKS[key];
        return [
            ...DIAGNOSTIC_COMMON,
            ...b.core.map(x => ({
                type: 'mcq', section: 'Core', level: 'Core', ...x
            })),
            {
                type: 'practical', section: 'Core Practical', level: 'Practical', skill: 'Core Practical', q: b.practical, starter: '// Write your solution or pseudocode here\n// Include input validation and a clear output.\n'
            }
        ];
    }
    let diagQs = buildDiagnosticBank(state.branch || 'Other');
    function topbar() { return `<header class="topbar"><div class="brand"><div class="mascot">${mascot}<span class="spark">✦</span></div><div><div>PLACEMENT<span style="color:var(--purple)">360</span></div><small>AI placement companion</small></div></div><nav class="nav"><button data-action="landing">Home</button></nav></header>`; }
    function shellFloat() {
        if (!state.authed)
            return '';
        return `<div class="ai-float ${state.aiOpen ? 'open' : ''}" id="aiFloat"><button class="ai-mini" data-action="toggleAI" aria-label="Open AI companion">${mascot}</button><div class="ai-pop"><div class="title">✦ PLACEMENT360 AI</div><div class="small">Ask me to organize your study, explain a concept, plan today, or remind you about weekly work.</div><div id="aiAnswer" class="small" style="margin-top:8px">Try: “Plan my study for today.”</div><div class="ai-input"><input id="aiInput" placeholder="Ask anything…"><button data-action="sendAI">Send</button></div></div></div>`;
    }
    function landing() { return `${topbar()}<main class="page"><section class="hero"><div><span class="eyebrow">✦ ONE CONVERSATIONAL PLACEMENT EXPERIENCE</span><h1>Prepare with an AI that actually <span style="color:var(--purple)">knows you.</span></h1><p>PLACEMENT360 connects diagnosis, study, assessments, projects, communication, attendance and interview practice into one continuous AI conversation.</p><div class="actions"><button class="primary" data-action="start">Create account / Login</button><button class="secondary" data-action="demo">Explore demo</button></div><p class="small" style="margin-top:17px"></p></div><div class="hero-card"><div class="hero-card-head"><div class="mascot" style="width:40px;height:40px">${mascot}</div><div><b>PLACEMENT360 AI</b><div class="small">Conversational placement coach</div></div><span class="status">● Ready</span></div><div class="mini-bubble mini-user">I don’t know what to prepare for my company.</div><div class="mini-bubble mini-ai">Tell me your branch, target role and company. I’ll build the route, then verify it with an adaptive diagnostic.</div><div class="mini-report"><b>Diagnosis → Preparation → Evidence → Re-assessment</b><p class="small" style="margin:8px 0 0">Skill updates happen from completed assessments and weekly assignments—not unsupported claims.</p></div></div></section><section class="section"><div style="margin:30px 0 22px"><span class="eyebrow">WHAT CHANGES</span><h2 style="font-size:35px;margin:12px 0">From a dashboard to a conversation.</h2><p class="muted">The product still has dashboards and assessments, but the AI guides the student through them instead of making the student hunt for features.</p></div><div class="grid4"><div class="feature"><div class="ficon lav">01</div><b>Talk first</b><span>Welcome conversation asks only branch, target job and desired company.</span></div><div class="feature"><div class="ficon blue">02</div><b>Diagnose deeply</b><span>Basic → intermediate → hard → practical + communication, with proctoring controls.</span></div><div class="feature"><div class="ficon mint">03</div><b>Improve with evidence</b><span>AI assignments and weekly assessments are the source of skill updates.</span></div><div class="feature"><div class="ficon peach">04</div><b>Learn theory + practice</b><span>Concepts use theory, worked examples, multiple examples and hands-on tasks like a coding platform.</span></div></div></section></main>${shellFloat()}`; }
    function auth() { return `${topbar()}<main class="auth-wrap"><div class="auth-card"><span class="eyebrow">${state.authMode === 'login' ? 'Login' : 'Create account'}</button><p class="small" style="margin-top:17px">This preview demonstrates the experience; production authentication would use secure server-side identity.</p></div></main>${shellFloat()}`; }
    function welcome() { return `${topbar()}<main class="convo-wrap"><section class="convo-card"><div class="welcome"><div class="welcome-mascot">${mascot}</div><div><span class="eyebrow">WELCOME STUDENT!</span><div class="convo-title">Let’s start a conversation.</div><div class="muted">I only need three things to understand your direction.</div></div></div><div class="chat-stream" id="welcomeChat">${state.convo.map(m => `<div class="chat-msg ${m.who}">${m.html}</div>`).join('')}</div>${conversationInput()}</section></main>${shellFloat()}`; }
    function conversationInput() {
        const prompts = [
            [
                'branch', 'My branch is…'
            ], [
                'role', 'My target job is…'
            ], [
                'company', 'Company I want to join…'
            ]
        ];
        const p = prompts[state.step];
        if (state.step >= 3)
            return `<div class="final-diagnostic"><b>Great. I have enough context.</b><p class="muted">Now I’ll check what you actually know—from fundamentals to advanced, communication and practical ability—before building your preparation process.</p><button class="primary" style="width:100%;margin-top:8px" data-action="startDiagnostic">TAKE ADAPTIVE DIAGNOSTIC →</button></div>`;
        return `<div><b>${p[1]}</b><div class="quick-choices">${getOptions(state.step).map(x => `<button class="chip" data-convo-choice="${escapeAttr(x)}">${x}</button>`).join('')}</div><div class="conversation-input"><input id="convoInput" placeholder="Type your answer…"><button class="primary" data-action="convoSend">Send</button></div><div class="next-row"><button class="ghost small" data-action="convoNext">Skip / let AI decide</button></div></div>`;
    }
    function getOptions(step) {
        if (step === 0)
            return [
                'CSE', 'CSM / AI & ML', 'ECE', 'EEE', 'Civil', 'Mechanical', 'Robotics / Mechatronics', 'Other'
            ];
        if (step === 1)
            return branchRoles[state.branch || 'CSE'];
        if (step === 2)
            return [
                'TCS', 'Infosys', 'Accenture', 'IBM', 'Deloitte', 'Amazon', 'Not sure yet'
            ];
        return [];
    }
    function pushConvo(who, html) { state.convo.push({
        who, html
    }); render(); }
    function setConvoAnswer(value) {
        const clean = value.trim();
        if (!clean)
            return;
        if (state.step === 0)
            state.branch = clean;
        if (state.step === 1)
            state.role = clean;
        if (state.step === 2) {
            state.company = clean;
            state.companyNoIdea = /no idea|not sure/i.test(clean);
        }
        state.convo.push({
            who: 'user', html: escape(clean)
        });
        const replies = [
            `Nice — <b>${escape(state.branch)}</b>. I’ll tailor the diagnostic to this branch.`, `Got it. Your target is <b>${escape(state.role)}</b>. I’ll keep the questions role-aware.`, state.companyNoIdea ? 'No problem. I’ll use common placement patterns across companies and your branch.' : `Great. I’ll also align the preparation route with <b>${escape(state.company)}</b>-style expectations.`
        ];
        state.convo.push({
            who: 'ai', html: replies[state.step]
        });
        state.step++;
        render();
    }
    function diagnosticIntro() { return `${topbar()}<main class="report-wrap"><section class="report-card"><span class="eyebrow">ADAPTIVE DIAGNOSTIC</span><h2 style="font-size:38px;letter-spacing:-.05em;margin:12px 0 8px">Before we plan, we verify.</h2><p class="muted">This is a dedicated assessment session, not a chat quiz. The questions move from basic to difficult. Your communication, practical thinking and branch-specific execution are also evaluated.</p><div class="cards" style="margin-top:16px"><div class="mini-card"><b>2 · Communication</b><p class="small">Two spoken questions shared across all branches.</p></div><div class="mini-card"><b>2 · Aptitude</b><p class="small">Two aptitude questions shared across all branches.</p></div><div class="mini-card"><b>3 · Core MCQs</b><p class="small">Three multiple-choice questions selected for your chosen branch.</p></div><div class="mini-card"><b>1 · Core practical</b><p class="small">One branch-specific practical task.</p></div></div><div class="eval-grid"><div class="eval-box"><b>Camera</b><div class="small">Required for the assessment session.</div></div><div class="eval-box"><b>Microphone</b><div class="small">Permission is requested when the assessment starts. The microphone stays OFF until you click Start recording for a spoken response.</div></div><div class="eval-box"><b>Screen share</b><div class="small">Keeps the intended work surface visible during the test.</div></div><div class="eval-box"><b>One-session mode</b><div class="small">Visibility changes trigger a warning and can pause the assessment.</div></div></div><div class="guard" style="margin-top:18px"><b>Assessment integrity</b><br><span class="small">Camera, microphone permission, screen sharing, fullscreen and one-session checks help keep the assessment focused. Microphone permission is requested at exam start, but microphone capture remains OFF until you click Start recording for a communication or soft-skills question.</span></div><button class="primary" style="width:100%;margin-top:20px" data-action="prepareAssessment">Enable camera + microphone + screen →</button></section></main>${shellFloat()}`; }
    function loading() { return `${topbar()}<main class="loading"><section class="comic-card"><div class="comic-scene">${mascot}</div><span class="eyebrow">ANALYZING YOUR PERFORMANCE</span><h2 style="font-size:38px;letter-spacing:-.05em;margin:13px 0 7px">Hang on<span class="dots"><span>.</span><span>.</span><span>.</span></span></h2><p class="muted">Our little coach is reviewing your answers, communication, practical response and branch context.</p><div class="spinner"></div><p class="small" id="loadingText">Finding root-level strengths and gaps…</p></section></main>${shellFloat()}`; }

    function viewContent() {
        const v = state.view;
        if (v === 'chat')
            return chatPanel();
        if (v === 'assessment')
            return assessmentPage();
        if (v === 'assignments')
            return assignments();
        if (v === 'attendance')
            return attendance();
        if (v === 'concepts')
            return concepts();
        if (v === 'interview')
            return interview();
        if (v === 'progress')
            return progress();
        return dashboardHome();
    }
    function dashboardHome() { return `<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><span class="eyebrow">YOUR AI PLACEMENT DASHBOARD</span><h2>Here is what matters today.</h2><p class="muted">The AI keeps the interface organized for you.
