    ]; return `<div class="panel"><span class="eyebrow">CONCEPTS</span><h2>Learn like a guided coding platform.</h2><p class="muted">Each concept follows Theory → Worked Example → Multiple Examples → Try It → Practical Task → Quick Check.</p><div class="topic-grid" style="margin-top:18px">${concepts.map(c => `<div class="topic-card" data-concept="${escapeAttr(c[0])}"><b>${c[0]}</b><small>${c[1]}</small></div>`).join('')}</div></div>${state.concept ? conceptDetail(state.concept) : ''}`; }
    function conceptDetail(name) { return `<div class="panel"><span class="eyebrow">CONCEPT LAB</span><h2>${escape(name)}</h2><div class="cards"><div class="mini-card"><b>1. Theory</b><p class="small">Understand the definition, why it exists, when to use it and common mistakes.</p></div><div class="mini-card"><b>2. Worked example</b><p class="small">See one fully explained example before trying it yourself.</p></div><div class="mini-card"><b>3. Multiple examples</b><p class="small">Easy → medium → hard, with different patterns.</p></div><div class="mini-card"><b>4. Practice</b><p class="small">Complete a practical task and explain your reasoning.</p></div></div><button class="primary" style="margin-top:15px" data-action="startConceptPractice">Try ${escape(name)} practice</button></div>`; }
    function interview() { return `<div class="panel"><span class="eyebrow">AI INTERVIEWER</span><h2>Practice the way an interviewer will listen.</h2><p class="muted">Questions adapt to your answers. You can speak your reasoning, defend projects and receive feedback on correctness, structure and communication.</p><button class="primary" data-action="startInterview">Start adaptive interview</button></div>`; }
    function progress() { return `<div class="panel"><span class="eyebrow">PROGRESS & EVIDENCE</span><h2>Your profile is built from what you demonstrate.</h2><div class="trend" style="height:190px">${[
        35, 41, 45, 49, 56, 61, 66
    ].map((h, i) => `<i style="--h:${h * 2.1}px" title="Week ${i + 1}"></i>`).join('')}</div><table class="table"><tr><th>Evidence source</th><th>Status</th><th>Effect</th></tr><tr><td>Adaptive diagnostic</td><td><span class="tag">Complete</span></td><td>Baseline established</td></tr><tr><td>Weekly assignment</td><td><span class="tag ${state.weeklyDone ? '' : 'warn'}">${state.weeklyDone ? 'Verified' : 'Pending'}</span></td><td>Skill evidence</td></tr><tr><td>Weekly assessment</td><td><span class="tag warn">Upcoming</span></td><td>Skill update after completion</td></tr></table></div>`; }
    function assessmentSession() { const a = state.assessment, q = diagQs[a.q]; const pct = Math.round((a.q / diagQs.length) * 100); return `${topbar()}<main class="page"><div class="assessment"><div class="guard"><b>Assessment integrity mode active.</b> Keep camera, microphone and shared screen available. Do not open another tab or window. A visibility change will pause the test.</div><div class="proctor"><div class="check ${a.camera ? 'ok' : ''}">📷 Camera: ${a.camera ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}</div><div class="check ${a.mic ? 'ok' : ''}">🎙 Microphone: ${a.mic ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}</div><div class="check ${a.screen ? 'ok' : ''}">🖥 Screen share: ${a.screen ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}</div></div><div class="progress-line"><i style="width:${pct}%"></i></div><div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;margin:10px 0 14px"><span class="small">Question ${a.q + 1} of ${diagQs.length}</span><span class="small">Progress ${pct}%</span></div><div class="test-layout"><section class="question-card"><span class="level-chip">${q.level}</span><h2 style="font-size:29px;letter-spacing:-.04em;margin:12px 0">${q.q}</h2>${q.type === 'mcq' ? mcqBlock(q) : q.type === 'voice' ? voiceBlock() : practicalBlock()}<div style="display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:18px"><button class="primary" data-action="nextAssessment">${a.q === diagQs.length - 1 ? 'Finish assessment' : 'Next question →'}</button></div></section><aside class="assessment-side"><div class="panel"><b>Integrity check</b><p class="small">No copy/paste, no context switching, one continuous session.</p><div class="camera-box"><video id="camVideo" autoplay muted playsinline></video><div class="camera-label">LIVE CAMERA</div></div><div class="mic-wave" title="microphone activity"><span></span><span></span><span></span><span></span><span></span></div></div></aside></div></div></main>${shellFloat()}`; }
    function mcqBlock(q) { return q.opts.map((x, i) => `<button class="answer-option ${state.assessment.answers[state.assessment.q] === i ? 'selected' : ''}" data-answer="${i}">${String.fromCharCode(65 + i)}. ${x}</button>`).join(''); }
    function voiceBlock() { const q = diagQs[state.assessment.q] || {}; return `<div class="panel" style="margin-top:16px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbf9ff,#f1f9ff)"><b>Communication question</b><p style="font-size:17px;line-height:1.55;margin:10px 0">${escape(q.q || 'Please speak your answer clearly.')}</p><p class="small">Speak for about 30–45 seconds. The response is evaluated for clarity, relevance, structure and fluency.</p><button class="secondary" data-action="recordVoice">🎙 Start speaking</button><p id="voiceStatus" class="small" style="margin-top:10px">Not started.</p></div>`; }
    function practicalBlock() {
        const q = diagQs[state.assessment.q] || {};
        const starter = q.starter || '// Write your solution here\n';
        const task = q.q || 'Complete the branch-specific practical task shown here.';
        return `<div class="panel" style="margin-top:16px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbf9ff,#f1f9ff)"><span class="level-chip">CORE PRACTICAL QUESTION</span><h3 style="margin:12px 0 8px">${escape(task)}</h3><p class="small">Write the solution in code, pseudocode or a clear algorithm. Include input validation and a clear result.</p></div><div class="editor"><div class="runbar"><span>Practical response</span><button class="secondary" style="padding:8px 11px" data-action="runCode">Check response</button></div><textarea id="codeBox">${escape(starter)}</textarea><div id="codeResult" class="small" style="margin-top:10px">Response not checked yet.</div></div>`;
    }
    function prepareAssessment() {
        state.assessment = {
            ...state.assessment, consent: true
        };
        requestProctor().then(() => {
            if (state.assessment.camera && state.assessment.mic && state.assessment.screen) {
                state.screen = 'assessment';
                render();
                attachVideo();
            }
            else
                render();
        });
    }
    async function requestProctor() {
        try {
            if (navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) {
                const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
                    video: true, audio: true
                });
                state.assessment.stream = stream;
                state.assessment.camera = true;
                state.assessment.mic = true;
            }
        }
        catch (e) {
            showToast('Camera/microphone permission was not granted. Please enable it to continue.');
        }
        try {
            if (navigator.mediaDevices?.getDisplayMedia) {
                const display = await navigator.mediaDevices.getDisplayMedia({
                    video: true, audio: false
                });
                state.assessment.displayStream = display;
                state.assessment.screen = true;
            }
        }
        catch (e) {
            showToast('Screen sharing is required for assessment integrity in this preview.');
        }
        try {
            await document.documentElement.requestFullscreen?.();
            state.assessment.fullscreen = !!document.fullscreenElement;
        }
        catch (e) { }
    }
    function attachVideo() {
        const video = document.getElementById('camVideo');
        const s = state.assessment.stream;
        if (video && s)
            video.srcObject = s;
    }
    function startDiagnostic() { diagQs = buildDiagnosticBank(state.branch || 'Other'); state.assessment = {
        ...state.assessment, mode: 'diagnostic', q: 0, answers: {}, voiceScore: 0, practicalDone: false, bank: diagQs
    }; state.screen = 'diagIntro'; render(); }
    function processAssessment() { return evaluateDiagnosticDetailed(); }
    function finishAssessment() { stopStreams(); state.screen = 'loading'; render(); setTimeout(() => { state.diagnosis = processAssessment(); state.screen = 'report'; render(); }, 2800); }
    function stopStreams() {
        for (const key of [
            'stream', 'displayStream'
        ]) {
            const s = state.assessment[key];
            if (s?.getTracks)
                s.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
        }
    }
    function aiAnswer(text) {
        const p = text.toLowerCase();
        if (p.includes('today'))
            return 'For today: 25 min concept theory, 20 min worked examples, 25 min practice and 10 min reflection. I would protect one focused block rather than spread the work across the day.';
        if (p.includes('remind'))
            return 'I can represent reminders in the prototype. In production, reminders would be scheduled from your assignment and attendance calendar.';
        if (p.includes('concept'))
            return 'Open Concepts to learn through Theory → Worked Example → Multiple Examples → Try It → Practical Task → Quick Check.';
        if (p.includes('assessment'))
            return 'Your weekly assessment is based on recent weak areas. Completing it can update skills; casual chat cannot.';
        return 'Ask me about your study plan, concepts, assessment, attendance, interview practice or the next best action.';
    }
    function render() {
        let html = '';
        if (state.screen === 'landing')
            html = landing();
        else if (state.screen === 'auth')
            html = auth();
        else if (state.screen === 'welcome')
            html = welcome();
        else if (state.screen === 'diagIntro')
            html = diagnosticIntro();
        else if (state.screen === 'assessment')
            html = assessmentSession();
        else if (state.screen === 'loading')
            html = loading();
        else if (state.screen === 'report')
            html = diagnosisReport();
        else if (state.screen === 'app')
            html = dashboard();
        root.innerHTML = html;
        bind();
        if (state.screen === 'assessment')
            attachVideo();
    }
    function bind() {
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-action]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => action(b.dataset.action));
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-view]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.view = b.dataset.view; state.screen = 'app'; render(); });
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-convo-choice]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => setConvoAnswer(b.dataset.convoChoice));
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-answer]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.assessment.answers[state.assessment.q] = Number(b.dataset.answer); render(); });
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-practical-choice]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.assessment.practicalDone = Number(b.dataset.practicalChoice) === 0; render(); });
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-concept]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.concept = b.dataset.concept; render(); });
        const input = root.querySelector('#convoInput');
        if (input)
            input.onkeydown = e => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    action('convoSend');
                }
            };
        const ai = root.querySelector('#aiInput');
        if (ai)
            ai.onkeydown = e => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    action('sendAI');
                }
            };
        const wa = root.querySelector('#workspaceChat');
        if (wa)
            wa.onkeydown = e => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    action('workspaceSend');
                }
            };
        document.onvisibilitychange = () => {
            if (state.screen === 'assessment' && document.hidden) {
                showToast('Tab/window switch detected. Assessment paused. Return to this session to continue.');
            }
        };
        document.oncopy = e => {
            if (state.screen === 'assessment') {
                e.preventDefault();
                showToast('Copy is disabled during the assessment.');
            }
        };
        document.oncontextmenu = e => {
            if (state.screen === 'assessment') {
                e.preventDefault();
            }
        };
    }
    function action(a) {
        if (a === 'start') {
            state.screen = 'auth';
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'demo') {
            state.branch = 'CSE';
            state.role = 'Software Developer';
            state.company = 'Not sure yet';
            state.companyNoIdea = true;
            state.step = 3;
            state.convo = [
                {
                    who: 'ai', html: 'Welcome back, Demo Student. I already have your context and can take you straight to the adaptive diagnostic.'
                }
            ];
            state.screen = 'welcome';
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'landing') {
            state.screen = 'landing';
            state.stop?.();
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'loginMode') {
            state.authMode = 'login';
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'signupMode') {
            state.authMode = 'signup';
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'authSubmit') {
            state.screen = 'welcome';
            state.convo = [
                {
                    who: 'ai', html: 'Welcome student! ✦ I’ll keep this simple. First: <b>what branch are you studying?</b>'
                }
            ];
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'convoSend') {
            const v = document.getElementById('convoInput')?.value || '';
            if (v.trim())
                setConvoAnswer(v);
        }
        else if (a === 'convoNext') {
            setConvoAnswer(state.step === 0 ? 'Other' : state.step === 1 ? 'Role to be discovered by AI' : 'Not sure yet');
        }
        else if (a === 'startDiagnostic') {
            startDiagnostic();
        }
        else if (a === 'prepareAssessment') {
            prepareAssessment();
        }
        else if (a === 'nextAssessment') {
            if (state.assessment.q === diagQs.length - 1)
                finishAssessment();
            else {
                state.assessment.q++;
                render();
            }
        }
        else if (a === 'recordVoice') {
            recordVoice();
        }
        else if (a === 'runCode') {
            const c = document.getElementById('codeBox')?.value || '';
            document.getElementById('codeResult').textContent = /return|print/.test(c) ? 'Simulation output: solution submitted for evaluation.' : 'Write a complete function first, then run again.';
        }
        else if (a === 'openPrep') {
            state.screen = 'app';
            state.view = 'dashboard';
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'openAI' || a === 'toggleAI') {
            state.aiOpen = !state.aiOpen;
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'sendAI') {
            const v = document.getElementById('aiInput')?.value || '';
            if (v.trim()) {
                const out = document.getElementById('aiAnswer');
                if (out)
                    out.textContent = aiAnswer(v);
                const inp = document.getElementById('aiInput');
                if (inp)
                    inp.value = '';
            }
        }
        else if (a === 'workspaceSend') {
            const v = document.getElementById('workspaceChat')?.value || '';
            if (v.trim())
                showToast(aiAnswer(v));
        }
        else if (a === 'completeWeekly') {
            state.weeklyDone = true;
            showToast('Weekly assignment evidence recorded. Your skill profile can now be updated from this evidence.');
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'markAttendance') {
            state.attendance = 7;
            showToast('Weekly attendance recorded.');
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'startWeeklyAssessment') {
            state.assessment.q = 0;
            showToast('Weekly assessment opened. Production mode would use a fresh AI-generated assessment.');
        }
        else if (a === 'startConceptPractice') {
            showToast('Practice mode opened. The production lesson would now launch theory, examples and hands-on practice.');
        }
        else if (a === 'startInterview') {
            showToast('Adaptive interview opened. Voice responses and NLP-style communication feedback are part of the production flow.');
        }
    }
    function recordVoice() {
        state.assessment.voiceScore = 2;
        const el = document.getElementById('voiceStatus');
        if (el)
            el.textContent = 'Speech captured. Prototype communication result: clear introduction detected. Production NLP would assess structure, relevance, clarity, fluency and fillers.';
    }
    function escape(s) { return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({
        '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#039;'
    }[c])); }
    function escapeAttr(s) { return escape(s).replace(/`/g, '&#096;'); }
    function showToast(t) { const x = document.createElement('div'); x.className = 'toast'; x.textContent = t; document.body.appendChild(x); setTimeout(() => x.remove(), 3200); }
    function landing() { return `<${'main'} class="page"><section class="hero" style="min-height:calc(100vh - 70px);padding-top:34px;padding-bottom:34px"><div><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:13px;margin-bottom:20px"><div class="mascot" style="width:58px;height:58px;border-radius:18px">${mascot}</div><div><b style="font-size:17px">PLACEMENT<span style="color:var(--purple)">360</span></b><div class="small">Your conversational placement coach</div></div></div><span class="eyebrow">✦ ONE CONVERSATIONAL PLACEMENT EXPERIENCE</span><h1>Prepare with an AI that actually <span style="color:var(--purple)">knows you.</span></h1><p>One guided experience for diagnosis, study, assessment, projects, communication, attendance, resume improvement and interview practice.</p><p class="small" style="margin-top:15px"></p></div><div class="hero-card" style="max-width:520px;justify-self:end"><div class="hero-card-head"><div class="mascot" style="width:42px;height:42px">${mascot}</div><div><b>PLACEMENT360 AI</b><div class="small">Available after login</div></div><span class="status">● Locked</span></div><div class="mini-bubble mini-ai">Create an account or log in to start your private conversation with the placement AI.</div><div class="mini-report"><b>Login → Conversation → Diagnosis → Preparation</b><p class="small" style="margin:8px 0 0">Your AI companion appears only after authentication and stays available throughout the student experience.</p></div></div></section></main>${shellFloat()}`; }
    function dashboard() { return `${topbar()}<div class="workspace"><aside class="sidebar"><div class="side-label">AI Workspace</div><button class="side-btn ${state.view === 'dashboard' ? 'active' : ''}" data-view="dashboard">◉ Dashboard</button><button class="side-btn ${state.view === 'today' ? 'active' : ''}" data-view="today">☀ Today’s Plan</button><button class="side-btn ${state.view === 'assessment' ? 'active' : ''}" data-view="assessment">⌁ Assessment</button><button class="side-btn ${state.view === 'resume' ? 'active' : ''}" data-view="resume">▤ Resume</button><button class="side-btn ${state.view === 'chat' ? 'active' : ''}" data-view="chat">✦ AI Conversation</button><button class="side-btn ${state.view === 'assignments' ? 'active' : ''}" data-view="assignments">✓ Weekly Assignments</button><button class="side-btn ${state.view === 'attendance' ? 'active' : ''}" data-view="attendance">◷ Weekly Attendance</button><div class="side-label">Learning</div><button class="side-btn ${state.view === 'concepts' ? 'active' : ''}" data-view="concepts">▦ Concepts</button><button class="side-btn ${state.view === 'interview' ? 'active' : ''}" data-view="interview">◉ Interview Practice</button><button class="side-btn ${state.view === 'progress' ? 'active' : ''}" data-view="progress">↗ Progress & Evidence</button><button class="side-btn" data-action="landing">← Sign out</button></aside><main class="mainspace"><div class="workspace-head"><div><b>PLACEMENT360</b><div class="small">${escape(state.branch)} · ${escape(state.role)} · ${escape(state.companyNoIdea ? 'Common placement route' : state.company)}</div></div><span class="tag">AI companion active</span></div><div class="content">${viewContent()}</div></main></div>${shellFloat()}`; }
    function viewContent() {
        const v = state.view;
        if (v === 'today')
            return todaysPlan();
        if (v === 'resume')
            return resumePanel();
        if (v === 'chat')
            return chatPanel();
        if (v === 'assessment')
            return assessmentPage();
        if (v === 'assignments')
            return assignments();
        if (v === 'attendance')
            return attendance();
        if (v === 'concepts')
            return concepts();
        if (v === 'interview')
            return interview();
        if (v === 'progress')
            return progress();
        return dashboardHome();
    }
    function dashboardHome() { state.skillEvidence = state.skillEvidence || 35; return `<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><span class="eyebrow">YOUR AI PLACEMENT DASHBOARD</span><h2>Everything important, one page.</h2><p class="muted">Your dashboard is a control center; the AI still guides the student through the work.</p></div><button class="primary" data-view="today">Open today’s plan</button></div><div class="dash-grid" style="margin-top:18px"><div class="panel" style="margin:0;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#faf8ff,#f1f9ff)"><div class="metric-big">${state.diagnosis ? state.diagnosis.level : 'Diagnostic pending'}</div><div class="muted">Current learning level</div><div style="margin-top:18px"><b>Evidence-based skill progress</b><div class="skillbar" style="margin-top:8px"><i style="width:${state.skillEvidence}%"></i></div><p class="small">${state.skillEvidence}% evidence strength · updated only by assessments and assignments.</p></div></div><div class="panel" style="margin:0"><b>Today at a glance</b><p class="small">Weekly assignment: ${state.weeklyDone ? 'Done' : 'Pending'} · Attendance: ${state.attendance ? 'Recorded' : 'Pending'}</p><button class="secondary" data-view="assessment">Open assessment</button></div></div></div><div class="cards"><div class="mini-card"><b>Today’s plan</b><p class="small">1 topic · 2 coding problems · 2 communication activities · 1 revision topic.</p><button class="secondary" data-view="today">View plan</button></div><div class="mini-card"><b>Resume improvement</b><p class="small">Upload your resume and the AI compares it with your student profile, target role and company context.</p><button class="secondary" data-view="resume">Review resume</button></div></div>`; }
    function todaysPlan() {
        return '<div class="panel"><span class="eyebrow">TODAY’S PLAN</span><h2>Simple goals for today.</h2><p class="muted">Complete these targets at your own pace and keep your progress moving.</p><div class="cards" style="margin-top:16px"><div class="mini-card"><b>1 · Today’s concept</b><p class="small">Study at least 1 topic and work through 2 examples.</p></div><div class="mini-card"><b>2 · Practical</b><p class="small">Finish at least 2 coding problems.</p></div><div class="mini-card"><b>3 · Communication</b><p class="small">Complete at least 2 communication lessons or practice activities.</p></div><div class="mini-card"><b>4 · Revision</b><p class="small">Revise 1 previously studied topic.</p></div></div></div>';
    }
    function resumePanel() { return `<div class="panel"><span class="eyebrow">AI RESUME REVIEW</span><h2>Upload your resume. Let the AI tailor it.</h2><p class="muted">The AI compares your resume with your present student data, target job and company context, then suggests concrete changes to make the resume stronger and more relevant.</p><div class="mini-card" style="margin-top:16px"><b>Current profile context</b><p class="small">${escape(state.branch)} · ${escape(state.role)} · ${escape(state.companyNoIdea ? 'Common placement patterns' : state.company)}</p></div><label class="secondary" style="display:inline-block;margin-top:16px">Choose resume<input id="resumeInput" type="file" accept=".pdf,.doc,.docx,.txt" style="display:none"></label>${state.resumeUploaded ? `<div class="diagnosis-note" style="margin-top:18px"><b>Uploaded:</b> ${escape(state.resumeName)}<br><br><b>AI recommendations</b><ul style="margin:9px 0 0;padding-left:18px"><li>Rewrite the summary around the target role instead of using a generic objective.</li><li>Turn project descriptions into measurable problem → action → result statements.</li><li>Prioritize skills and evidence that match ${escape(state.companyNoIdea ? 'common placement screening' : state.company || 'your chosen company')}.</li><li>Add missing proof: coding profiles, project links, certifications and practical outcomes.</li><li>Remove low-value repetition and keep each section easy for recruiters to scan.</li></ul><p class="small" style="margin-top:12px">This preview uses your profile context to demonstrate the review flow; production AI would parse the uploaded document content directly.</p></div>` : ''}</div>`; }
    function assessmentPage() {
        if (!state.diagnosis)
            return `<div class="panel"><span class="eyebrow">ASSESSMENT</span><h2>Complete your adaptive diagnostic first.</h2><p class="muted">The first assessment establishes your baseline across fundamentals, problem solving, communication and practical ability.</p><button class="primary" data-action="startDiagnostic">Take adaptive diagnostic</button></div>`;
        return `<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><span class="eyebrow">AI-GENERATED ASSESSMENT</span><h2>Fresh questions every time.</h2><p class="muted">The next assessment is generated from your previous weak areas and recent concepts. Completing it can update your skill evidence.</p></div><span class="tag">Skills update from evidence</span></div><div class="cards" style="margin-top:16px"><div class="mini-card"><b>Adaptive questions</b><p class="small">Newly selected questions across theory, application and reasoning.</p></div><div class="mini-card"><b>Communication</b><p class="small">A spoken response evaluates clarity, structure, relevance and delivery.</p></div></div><button class="primary" style="margin-top:16px" data-action="startWeeklyAssessment">Start fresh AI assessment →</button></div><div class="panel"><span class="eyebrow">SKILL UPDATE RULE</span><h3 style="margin-top:12px">Chat recommendations do not change mastery.</h3><p class="muted">Only completed assessments and weekly assignments contribute evidence to your skill profile.</p></div>`;
    }
    function weeklyQuestionBank() { return [
        {
            type: 'mcq', level: 'Basic', skill: 'Core concept', q: 'Which statement best describes a function?', opts: [
                'A reusable block of logic', 'Only a database table', 'A compiler error', 'A file extension'
            ], ans: 0
        }, {
            type: 'mcq', level: 'Basic', skill: 'Aptitude', q: 'If 5 notebooks cost ₹150, what is the cost of 8 notebooks at the same rate?', opts: [
                '₹180', '₹220', '₹240', '₹300'
            ], ans: 2
        }, {
            type: 'mcq', level: 'Basic', skill: 'Programming', q: 'Which data type commonly stores a whole number in Python?', opts: [
                'int', 'str', 'list', 'bool'
            ], ans: 0
        }, {
            type: 'mcq', level: 'Basic', skill: 'Communication', q: 'Which opening is strongest in a professional self-introduction?', opts: [
                'A random personal story', 'Name + education/role + relevant goal', 'Only your marks', '“I have nothing to say”'
            ], ans: 1
        }, {
            type: 'mcq', level: 'Intermediate', skill: 'Problem solving', q: 'Which approach is usually better when the same expensive calculation appears repeatedly?', opts: [
                'Recalculate it every time', 'Reuse a stored result', 'Add more print statements', 'Use a larger variable name'
            ], ans: 1
        }, {
            type: 'mcq', level: 'Intermediate', skill: 'Data', q: 'Which SQL operation combines rows from two tables using a related column?', opts: [
                'JOIN', 'DROP', 'TRUNCATE', 'RENAME'
            ], ans: 0
        }, {
            type: 'mcq', level: 'Intermediate', skill: 'DSA', q: 'Which structure follows first-in, first-out order?', opts: [
                'Queue', 'Stack', 'Tree', 'Graph'
            ], ans: 0
        }, {
            type: 'mcq', level: 'Intermediate', skill: 'Debugging', q: 'A program gives the wrong output but runs successfully. What should you inspect first?', opts: [
                'The logic and test cases', 'The laptop wallpaper', 'The font', 'The browser theme'
            ], ans: 0
        }, {
            type: 'mcq', level: 'Advanced', skill: 'Reasoning', q: 'A solution works for small inputs but becomes slow for large inputs. What should you inspect first?', opts: [
                'Algorithmic complexity', 'Font size', 'Variable color', 'Comment length'
            ], ans: 0
        }, {
            type: 'mcq', level: 'Advanced', skill: 'SQL', q: 'Why might an index improve a frequent lookup?', opts: [
                'It can reduce the amount of data searched', 'It always removes rows', 'It changes a table into a file', 'It guarantees zero storage use'
            ], ans: 0
        }, {
            type: 'mcq', level: 'Advanced', skill: 'Problem solving', q: 'What is a strong response when requirements are ambiguous?', opts: [
                'Guess silently', 'Ask a focused clarification and state assumptions', 'Stop immediately', 'Ignore the ambiguity'
            ], ans: 1
        }, {
            type: 'voice', level: 'Advanced', skill: 'Communication', q: 'Speak for about 45 seconds: explain one project or technical topic so that a recruiter from another branch can understand it.'
        }, {
            type: 'voice', level: 'Complex', skill: 'Soft skills', q: 'Speak for about 45 seconds: explain how you would handle a disagreement with a teammate when the deadline is close.'
        }
    ]; }
    function currentQuestions() { return state.assessment.mode === 'weekly' ? (state.assessment.bank || []) : diagQs; }
    function assessmentSession() { const a = state.assessment, qs = currentQuestions(), q = qs[a.q]; const pct = Math.round((a.q / qs.length) * 100); return `${topbar()}<main class="page"><div class="assessment"><div class="guard"><b>Assessment integrity mode active.</b> Camera, microphone and screen share must remain available. Opening another tab/window pauses the session.</div><div class="proctor"><div class="check ${a.camera ? 'ok' : ''}">📷 Camera: ${a.camera ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}</div><div class="check ${a.mic ? 'ok' : ''}">🎙 Microphone: ${a.mic ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}</div><div class="check ${a.screen ? 'ok' : ''}">🖥 Screen share: ${a.screen ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}</div></div><div class="progress-line"><i style="width:${pct}%"></i></div><div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;margin:10px 0 14px"><span class="small">${state.assessment.mode === 'weekly' ? 'Weekly AI assessment' : 'Adaptive diagnostic'} · Question ${a.q + 1} of ${qs.length}</span><span class="small">Progress ${pct}%</span></div><div class="test-layout"><section class="question-card"><span class="level-chip">${q.level}</span><h2 style="font-size:29px;letter-spacing:-.04em;margin:12px 0">${q.q}</h2>${q.type === 'mcq' ? mcqBlock(q) : q.type === 'voice' ? voiceBlock() : practicalBlock()}<div style="display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin-top:18px"><button class="primary" data-action="nextAssessment">${a.q === qs.length - 1 ? 'Finish assessment' : 'Next question →'}</button></div></section><aside class="assessment-side"><div class="panel"><b>Integrity check</b><p class="small">Copy/paste is disabled in this preview and visibility changes pause the session.</p><div class="camera-box"><video id="camVideo" autoplay muted playsinline></video><div class="camera-label">LIVE CAMERA</div></div><div class="mic-wave" title="microphone activity"><span></span><span></span><span></span><span></span><span></span></div></div></aside></div></div></main>${shellFloat()}`; }
    function render() {
        let html = '';
        if (state.screen === 'landing')
            html = landing();
        else if (state.screen === 'auth')
            html = auth();
        else if (state.screen === 'welcome')
            html = welcome();
        else if (state.screen === 'diagIntro')
            html = diagnosticIntro();
        else if (state.screen === 'assessment')
            html = assessmentSession();
        else if (state.screen === 'loading')
            html = loading();
        else if (state.screen === 'report')
            html = diagnosisReport();
        else if (state.screen === 'app')
            html = dashboard();
        root.innerHTML = html;
        bind();
        if (state.screen === 'assessment')
            attachVideo();
    }
    function bind() {
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-action]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => action(b.dataset.action));
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-view]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.view = b.dataset.view; state.screen = 'app'; render(); });
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-convo-choice]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => setConvoAnswer(b.dataset.convoChoice));
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-answer]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.assessment.answers[state.assessment.q] = Number(b.dataset.answer); render(); });
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-practical-choice]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.assessment.practicalDone = Number(b.dataset.practicalChoice) === 0; render(); });
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-concept]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.concept = b.dataset.concept; render(); });
        const input = root.querySelector('#convoInput');
        if (input)
            input.onkeydown = e => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    action('convoSend');
                }
            };
        const ai = root.querySelector('#aiInput');
        if (ai)
            ai.onkeydown = e => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    action('sendAI');
                }
            };
        const wa = root.querySelector('#workspaceChat');
        if (wa)
            wa.onkeydown = e => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    action('workspaceSend');
                }
            };
        const ri = root.querySelector('#resumeInput');
        if (ri)
            ri.onchange = e => {
                const f = e.target.files?.[0];
                if (!f)
                    return;
                state.resumeUploaded = true;
                state.resumeName = f.name;
                state.resumeSize = f.size;
                state.view = 'resume';
                state.screen = 'app';
                render();
                showToast('Resume uploaded. AI review prepared from your profile context.');
            };
        document.onvisibilitychange = () => {
            if (state.screen === 'assessment' && document.hidden)
                showToast('Tab/window switch detected. Assessment paused. Return to this session to continue.');
        };
        document.oncopy = e => {
            if (state.screen === 'assessment') {
                e.preventDefault();
                showToast('Copy is disabled during the assessment.');
            }
        };
        document.oncontextmenu = e => {
            if (state.screen === 'assessment') {
                e.preventDefault();
            }
        };
    }
    function finishWeeklyAssessment() { stopStreams(); state.weeklyAssessmentDone = true; state.skillEvidence = Math.min(100, (state.skillEvidence || 35) + 10); state.screen = 'app'; state.view = 'assessment'; render(); showToast('Fresh assessment completed. Skill evidence updated from this assessment.'); }
    function action(a) {
        if (a === 'start') {
            state.screen = 'auth';
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'landing') {
            stopStreams();
            state.authed = false;
            state.aiOpen = false;
            state.screen = 'landing';
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'loginMode') {
            state.authMode = 'login';
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'signupMode') {
            state.authMode = 'signup';
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'authSubmit') {
            state.authed = true;
            if (state.authMode === 'signup') {
                state.branch = '';
                state.role = '';
                state.company = '';
                state.companyNoIdea = false;
                state.step = 0;
                state.screen = 'welcome';
                state.convo = [
                    {
                        who: 'ai', html: 'Welcome student! ✦ I’ll keep this simple. First: <b>what branch are you studying?</b>'
                    }
                ];
                render();
            }
            else {
                state.branch = 'CSE';
                state.role = 'Software Developer';
                state.company = 'Not sure yet';
                state.companyNoIdea = true;
                state.screen = 'app';
                state.view = 'dashboard';
                state.convo = [];
                render();
                showToast('Welcome back. Your existing profile is loaded.');
            }
        }
        else if (a === 'convoSend') {
            const v = document.getElementById('convoInput')?.value || '';
            if (v.trim())
                setConvoAnswer(v);
        }
        else if (a === 'convoNext') {
            setConvoAnswer(state.step === 0 ? 'Other' : state.step === 1 ? 'Role to be discovered by AI' : 'Not sure yet');
        }
        else if (a === 'startDiagnostic') {
            startDiagnostic();
        }
        else if (a === 'prepareAssessment') {
            prepareAssessment();
        }
        else if (a === 'nextAssessment') {
            const qs = currentQuestions();
            if (state.assessment.q === qs.length - 1) {
                if (state.assessment.mode === 'weekly')
                    finishWeeklyAssessment();
                else
                    finishAssessment();
            }
            else {
                state.assessment.q++;
                render();
            }
        }
        else if (a === 'recordVoice') {
            recordVoice();
        }
        else if (a === 'runCode') {
            const c = document.getElementById('codeBox')?.value || '';
            const r = document.getElementById('codeResult');
            if (r)
                r.textContent = /return|print|System\.out/.test(c) ? 'Simulation output: solution submitted for evaluation.' : 'Write a complete function first, then run again.';
        }
        else if (a === 'openPrep') {
            state.screen = 'app';
            state.view = 'dashboard';
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'openAI' || a === 'toggleAI') {
            if (!state.authed)
                return;
            state.aiOpen = !state.aiOpen;
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'sendAI') {
            if (!state.authed)
                return;
            const v = document.getElementById('aiInput')?.value || '';
            if (v.trim()) {
                const out = document.getElementById('aiAnswer');
                if (out)
                    out.textContent = aiAnswer(v);
                const inp = document.getElementById('aiInput');
                if (inp)
                    inp.value = '';
            }
        }
        else if (a === 'workspaceSend') {
            if (!state.authed)
                return;
            const input = document.getElementById('workspaceChat');
            const v = input?.value || '';
            if (v.trim()) {
                if (!state.workspaceConvo?.length) {
                    state.workspaceConvo = [
                        {
                            who: 'ai', html: 'Hello! I know your branch, target job and company context. Ask me to plan study, explain a concept, prepare for an interview or remind you about your weekly work.'
                        }, {
                            who: 'user', html: 'What should I do today?'
                        }, {
                            who: 'ai', html: 'Start with your highest-priority concept, do two worked examples, then complete one small evidence task. I’ll update progress from the evidence you actually submit.'
                        }
                    ];
                }
                state.workspaceConvo.push({
                    who: 'user', html: escape(v.trim())
                });
                state.workspaceConvo.push({
                    who: 'ai', html: escape(aiAnswer(v.trim()))
                });
                if (input)
                    input.value = '';
                render();
                setTimeout(() => { const stream = document.getElementById('dashChat'); if (stream)
                    stream.scrollTop = stream.scrollHeight; }, 0);
            }
        }
        else if (a === 'completeWeekly') {
            state.weeklyDone = true;
            state.skillEvidence = Math.min(100, (state.skillEvidence || 35) + 8);
            showToast('Weekly assignment evidence recorded. Your skill profile can now be updated from this evidence.');
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'markAttendance') {
            state.attendance = 7;
            showToast('Weekly attendance recorded.');
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'startWeeklyAssessment') {
            state.assessment = {
                ...state.assessment, mode: 'weekly', q: 0, answers: {}, camera: false, mic: false, screen: false, practicalDone: false, bank: [
                    ...weeklyQuestionBank()
                ].sort(() => Math.random() - .5).slice(0, 6)
            };
            prepareAssessment();
        }
        else if (a === 'startConceptPractice') {
            showToast('Practice mode opened: theory → examples → hands-on task → quick check.');
        }
        else if (a === 'startInterview') {
            showToast('Adaptive interview opened. Voice responses and NLP-style communication feedback are part of the production flow.');
        }
        else if (a === 'completeToday') {
            state.todayCarry = false;
            state.todayDone = true;
            showToast('Today’s plan completed. No carry-over will be added tomorrow.');
            render();
        }
        else if (a === 'markAbsent') {
            state.todayCarry = true;
            state.todayDone = false;
            showToast('Today marked incomplete. The unfinished task is carried into tomorrow with a 24-hour priority window.');
            render();
        }
    }
    let enhancingUI = false;
    const companyChoiceList = [
        'TCS', 'Infosys', 'Accenture', 'IBM', 'Deloitte', 'Amazon', 'Microsoft', 'Wipro', 'Cognizant', 'Capgemini', 'HCLTech', 'Tech Mahindra', 'Not sure yet'
    ];
    function updatePermissionButtonState() {
        const c = document.getElementById('allowCameraBtn'), m = document.getElementById('allowMicBtn'), s = document.getElementById('allowScreenBtn'), go = document.getElementById('enterAssessmentBtn');
        if (c)
            c.textContent = state.assessment.camera ? '✓ Camera enabled' : 'Allow camera access';
        if (m)
            m.textContent = state.assessment.mic ? '✓ Microphone enabled' : 'Allow microphone access';
        if (s)
            s.textContent = state.assessment.screen ? '✓ Screen sharing enabled' : 'Allow screen sharing';
        if (go) {
            go.disabled = !(state.assessment.camera && state.assessment.mic && state.assessment.screen);
            go.style.opacity = go.disabled ? '.55' : '1';
            go.style.cursor = go.disabled ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer';
        }
    }
    async function enableCamera() {
        try {
            if (!window.isSecureContext || !navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) {
                showToast('Camera access needs a secure browser session. Please open the preview in a supported HTTPS browser.');
                return;
            }
            if (state.assessment.cameraStream?.getTracks)
                state.assessment.cameraStream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
            const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
                video: true, audio: false
            });
            state.assessment.cameraStream = stream;
            state.assessment.camera = true;
            state.assessment.micTrack = null;
            state.assessment.micAvailable = !!state.assessment.micStream?.getAudioTracks?.().some(t => t.readyState === 'live');
            stream.getVideoTracks()[0]?.addEventListener('ended', () => { state.assessment.camera = false; updatePermissionButtonState(); });
            showToast('Camera access enabled.');
            updatePermissionButtonState();
        }
        catch (e) {
            showToast('Camera permission was blocked. Choose Allow camera access in the browser prompt/site settings, then try again.');
        }
        render();
        enhanceUI();
    }
    async function enableMic() {
        try {
            if (!window.isSecureContext || !navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) {
                showToast('Microphone access needs a secure browser session. Please open the preview in a supported HTTPS browser.');
                return;
            }
            if (state.assessment.micStream?.getTracks)
                state.assessment.micStream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
            const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
                video: false, audio: true
            });
            state.assessment.micStream = stream;
            state.assessment.mic = true;
            stream.getAudioTracks()[0]?.addEventListener('ended', () => { state.assessment.mic = false; updatePermissionButtonState(); });
            showToast('Microphone access enabled.');
            updatePermissionButtonState();
        }
        catch (e) {
            showToast('Microphone permission was blocked. Choose Allow microphone access in the browser prompt/site settings, then try again.');
        }
        render();
        enhanceUI();
    }
    async function enableScreen() {
        try {
            if (!window.isSecureContext || !navigator.mediaDevices?.getDisplayMedia) {
                showToast('Screen sharing needs a supported secure browser session. Please open the preview in an HTTPS browser.');
                return;
            }
            if (state.assessment.displayStream?.getTracks)
                state.assessment.displayStream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
            const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getDisplayMedia({
                video: true, audio: false
            });
            state.assessment.displayStream = stream;
            state.assessment.screen = true;
            const track = stream.getVideoTracks()[0];
            track?.addEventListener('ended', () => {
                state.assessment.screen = false;
                updatePermissionButtonState();
                if (state.screen === 'assessment') {
                    state.screen = 'diagIntro';
                    render();
                    showToast('Screen sharing stopped. Re-enable it before continuing.');
                }
            });
            showToast('Screen sharing enabled. Keep the shared screen active during the assessment.');
            updatePermissionButtonState();
        }
        catch (e) {
            showToast('Screen sharing was cancelled or blocked. Choose a screen/window in the browser share prompt, then try again.');
        }
        render();
        enhanceUI();
    }
    prepareAssessment = async function () {
        if (!(state.assessment.camera && state.assessment.mic && state.assessment.screen)) {
            showToast('Enable camera, microphone and screen sharing first. All three are required to continue.');
            return;
        }
        try {
            await document.documentElement.requestFullscreen?.();
            state.assessment.fullscreen = !!document.fullscreenElement;
        }
        catch (e) { }
        state.assessment.consent = true;
        state.screen = 'assessment';
        render();
        enhanceUI();
        attachVideo();
    };
    stopStreams = function () {
        for (const key of [
            'stream', 'cameraStream', 'micStream', 'displayStream'
        ]) {
            const s = state.assessment[key];
            if (s?.getTracks)
                s.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
        }
        state.assessment.camera = false;
        state.assessment.mic = false;
        state.assessment.screen = false;
    };
    attachVideo = function () {
        const video = document.getElementById('camVideo');
        if (!video)
            return;
        const tracks = [];
        const cs = state.assessment.cameraStream, ms = state.assessment.micStream;
        if (cs?.getVideoTracks)
            tracks.push(...cs.getVideoTracks());
        if (ms?.getAudioTracks)
            tracks.push(...ms.getAudioTracks());
        if (tracks.length) {
            video.srcObject = new MediaStream(tracks);
            video.muted = true;
            video.play?.().catch(() => { });
        }
    };
    function toggleMultiChoice(value) {
        if (state.step === 1) {
            state.jobGoals = state.jobGoals || [];
            if (state.jobGoals.includes(value))
                state.jobGoals = state.jobGoals.filter(x => x !== value);
            else
                state.jobGoals.push(value);
            state.role = state.jobGoals.join(', ');
        }
        else if (state.step === 2) {
            state.companies = state.companies || [];
            if (value === 'Not sure yet') {
                if (state.companyNoIdea) {
                    state.companyNoIdea = false;
                }
                else {
                    state.companies = [];
                    state.companyNoIdea = true;
                }
            }
            else {
                state.companyNoIdea = false;
                if (state.companies.includes(value))
                    state.companies = state.companies.filter(x => x !== value);
                else
                    state.companies.push(value);
            }
            state.company = state.companyNoIdea ? 'Not sure yet' : state.companies.join(', ');
        }
    }
    function confirmMultiChoice() {
        if (state.step === 1) {
            if (!state.jobGoals?.length) {
                showToast('Choose at least one target job first.');
                return;
            }
            state.convo.push({
                who: 'user', html: escape(state.role)
            });
            state.convo.push({
                who: 'ai', html: 'Nice. I’ll keep those job goals in view and ask focused questions. Next, choose the companies you’d like to target.'
            });
            state.step = 2;
            render();
            enhanceUI();
            return;
        }
        if (state.step === 2) {
            if (!state.companies?.length && !state.companyNoIdea) {
                showToast('Choose at least one company, or select “Not sure yet.”');
                return;
            }
            state.company = state.companyNoIdea ? 'Not sure yet' : state.companies.join(', ');
            state.convo.push({
                who: 'user', html: escape(state.company)
            });
            state.convo.push({
                who: 'ai', html: state.companyNoIdea ? 'That’s completely fine. I’ll prepare you for common placement patterns across companies.' : 'Perfect. I’ll align your preparation with ' + escape(state.company) + '-style expectations.'
            });
            state.step = 3;
            render();
            enhanceUI();
        }
    }
    function enhanceWelcome() {
        if (state.screen !== 'welcome')
            return;
        state.jobGoals = state.jobGoals || [];
        state.companies = state.companies || [];
        const badge = root.querySelector('.welcome .eyebrow');
        if (badge)
            badge.textContent = 'WELCOME, ' + (state.studentName || 'STUDENT').toUpperCase() + '!';
        const first = root.querySelector('#welcomeChat .chat-msg.ai');
        if (first && state.convo.length === 1 && /I’ll keep this simple|Welcome student/i.test(first.textContent || '')) {
            first.innerHTML = 'Hey <b>' + escape(state.studentName || 'there') + '!</b> 👋 Before we dive in, let’s get to know you a little better. I’ll ask just a few focused questions so I can build the right placement route for you.';
        }
        if (state.step === 1 || state.step === 2) {
            const wrap = root.querySelector('.quick-choices');
            if (!wrap || wrap.dataset.multiEnhanced === '1')
                return;
            const options = state.step === 1 ? getOptions(1) : companyChoiceList;
            wrap.innerHTML = '';
            options.forEach(value => { const b = document.createElement('button'); const selected = state.step === 1 ? state.jobGoals.includes(value) : (value === 'Not sure yet' ? state.companyNoIdea : state.companies.includes(value)); b.className = 'chip ' + (selected ? 'selected' : ''); b.textContent = value; b.type = 'button'; b.setAttribute('data-welcome-choice', value); b.addEventListener('click', () => { toggleMultiChoice(value); render(); }); wrap.appendChild(b); });
            wrap.dataset.multiEnhanced = '1';
            const next = document.createElement('div');
            next.className = 'next-row convo-multi-confirm';
            const btn = document.createElement('button');
            btn.className = 'primary';
            btn.type = 'button';
            btn.textContent = state.step === 1 ? 'Continue with job goals →' : 'Continue with company choices →';
            btn.onclick = confirmMultiChoice;
            next.appendChild(btn);
            wrap.parentElement.insertBefore(next, wrap.nextSibling);
        }
    }
    function enhanceDiagnostic() {
        if (state.screen !== 'diagIntro')
            return;
        const old = root.querySelector('[data-action="prepareAssessment"]');
        if (!old || root.querySelector('.permission-controls'))
            return;
        const box = document.createElement('div');
        box.className = 'permission-controls';
        box.style.display = 'grid';
        box.style.gridTemplateColumns = 'repeat(3,1fr)';
        box.style.gap = '10px';
        box.style.marginTop = '18px';
        box.innerHTML = '<button id="allowCameraBtn" class="secondary" type="button">Allow camera access</button><button id="allowMicBtn" class="secondary" type="button">Allow microphone access</button><button id="allowScreenBtn" class="secondary" type="button">Allow screen sharing</button>';
        old.replaceWith(box);
        const hint = document.createElement('p');
        hint.className = 'small permission-hint';
        hint.style.margin = '10px 0 0';
        hint.textContent = 'Click each permission button and choose Allow in your browser prompt. For screen sharing, select the screen/window you want the assessment to monitor.';
        box.after(hint);
        const go = document.createElement('button');
        go.id = 'enterAssessmentBtn';
        go.className = 'primary';
        go.type = 'button';
        go.style.width = '100%';
        go.style.marginTop = '16px';
        go.textContent = 'Continue to assessment →';
        hint.after(go);
        document.getElementById('allowCameraBtn').onclick = enableCamera;
        document.getElementById('allowMicBtn').onclick = enableMic;
        document.getElementById('allowScreenBtn').onclick = enableScreen;
        go.onclick = prepareAssessment;
        updatePermissionButtonState();
    }
    function enhanceUI() {
        if (enhancingUI)
            return;
        enhancingUI = true;
        enhanceWelcome();
        enhanceDiagnostic();
        enhancingUI = false;
    }
    const originalAction = action;
    action = function (a) {
        if (a === 'authSubmit') {
            if (state.authMode === 'signup') {
                const n = document.getElementById('name');
                state.studentName = (n?.value || '').trim();
                if (!state.studentName) {
                    showToast('Please enter your name first.');
                    return;
                }
            }
            else {
                const email = document.getElementById('authEmail')?.value || '';
                if (!state.studentName)
                    state.studentName = (email.split('@')[0] || 'Student').replace(/[._-]+/g, ' ').trim() || 'Student';
            }
        }
        originalAction(a);
        if (a === 'authSubmit' && state.authMode === 'signup' && state.screen === 'welcome') {
            state.jobGoals = [];
            state.companies = [];
            state.step = 0;
            state.convo = [
                {
                    who: 'ai', html: 'Hey <b>' + escape(state.studentName) + '</b>! 👋 Before we dive in, let’s get to know you a little better. I’ll ask just a few focused questions so I can build the right placement route for you.'
                }
            ];
            render();
            enhanceUI();
        }
    };
    render();
    enhanceUI();
    function shellFloat() {
        if (!state.authed)
            return `<div class="ai-float ${state.aiOpen ? 'open' : ''}" id="aiFloat"><button class="ai-mini" data-action="toggleAI" aria-label="Open PLACEMENT360 AI">${mascot}</button><div class="ai-pop" style="padding:12px"><div class="title">✦ PLACEMENT360 AI</div><div class="small">Your coach stays here. No page change.</div><div style="max-height:190px;overflow:auto;margin-top:9px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:7px">${(state.aiMessages || [
                {
                    who: 'ai', text: 'Hi! I’m your PLACEMENT360 coach. Ask me what to study, what to do next, or how to improve.'
                }
            ]).slice(-5).map(m => `<div style="padding:9px 10px;border-radius:12px;line-height:1.4;font-size:12px;background:${m.who === 'ai' ? '#eaf4ff' : '#eeeaff'};align-self:${m.who === 'ai' ? 'flex-start' : 'flex-end'};max-width:90%">${escape(m.text)}</div>`).join('')}</div><div class="quick-choices" style="margin:9px 0 7px"><button class="chip" data-ai-prompt="What should I study today?">Study today</button><button class="chip" data-ai-prompt="Explain my weak concept">Explain gap</button><button class="chip" data-ai-prompt="Remind me about my task">Remind me</button></div><div class="ai-input"><input id="aiInput" placeholder="Message your coach…"><button data-action="sendAI">Send</button></div></div></div>`;
    }
    const existingAction = action;
    action = function (a) {
        if (a === 'toggleAI') {
            if (!state.authed)
                return;
            state.aiOpen = !state.aiOpen;
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'sendAI') {
            if (!state.authed)
                return;
            const input = document.getElementById('aiInput');
            const value = (input?.value || '').trim();
            if (!value)
                return;
            state.aiMessages = state.aiMessages || [];
            state.aiMessages.push({
                who: 'user', text: value
            });
            state.aiMessages.push({
                who: 'ai', text: aiAnswer(value)
            });
            state.aiOpen = true;
            render();
            return;
        }
        existingAction(a);
    };
    function bindAIQuickPrompts() {
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-ai-prompt]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
            if (!state.authed)
                return;
            const value = b.dataset.aiPrompt || '';
            state.aiMessages = state.aiMessages || [];
            state.aiMessages.push({
                who: 'user', text: value
            });
            state.aiMessages.push({
                who: 'ai', text: aiAnswer(value)
            });
            state.aiOpen = true;
            render();
        });
    }
    const oldBind = bind;
    bind = function () { oldBind(); bindAIQuickPrompts(); };
    const P360_STORE = 'placement360:user:';
    const P360_24H = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    function clearLegacyAccountsOnce() {
        try {
            const flag = 'placement360:accounts-reset-v2';
            if (localStorage.getItem(flag))
                return;
            Object.keys(localStorage).filter(k => k.indexOf(P360_STORE) === 0 || /^placement360:(account|auth|user|profile)/i.test(k)).forEach(k => localStorage.removeItem(k));
            localStorage.removeItem('placement360:accounts-reset-v1');
            localStorage.setItem(flag, '1');
        }
        catch (e) { }
    }
    function p360UserKey(email) { return P360_STORE + String(email || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
    function p360LoadUser(email) {
        try {
            const raw = localStorage.getItem(p360UserKey(email));
            return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
        }
        catch (e) {
            return null;
        }
    }
    function p360Persist() {
        if (!state.userEmail)
            return;
        try {
            const a = state.assessment || {};
            const rec = {
                email: state.userEmail, studentName: state.studentName || '', branch: state.branch || '', role: state.role || '',
                company: state.company || '', companyNoIdea: !!state.companyNoIdea, jobGoals: state.jobGoals || [], companies: state.companies || [],
                step: state.step || 0, convo: state.convo || [], diagnosis: state.diagnosis || null, diagnosticStarted: !!state.diagnosticStarted,
                weeklyAssessmentDone: !!state.weeklyAssessmentDone, todayCarry: !!state.todayCarry,
                assessmentLockUntil: Number(state.assessmentLockUntil || 0), assessmentStartedAt: Number(state.assessmentStartedAt || a.startedAt || 0),
                assessment: {
                    mode: a.mode || '', q: Number(a.q || 0), answers: a.answers || {}, bank: a.bank || [], practicalDone: !!a.practicalDone,
                    voiceScore: Number(a.voiceScore || 0)
                }
            };
            localStorage.setItem(p360UserKey(state.userEmail), JSON.stringify(rec));
        }
        catch (e) { }
    }
    function p360ApplyUser(u) {
        state.userEmail = u.email || state.userEmail || '';
        state.studentName = u.studentName || state.studentName || 'Student';
        state.branch = u.branch || '';
        state.role = u.role || '';
        state.company = u.company || '';
        state.companyNoIdea = !!u.companyNoIdea;
        state.jobGoals = u.jobGoals || [];
        state.companies = u.companies || [];
        state.step = Number(u.step || 0);
        state.convo = u.convo || [];
        state.diagnosis = u.diagnosis || null;
        state.diagnosticStarted = !!u.diagnosticStarted;
        state.weeklyAssessmentDone = !!u.weeklyAssessmentDone;
        state.todayCarry = !!u.todayCarry;
        state.assessmentLockUntil = Number(u.assessmentLockUntil || 0);
        state.assessmentStartedAt = Number(u.assessmentStartedAt || 0);
        const pa = u.assessment || {};
        state.assessment = {
            ...state.assessment, mode: pa.mode || '', q: Number(pa.q || 0), answers: pa.answers || {}, bank: pa.bank || [], practicalDone: !!pa.practicalDone, voiceScore: Number(pa.voiceScore || 0), camera: false, mic: false, screen: false, stream: null, cameraStream: null, micStream: null, displayStream: null, fullscreen: false
        };
    }
    function p360AssessmentLocked() {
        const until = Number(state.assessmentLockUntil || 0);
        if (until && Date.now() >= until) {
            state.assessmentLockUntil = 0;
            state.assessmentStartedAt = 0;
            return false;
        }
        return until > Date.now();
    }
    function p360DiagnosticPending() { return !!state.authed && !state.diagnosis; }
    function p360ForceGate() {
        if (p360DiagnosticPending()) {
            if (state.screen === 'assessment' && state.assessment.mode === 'diagnostic')
                return true;
            state.screen = state.step >= 3 ? 'welcome' : 'welcome';
            state.view = 'dashboard';
            render();
            showToast('Complete your adaptive diagnostic before moving ahead.');
            return true;
        }
        if (p360AssessmentLocked()) {
            state.screen = 'app';
            state.view = 'assessment';
            render();
            showToast('Your assessment is locked for this 24-hour window. Complete it before opening another section.');
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    function p360EnhanceAssessment() {
        if (state.screen !== 'assessment')
            return;
        const host = root.querySelector('.assessment');
        if (!host)
            return;
        if (!root.querySelector('#endAssessmentBtn')) {
            const row = document.createElement('div');
            row.style.cssText = 'display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin:12px 0 10px;flex-wrap:wrap';
            const note = document.createElement('div');
            note.className = 'small';
            note.textContent = state.assessment.mode === 'weekly' ? 'This assessment stays locked across logout/login for 24 hours unless you finish it.' : 'This diagnostic has no 24-hour timeout. You must complete it before entering the placement workspace.';
            const end = document.createElement('button');
            end.id = 'endAssessmentBtn';
            end.className = 'secondary';
            end.type = 'button';
            end.textContent = state.assessment.mode === 'weekly' ? 'End assessment' : 'Exit diagnostic';
            end.onclick = () => action(state.assessment.mode === 'weekly' ? 'endAssessment' : 'logoutDuringAssessment');
            row.append(note, end);
            const first = host.querySelector('.proctor');
            if (first)
                first.before(row);
            else
                host.prepend(row);
        }
    }
    function p360EnhanceAssessmentPage() {
        if (state.screen !== 'app' || state.view !== 'assessment' || !p360AssessmentLocked())
            return;
        const content = root.querySelector('.content');
        if (!content || root.querySelector('#assessmentLockNotice'))
            return;
        const n = document.createElement('div');
        n.id = 'assessmentLockNotice';
        n.className = 'guard';
        n.style.marginBottom = '16px';
        const remaining = Math.max(0, state.assessmentLockUntil - Date.now());
        const hrs = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(remaining / 3600000));
        n.innerHTML = '<b>24-hour assessment lock active.</b><br><span class="small">You can log out and return later, but the next sections stay locked for about ' + hrs + ' more hour(s) until the current assessment is completed or the 24-hour window expires.</span>';
        content.prepend(n);
    }
    const baseShellFloatForGate = shellFloat;
    shellFloat = function () {
        if (state.screen === 'assessment' || state.screen === 'diagIntro' || state.screen === 'loading')
            return '';
        return baseShellFloatForGate();
    };
    const baseBindForGate = bind;
    bind = function () {
        baseBindForGate();
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-view]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
            if (!state.authed)
                return;
            if (p360DiagnosticPending() || p360AssessmentLocked()) {
                p360ForceGate();
                return;
            }
            state.view = b.dataset.view;
            state.screen = 'app';
            render();
        });
        p360EnhanceAssessment();
        p360EnhanceAssessmentPage();
    };
    const baseRenderForGate = render;
    render = function () {
        baseRenderForGate();
        if (state.authed)
            p360Persist();
        p360EnhanceAssessment();
        p360EnhanceAssessmentPage();
    };
    const baseActionForGate = action;
    action = function (a) {
        if (a === 'authSubmit') {
            const email = (document.getElementById('authEmail')?.value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
            if (!email) {
                showToast('Enter your email first.');
                return;
            }
            if (state.authMode === 'signup') {
                const name = (document.getElementById('name')?.value || '').trim();
                if (!name) {
                    showToast('Please enter your name first.');
                    return;
                }
                if (p360LoadUser(email)) {
                    showToast('An account already exists. Please use Login.');
                    return;
                }
                state.userEmail = email;
                state.studentName = name;
                state.authed = true;
                state.diagnosis = null;
                state.diagnosticStarted = false;
                state.assessmentLockUntil = 0;
                state.branch = '';
                state.role = '';
                state.company = '';
                state.companyNoIdea = false;
                state.jobGoals = [];
                state.companies = [];
                state.step = 0;
                state.convo = [
                    {
                        who: 'ai', html: 'Hey <b>' + escape(name) + '</b>! 👋 Before we dive in, let’s get to know you a little better. I’ll ask just a few focused questions so I can build the right placement route for you.'
                    }
                ];
                state.screen = 'welcome';
                state.view = 'dashboard';
                render();
                return;
            }
            const u = p360LoadUser(email);
            if (!u) {
                showToast('No existing account found for this email. Create an account first.');
                return;
            }
            state.userEmail = email;
            state.authed = true;
            p360ApplyUser(u);
            if (!state.diagnosis) {
                state.screen = 'welcome';
                state.view = 'dashboard';
                render();
                showToast('Diagnosis is still pending. Complete it before entering the placement workspace.');
                return;
            }
            if (p360AssessmentLocked()) {
                state.screen = 'app';
                state.view = 'assessment';
                render();
                showToast('Your 24-hour assessment window is still active. Complete the assessment before opening another section.');
                return;
            }
            state.screen = 'app';
            state.view = 'dashboard';
            render();
            showToast('Welcome back. Your completed profile is loaded.');
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'start' && state.authed && (p360DiagnosticPending() || p360AssessmentLocked())) {
            p360ForceGate();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'landing') {
            if (state.authed && state.screen === 'assessment' && state.assessment.mode === 'weekly') {
                state.assessmentLockUntil = state.assessmentLockUntil || Date.now() + P360_24H;
                state.assessmentStartedAt = state.assessmentStartedAt || Date.now();
                p360Persist();
            }
            else if (state.authed && state.screen === 'assessment' && state.assessment.mode === 'diagnostic') {
                state.diagnosticStarted = true;
                p360Persist();
            }
            baseActionForGate(a);
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'toggleAI' || a === 'openAI') {
            if (state.screen === 'assessment') {
                showToast('The AI companion is paused during assessments. Finish or end the test first.');
                return;
            }
        }
        if (a === 'startDiagnostic') {
            if (!state.authed)
                return;
            state.diagnosticStarted = true;
            state.assessment = {
                ...state.assessment, mode: 'diagnostic', q: state.assessment.mode === 'diagnostic' ? state.assessment.q : 0, answers: state.assessment.mode === 'diagnostic' ? (state.assessment.answers || {}) : {}, practicalDone: false, voiceScore: 0, camera: false, mic: false, screen: false, stream: null, cameraStream: null, micStream: null, displayStream: null
            };
            state.screen = 'diagIntro';
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'startWeeklyAssessment') {
            if (p360DiagnosticPending()) {
                p360ForceGate();
                return;
            }
            const resume = p360AssessmentLocked() && state.assessment.mode === 'weekly' && state.assessment.bank?.length;
            state.assessment = {
                ...state.assessment, mode: 'weekly', q: resume ? state.assessment.q : 0, answers: resume ? (state.assessment.answers || {}) : {}, camera: false, mic: false, screen: false, practicalDone: resume ? !!state.assessment.practicalDone : false, bank: resume ? state.assessment.bank : [
                    ...weeklyQuestionBank()
                ].sort(() => Math.random() - .5).slice(0, 6)
            };
            state.assessmentStartedAt = resume ? (state.assessmentStartedAt || Date.now()) : Date.now();
            state.assessmentLockUntil = resume ? (state.assessmentLockUntil || Date.now() + P360_24H) : Date.now() + P360_24H;
            state.weeklyAssessmentDone = false;
            state.screen = 'app';
            state.view = 'assessment';
            render();
            prepareAssessment();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'prepareAssessment') {
            prepareAssessment();
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (state.screen === 'assessment') {
                    state.assessmentStartedAt = state.assessmentStartedAt || Date.now();
                    if (state.assessment.mode === 'weekly')
                        state.assessmentLockUntil = state.assessmentLockUntil || Date.now() + P360_24H;
                    p360Persist();
                    render();
                }
            }, 80);
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'endAssessment') {
            if (state.assessment.mode !== 'weekly')
                return;
            state.assessmentLockUntil = Date.now() + P360_24H;
            state.assessmentStartedAt = state.assessmentStartedAt || Date.now();
            stopStreams();
            state.screen = 'app';
            state.view = 'assessment';
            p360Persist();
            render();
            showToast('Assessment ended. The remaining 24-hour window is still locked. Return and complete it to continue.');
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'logoutDuringAssessment') {
            state.diagnosticStarted = true;
            stopStreams();
            p360Persist();
            baseActionForGate('landing');
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'nextAssessment') {
            const qs = currentQuestions(), last = state.assessment.q === qs.length - 1;
            baseActionForGate(a);
            if (last && state.assessment.mode === 'diagnostic')
                setTimeout(() => { state.diagnosis && p360Persist(); }, 3000);
            if (last && state.assessment.mode === 'weekly') {
                state.assessmentLockUntil = 0;
                state.assessmentStartedAt = 0;
                p360Persist();
            }
            return;
        }
        if ((a === 'openPrep' || a === 'landing') && p360DiagnosticPending()) {
            p360ForceGate();
            return;
        }
        baseActionForGate(a);
    };
    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', e => {
        if (!state.authed)
            return;
        if (state.screen === 'assessment' && state.assessment.mode === 'weekly') {
            state.assessmentLockUntil = state.assessmentLockUntil || Date.now() + P360_24H;
            state.assessmentStartedAt = state.assessmentStartedAt || Date.now();
            p360Persist();
            e.preventDefault();
            e.returnValue = 'Your assessment is still active.';
        }
        else if (state.screen === 'assessment' && state.assessment.mode === 'diagnostic') {
            state.diagnosticStarted = true;
            p360Persist();
        }
    });
    const prevEnhanceWelcome = enhanceWelcome;
    enhanceWelcome = function () {
        prevEnhanceWelcome();
        if (state.screen === 'welcome' && state.authed && !state.diagnosis) {
            const card = root.querySelector('.convo-card');
            if (card && !root.querySelector('#diagnosticGateNote') && state.step >= 3) {
                const n = document.createElement('div');
                n.id = 'diagnosticGateNote';
                n.className = 'diagnosis-note';
                n.style.marginTop = '18px';
                n.innerHTML = '<b>Diagnostic required:</b> you are not considered a returning placement user until this diagnostic is completed. There is no 24-hour expiry on this requirement.';
                card.appendChild(n);
            }
        }
    };
    function studyInit() {
        state.studyProgress = state.studyProgress || {};
        state.studyReviewed = state.studyReviewed || {};
        state.labDone = state.labDone || {};
        state.studyAttendance = state.studyAttendance || {};
        state.catchUpQueue = state.catchUpQueue || [];
        state.learningStreak = state.learningStreak || 0;
        state.activeStudyTopic = state.activeStudyTopic || '';
    }
    function studyCatalog() {
        const b = state.branch || 'CSE';
        if (/ECE/i.test(b))

            return [
