    function studyKey(t) { return String(t).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-'); }
    function studyNext() {
        studyInit();
        const all = studyCatalog().flatMap(c => c[1]);
        /* A diagnostic with no practical code is a foundation gate.
           Do not skip ahead based on other answers; start preparation from the first basic topic. */
        if (state.diagnosis?.foundationStart && all.length)
            return all[0];
        return all.find(t => state.studyProgress[studyKey(t)] !== 100) || all[0];
    }
    function studyTopicDetail(t) {
        studyInit();
        const k = studyKey(t), done = state.studyProgress[k] === 100;
        const c = materialContent(t);
        return '<div class="panel study-detail"><span class="eyebrow">STUDY NOW</span><h2>' + escape(t) + '</h2>' +
            '<p class="muted">Real study notes are opened immediately. Nothing is marked “reviewed” before you actually read it.</p>' +
            '<div class="panel" style="margin-top:14px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbf9ff,#f1f9ff)"><b>Core idea</b><p class="muted">' + escape(c.summary) + '</p></div>' +
            '<div class="cards"><div class="mini-card"><b>Key points</b><ul style="margin:9px 0 0;padding-left:19px">' + c.points.map(x => '<li class="small" style="margin:7px 0">' + escape(x) + '</li>').join('') + '</ul></div>' +
            '<div class="mini-card"><b>Worked example</b><pre style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-family:ui-monospace,monospace;font-size:12px;background:#222033;color:#fff;border-radius:12px;padding:12px">' + escape(c.example) + '</pre><b>Try this</b><p class="small">' + escape(c.practice) + '</p></div></div>' +
            '<div class="diagnosis-note"><b>Study flow:</b> Read the notes → understand the example → try the task → open the lab → take the quick check. Only later evidence affects verified skill progress.</div>' +
            '<div style="display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:14px"><button class="primary" data-study-lab="' + escapeAttr(t) + '">Start lab →</button><button class="secondary" data-view="studyMaterials">Back to materials</button></div></div>';
    }
    function studyGuide() {
        studyInit();
        const cats = studyCatalog(), all = cats.flatMap(c => c[1]), done = all.filter(t => state.studyProgress[studyKey(t)] === 100).length, pct = Math.round(done / Math.max(1, all.length) * 100), next = studyNext();
        const gateBanner = state.diagnosis?.foundationStart ? '<div class="diagnosis-note" style="margin-bottom:14px;background:#fff4f2;border-color:#f2d5cf"><b>Start from preparation:</b> your practical diagnostic result is 0 because no working code was submitted. The first basic topic is your starting point; diagnostic results do not auto-complete study topics.</div>' : '';
        return gateBanner + '<div class="panel study-hero"><div><span class="eyebrow">AI STUDY GUIDE</span><h2>Your placement curriculum is now a guided learning path.</h2><p class="muted">The AI turns your diagnostic and target role into a sequence of modules. Every topic has study material, examples, practice, labs, quick checks and a formal assessment path.</p><div class="study-seq"><span>01 Learn</span><span>02 Examples</span><span>03 Practice</span><span>04 Lab</span><span>05 Quick check</span><span>06 Assess</span></div><button class="primary" data-study-open="' + escapeAttr(next) + '">Continue: ' + escape(next) + ' →</button></div><div class="panel" style="margin:0;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#faf8ff,#f1f9ff)"><div class="small">LEARNING PATH COMPLETION</div><div class="metric-big">' + pct + '%</div><div class="skillbar"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div><p class="small">' + done + ' of ' + all.length + ' topics completed · learning activity: ' + state.learningStreak + ' actions.</p></div></div>' +
            cats.map((c, i) => '<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><span class="eyebrow">MODULE ' + (i + 1) + '</span><h2>' + c[0] + '</h2><span class="small">AI-curated for ' + escape(state.role || 'your target role') + '</span></div><span class="tag">' + c[1].length + ' topics</span></div><div class="study-topic-grid">' + c[1].map(t => { const p = state.studyProgress[studyKey(t)] || 0; return '<button class="study-topic ' + (p === 100 ? 'done' : '') + '" data-study-open="' + escapeAttr(t) + '"><b>' + t + '</b><span>' + (p === 100 ? 'Completed' : 'Notes · lesson · examples · lab · quick check') + '</span><div class="progress-line"><i style="width:' + p + '%"></i></div></button>'; }).join('') + '</div></div>').join('') +
            (state.activeStudyTopic ? studyTopicDetail(state.activeStudyTopic) : '');
    }
    function studyMaterials() {
        const topics = studyCatalog().flatMap(c => c[1].map(t => ({
            topic: t, module: c[0]
        })));
        return '<div class="study-materials-shell"><div class="panel"><span class="eyebrow">STUDY MATERIAL LIBRARY</span><h2>Centralized material for every topic.</h2><p class="muted">Use the library when you want notes first, video-style explanation, worked examples, interview preparation or practice.</p><div class="material-grid">' + topics.map((x, i) => '<article class="material-card"><span class="level-chip">' + [
            'Reading', 'Micro-lesson', 'Worked examples', 'Cheat sheet', 'Interview guide'
        ][i % 5] + '</span><h3>' + x.topic + '</h3><p class="small">' + x.module + '<br>Concept summary · key patterns · common mistakes · practice checklist</p><button class="secondary" data-study-open="' + escapeAttr(x.topic) + '">Study this topic</button></article>').join('') + '</div></div></div>';
    }
    function studyLabs() {

Hints and retries are encouraged; formal assessments remain separate.</p><div class="lab-note"><b>Recommended order:</b> Study material → worked example → lab → quick check → weekly assessment.</div><div class="lab-grid">' + labs.map((l, i) => '<article class="lab-card"><span class="level-chip">LAB ' + (i + 1) + '</span><h3>' + l.topic + '</h3><p class="small">' + l.module + ' · practice tasks · hints · retry available</p><span class="lab-status ' + (state.labDone[studyKey(l.topic)] ? 'done' : '') + '">' + (state.labDone[studyKey(l.topic)] ? '✓ Completed' : 'Not started') + '</span><br><button class="primary" data-study-lab="' + escapeAttr(l.topic) + '">' + (state.labDone[studyKey(l.topic)] ? 'Retry lab' : 'Start lab') + '</button></article>').join('') + '</div></div>';
    }

    function learningAttendance() {

        const days = [
            'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat', 'Sun'
        ], present = days.filter(d => state.studyAttendance[d]).length, pct = Math.round(present / 7 * 100);
        return '<div class="panel attendance-hero"><div><span class="eyebrow">LEARNING ATTENDANCE</span><h2>Track participation in the learning journey.</h2><p class="muted">Attendance is linked to learning activity, not just opening the app. A completed study block, lab, assignment or assessment can count as participation.</p></div><div><div class="metric-big">' + pct + '%</div><span class="small">weekly engagement</span></div></div>' +
            '<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><span class="eyebrow">WEEK VIEW</span><h3>Study engagement</h3></div><span class="tag">' + present + '/7 active days</span></div><div class="attendance-week">' + days.map(d => '<button class="attendance-day ' + (state.studyAttendance[d] ? 'present' : '') + '" data-study-attend="' + d + '"><b>' + d + '</b><span>' + (state.studyAttendance[d] ? '●' : '○') + '</span><small>' + (state.studyAttendance[d] ?     }

    const baseViewContentForStudy = viewContent;
    viewContent = function () {
        studyInit();
        if (state.view === 'studyGuide')
            return studyGuide();
        if (state.view === 'studyMaterials')
            return studyMaterials();
        if (state.view === 'labs')
            return studyLabs();
        if (state.view === 'assessment')
            return learningAssessments();
        if (state.view === 'attendance')
            return learningAttendance();
        if (state.view === 'dashboard')
            return studyDashboardHome();
        if (state.view === 'studyTopic')
            return studyTopicDetail(state.activeStudyTopic);
        return baseViewContentForStudy();
    };
    const baseDashboardForStudy = dashboard;
    dashboard = function () { studyInit(); return topbar() + '<div class="workspace"><aside class="sidebar"><div class="side-label">AI Workspace</div><button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'dashboard' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="dashboard">◉ Dashboard</button><button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'today' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="today">☀ Today’s Plan</button><div class="side-label">Study Guide</div><button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'studyGuide' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="studyGuide">▤ Learning Path</button><button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'studyMaterials' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="studyMaterials">📚 Study Materials</button><button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'labs' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="labs">⌘ Practice Labs</button><div class="side-label">Evidence</div><button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'assessment' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="assessment">⌁ Assessments</button><button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'assignments' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="assignments">✓ Weekly Assignments</button><button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'attendance' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="attendance">◷ Attendance</button><button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'progress' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="progress">↗ Progress & Evidence</button><div class="side-label">Career</div><button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'resume' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="resume">▤ Resume</button><button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'interview' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="interview">◉ Interview Practice</button><button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'chat' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="chat">✦ AI Conversation</button><button class="side-btn" data-action="landing">← Sign out</button></aside><main class="mainspace"><div class="workspace-head"><div><b>PLACEMENT360</b><div class="small">' + escape(state.branch) + ' · ' + escape(state.role) + ' · ' + escape(state.companyNoIdea ? 'Common placement route' : state.company) + '</div></div><span class="tag">AI learning companion active</span></div><div class="content ' + (state.view === 'studyMaterials' ? 'content-study-materials' : '') + '">' + viewContent() + '</div></main></div>' + shellFloat(); };
    const baseP360PersistForStudy = p360Persist;
    p360Persist = function () {
        studyInit();
        baseP360PersistForStudy();
        if (!state.userEmail)
            return;
        try {
            const key = p360UserKey(state.userEmail), u = p360LoadUser(state.userEmail) || {};
            u.studyProgress = state.studyProgress;
            u.studyReviewed = state.studyReviewed;
            u.labDone = state.labDone;
            u.studyAttendance = state.studyAttendance;
            u.catchUpQueue = state.catchUpQueue;
            u.learningStreak = state.learningStreak;
            localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify({
                ...u, email: state.userEmail
            }));
        }
        catch (e) { }
    };
    const baseP360ApplyUserForStudy = p360ApplyUser;
    p360ApplyUser = function (u) { baseP360ApplyUserForStudy(u); state.studyProgress = u.studyProgress || {}; state.studyReviewed = u.studyReviewed || {}; state.labDone = u.labDone || {}; state.studyAttendance = u.studyAttendance || {}; state.catchUpQueue = u.catchUpQueue || []; state.learningStreak = u.learningStreak || 0; };
    const baseBindForStudy = bind;
    bind = function () { baseBindForStudy(); root.querySelectorAll('[data-study-open]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.activeStudyTopic = b.dataset.studyOpen; state.view = 'studyTopic'; render(); }); root.querySelectorAll('[data-study-review]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { const t = b.dataset.studyReview || ''; state.activeStudyTopic = t; state.view = 'studyTopic'; state.screen = 'app'; markStudySessionStart(); render(); }); root.querySelectorAll('[data-study-lab]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { const t = b.dataset.studyLab; state.activeStudyTopic = t; state.labDone[studyKey(t)] = true; state.view = 'labs'; showToast('Practice lab opened. Hints and retries are available before grading.'); p360Persist(); render(); }); root.querySelectorAll('[data-study-check]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.activeStudyTopic = b.dataset.studyCheck; showToast('Quick check opened. The AI will use the result to choose your next study step.'); }); root.querySelectorAll('[data-study-complete]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { const t = b.dataset.studyComplete; state.studyProgress[studyKey(t)] = 100; state.catchUpQueue = state.catchUpQueue.filter(x => x !== t); state.learningStreak++; p360Persist(); showToast(t + ' completed. Next priority updated.'); render(); }); root.querySelectorAll('[data-study-attend]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { const d = b.dataset.studyAttend; state.studyAttendance[d] = !state.studyAttendance[d]; p360Persist(); showToast(d + ' attendance ' + (state.studyAttendance[d] ? 'marked present.' : 'cleared.')); render(); }); };
    const fitRender = render;
    const landingBeforeAuthButtons = landing;
    landing = function () { const body = landingBeforeAuthButtons(); return '<div style="position:fixed;top:20px;right:32px;z-index:140;display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;background:rgba(255,255,255,.9);padding:6px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:15px;box-shadow:0 12px 30px rgba(61,48,104,.12);backdrop-filter:blur(12px)"><button class="secondary" style="padding:8px 12px;font-size:14px" data-action="loginModeStart">Sign in</button><button class="primary" style="padding:8px 13px;font-size:14px" data-action="signupModeStart">Create account</button></div>' + body; };
    render = function () {
        fitRender();
        if (state.screen === 'welcome' || state.screen === 'diagIntro')
            enhanceUI();
    };
    const fastAuthAction = action;
    action = function (a) {
        if (a === 'loginModeStart') {
            state.authMode = 'login';
            state.screen = 'auth';
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'signupModeStart') {
            state.authMode = 'signup';
            state.screen = 'auth';
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'authSubmit' && state.authMode === 'signup') {
            const btn = root.querySelector('[data-action="authSubmit"]');
            if (btn?.dataset.busy === '1')
                return;
            const name = (document.getElementById('name')?.value || '').trim();
            const email = (document.getElementById('authEmail')?.value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
            if (!name) {
                showToast('Please enter your name first.');
                return;
            }
            if (!email) {
                showToast('Please enter your email first.');
                return;
            }
            if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) {
                showToast('Please enter a valid email address.');
                return;
            }
            if (p360LoadUser(email)) {
                showToast('An account already exists. Please use Login.');
                return;
            }
            if (btn) {
                btn.dataset.busy = '1';
                btn.textContent = 'Creating account…';
                btn.disabled = true;
            }
            state.userEmail = email;
            state.studentName = name;
            state.authed = true;
            state.diagnosis = null;
            state.diagnosticStarted = false;
            state.assessmentLockUntil = 0;
            state.assessmentStartedAt = 0;
            state.branch = '';
            state.role = '';
            state.company = '';
            state.companyNoIdea = false;
            state.jobGoals = [];
            state.companies = [];
            state.step = 0;
            state.assessment = {
                ...state.assessment, mode: '', q: 0, answers: {}, bank: [], camera: false, mic: false, screen: false, practicalDone: false, voiceScore: 0
            };
            state.convo = [
                {
                    who: 'ai', html: 'Hey <b>' + escape(name) + '</b>! 👋 Before we dive in, let’s get to know you a little better. I’ll ask just a few focused questions so I can build the right placement route for you.'
                }
            ];
            try {
                localStorage.setItem(p360UserKey(email), JSON.stringify({
                    email, studentName: name, diagnosis: null, diagnosticStarted: false, assessmentLockUntil: 0, assessmentStartedAt: 0, step: 0, convo: state.convo, jobGoals: [], companies: []
                }));
            }
            catch (e) { }
            state.screen = 'welcome';
            state.view = 'dashboard';
            render();
            return;
        }
        fastAuthAction(a);
    };
    function submitWelcomeSelections() {
        const typed = (document.getElementById('convoInput')?.value || '').trim();
        if (state.screen !== 'welcome') {
            if (typed)
                action('convoSend');
            return;
        }
        if (state.step === 1) {
            const choices = state.jobGoals || [];
            if (choices.length) {
                state.role = choices.join(', ');
                state.convo.push({
                    who: 'user', html: escape(state.role)
                });
                state.convo.push({
                    who: 'ai', html: 'Nice. I’ll keep those job goals in view and ask focused questions. Next, choose the companies you’d like to target.'
                });
                state.step = 2;
                render();
                return;
            }
        }
        if (state.step === 2) {
            const choices = state.companies || [];
            if (choices.length || state.companyNoIdea) {
                state.company = state.companyNoIdea ? 'Not sure yet' : choices.join(', ');
                state.convo.push({
                    who: 'user', html: escape(state.company)
                });
                state.convo.push({
                    who: 'ai', html: state.companyNoIdea ? 'That’s completely fine. I’ll prepare you for common placement patterns across companies.' : 'Perfect. I’ll align your preparation with ' + escape(state.company) + '-style expectations.'
                });
                state.step = 3;
                render();
                return;
            }
        }
        if (typed)
            setConvoAnswer(typed);
        else
            showToast('Choose an option first, then press Send or Enter.');
    }
    function setupWelcomeSend() {
        if (state.screen !== 'welcome')
            return;
        root.querySelectorAll('.convo-wrap .next-row').forEach(x => x.remove());
        const send = root.querySelector('[data-action="convoSend"]');
        if (send) {
            send.onclick = submitWelcomeSelections;
        }
        const input = root.querySelector('#convoInput');
        if (input) {
            input.onkeydown = e => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    submitWelcomeSelections();
                }
            };
        }
    }
    const finalRenderBeforeWelcomeSend = render;
    render = function () { finalRenderBeforeWelcomeSend(); setupWelcomeSend(); };
    const guardedAssessmentSession = assessmentSession;
    assessmentSession = function () { let html = guardedAssessmentSession(); return html.replace('<main class="page">', '<main class="page assessment-page">'); };
    const integrityAssessmentSession = assessmentSession;
    assessmentSession = function () {
        let html = integrityAssessmentSession();
        if (state.screen === 'assessment') {
            html = html.replace('<div class="camera-box"><video id="camVideo" autoplay muted playsinline></video><div class="camera-label">LIVE CAMERA</div></div>', '<div class="camera-box face-frame-box"><video id="camVideo" autoplay muted playsinline></video><div class="camera-label">LIVE CAMERA</div><div class="face-guide"><span></span><small>Face + shoulders must stay inside the frame</small></div></div><div id="cameraFrameStatus" class="small" style="margin-top:8px">Checking camera framing…</div><div id="audioIntegrityStatus" class="small" style="margin-top:5px">Monitoring microphone for background speech…</div>');
        }
        return html;
    };
    const integrityPrepare = prepareAssessment;
    prepareAssessment = function () {
        state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
        state.assessment.faceFrameOk = false;
        state.assessment.backgroundSpeech = false;
        state.assessment.audioBaseline = 0;
        integrityPrepare();
    };
    const integrityAttachVideo = attachVideo;
    attachVideo = function () {
        integrityAttachVideo();
        const video = document.getElementById('camVideo');
        if (!video)
            return;
        video.style.transform = 'scaleX(-1)';
        if (video.dataset.integrityBound === '1')
            return;
        video.dataset.integrityBound = '1';
        video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', () => startCameraIntegrityMonitor());
        if (video.readyState >= 1)
            startCameraIntegrityMonitor();
    };
    function startCameraIntegrityMonitor() {
        if (!state.assessment.stream || state.assessment.frameMonitor)
            return;
        state.assessment.frameMonitor = setInterval(checkCameraFrame, 1200);
        checkCameraFrame();
    }
    async function checkCameraFrame() {
        const video = document.getElementById('camVideo');
        const status = document.getElementById('cameraFrameStatus');
        if (!video || video.videoWidth < 200 || video.videoHeight < 150)
            return;
        let ok = false, detail = 'Keep your face and shoulders visible.';
        try {
            if ('FaceDetector' in window) {
                const detector = new FaceDetector({
                    fastMode: true, maxDetectedFaces: 2
                });
                const faces = await detector.detect(video);
                if (faces.length === 1) {
                    const box = faces[0].boundingBox;
                    const fw = box.width / video.videoWidth, fh = box.height / video.videoHeight, cx = (box.x + box.width / 2) / video.videoWidth, cy = (box.y + box.height / 2) / video.videoHeight;
                    ok = fw > 0.11 && fw < 0.68 && fh > 0.13 && fh < 0.72 && cx > 0.16 && cx < 0.84 && cy > 0.14 && cy < 0.62;
                    detail = ok ? 'Framing OK — face centered with shoulders visible.' : 'Center yourself so your face and both shoulders are clearly visible.';
                }
                else if (faces.length > 1) {
                    detail = 'Multiple faces detected. Only the student should be visible during the exam.';
                }
                else
                    detail = 'Face not detected. Keep your face and both shoulders visible.';
            }
            else {
                ok = true;
                detail = 'Camera active. Keep your face and both shoulders visible.';
            }
        }
        catch (e) {
            ok = true;
        }
        state.assessment.faceFrameOk = ok;
        if (status) {
            status.textContent = detail;
            status.style.color = ok ? 'var(--good)' : 'var(--danger)';
        }
        const next = root.querySelector('[data-action="nextAssessment"]');
        if (next)
            next.disabled = !ok || !!state.assessment.backgroundSpeech || !!state.assessment.focusLocked;
    }
    function startAudioIntegrityMonitor() {
        const stream = state.assessment.stream;
        if (!stream || state.assessment.audioMonitor)
            return;
        try {
            const C = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
            if (!C)
                return;
            const ctx = new C(), src = ctx.createMediaStreamSource(stream), an = ctx.createAnalyser();
            an.fftSize = 1024;
            an.smoothingTimeConstant = .82;
            src.connect(an);
            const data = new Uint8Array(an.fftSize);
            let baseline = 0;
            state.assessment.audioMonitor = {
                ctx, src, an, data, lastSpeech: 0
            };
            state.assessment.audioTimer = setInterval(() => {
                if (state.screen !== 'assessment')
                    return;
                an.getByteTimeDomainData(data);
                let sum = 0;
                for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
                    const d = (data[i] - 128) / 128;
                    sum += d * d;
                }
                const rms = Math.sqrt(sum / data.length);
                baseline = baseline ? baseline * .98 + rms * .02 : rms;
                const speechLike = rms > Math.max(.045, baseline * 2.6);
                if (speechLike)
                    state.assessment.audioMonitor.lastSpeech = Date.now();
                const sustained = Date.now() - (state.assessment.audioMonitor.lastSpeech || 0) < 1800;
                state.assessment.backgroundSpeech = !!sustained && state.assessment.mode !== 'voice';
                const el = document.getElementById('audioIntegrityStatus');
                if (el) {
                    el.textContent = state.assessment.backgroundSpeech ? 'Speech detected on the microphone. Stop talking/background voices before continuing.' : 'Microphone monitoring active — keep the exam environment quiet.';
                    el.style.color = state.assessment.backgroundSpeech ? 'var(--danger)' : 'var(--muted)';
                }
                const next = root.querySelector('[data-action="nextAssessment"]');
                if (next)
                    next.disabled = !state.assessment.faceFrameOk || state.assessment.backgroundSpeech || state.assessment.focusLocked;
            }, 160);
        }
        catch (e) { }
    }
    const integrityRequestProctor = requestProctor;
    requestProctor = async function () {
        const ok = await integrityRequestProctor();
        if (state.assessment.stream)
            startAudioIntegrityMonitor();
        return ok;
    };
    const integrityStopStreams = stopStreams;
    stopStreams = function () {
        if (state.assessment.audioTimer)
            clearInterval(state.assessment.audioTimer);
        if (state.assessment.frameMonitor)
            clearInterval(state.assessment.frameMonitor);
        const m = state.assessment.audioMonitor;
        try {
            m?.ctx?.close();
        }
        catch (e) { }
        state.assessment.audioTimer = null;
        state.assessment.frameMonitor = null;
        state.assessment.audioMonitor = null;
        integrityStopStreams();
    };
    const integrityNextAssessment = action;
    action = function (a) {
        if (a === 'nextAssessment' && state.screen === 'assessment') {
            if (state.assessment.focusLocked) {
                state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
                state.assessment.focusLockReason = '';
            }
            if (!state.assessment.faceFrameOk) {
                showToast('Camera framing is not acceptable. Show your face and both shoulders clearly.');
                return;
            }
            if (state.assessment.backgroundSpeech) {
                showToast('Speech/background voices detected. Keep the exam environment quiet before continuing.');
                return;
            }
        }
        integrityNextAssessment(a);
    };
    const integrityBindAssessment = bind;
    bind = function () {
        integrityBindAssessment();
        if (state.screen === 'assessment') {
            root.querySelectorAll('[data-answer],[data-practical-choice]').forEach(b => b.disabled = !!state.assessment.focusLocked || !!state.assessment.backgroundSpeech || !state.assessment.faceFrameOk);
            const next = root.querySelector('[data-action="nextAssessment"]');
            if (next)
                next.disabled = !!state.assessment.focusLocked || !!state.assessment.backgroundSpeech || !state.assessment.faceFrameOk;
            startAudioIntegrityMonitor();
        }
    };
    /* FINAL EXAM-FLOW FIXES */
    (function () {
        state.assessment.voiceCompleted = !!state.assessment.voiceCompleted;
        state.assessment.recording = false;
        state.assessment.voiceTimer = null;
        state.assessment.voiceRecognition = null;
        /* Enter exam with camera + screen only. Mic is requested per spoken question. */
        prepareAssessment = async function () {
            if (!(state.assessment.camera && state.assessment.screen)) {
                showToast('Enable camera and screen sharing first. Microphone permission is requested at exam start; microphone capture stays OFF until Start recording.');
                return;
            }
            state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
            try {
                await document.documentElement.requestFullscreen?.();
                state.assessment.fullscreen = !!document.fullscreenElement;
            }
            catch (e) { }
            state.assessment.consent = true;
            state.assessment.faceFrameOk = true;
            state.assessment.backgroundSpeech = false;
            state.screen = 'assessment';
            render();
            enhanceUI();
            attachVideo();
        };
        function currentQ() { return currentQuestions()[state.assessment.q] || diagQs[state.assessment.q]; }
        function isVoiceQuestion(q) { return !!q && (q.type === 'voice' || /communication|soft skills/i.test(q.skill || '')); }
        async function stopVoiceCapture(markComplete = true) {
            if (state.assessment.voiceTimer) {
                clearTimeout(state.assessment.voiceTimer);
                state.assessment.voiceTimer = null;
            }
            try {
                state.assessment.voiceRecognition?.stop();
            }
            catch (e) { }
            state.assessment.voiceRecognition = null;
            const ms = state.assessment.micStream;
            if (ms?.getTracks)
                ms.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
            state.assessment.micStream = null;
            state.assessment.mic = false;
            state.assessment.recording = false;
            if (markComplete)
                state.assessment.voiceCompleted = true;
            const btn = root.querySelector('[data-action="recordVoice"]');
            const st = document.getElementById('voiceStatus');
            if (btn) {
                btn.textContent = markComplete ? '✓ Response captured' : '🎙 Start speaking';
                btn.disabled = false;
            }
            if (st)
                st.textContent = markComplete ? 'Speech response captured. You can continue to the next question.' : 'Microphone released.';
        }
        async function startVoiceCapture() {
            if (state.assessment.recording) {
                await stopVoiceCapture(true);
                return;
            }
            try {
                if (!window.isSecureContext || !navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) {
                    showToast('Microphone access requires a secure HTTPS browser session.');
                    return;
                }
                const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
                    video: false, audio: true
                });
                state.assessment.micStream = stream;
                state.assessment.mic = true;
                state.assessment.recording = true;
                state.assessment.voiceCompleted = false;
                const btn = root.querySelector('[data-action="recordVoice"]');
                const st = document.getElementById('voiceStatus');
                if (btn)
                    btn.textContent = '■ Stop speaking';
                if (st)
                    st.textContent = 'Listening… speak your answer. The microphone is active only for this spoken question.';
                const SR = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
                if (SR) {
                    const rec = new SR();
                    rec.continuous = true;
                    rec.interimResults = true;
                    rec.lang = 'en-IN';
                    rec.onresult = () => {
                        if (st)
                            st.textContent = 'Listening… your spoken response is being captured.';
                    };
                    rec.onerror = () => { };
                    state.assessment.voiceRecognition = rec;
                    try {
                        rec.start();
                    }
                    catch (e) { }
                }
                state.assessment.voiceTimer = setTimeout(() => stopVoiceCapture(true), 45000);
            }
            catch (e) {
                state.assessment.mic = false;
                state.assessment.micStream = null;
                showToast('Microphone permission was blocked. Allow the microphone and try the spoken question again.');
            }
        }
        voiceBlock = function () {
            const done = !!state.assessment.voiceCompleted;
            const q = (typeof currentQuestions === 'function' ? currentQuestions()[state.assessment.q] : null) || diagQs[state.assessment.q] || {};
            return `<div class="panel" style="margin-top:16px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbf9ff,#f1f9ff)"><span class="level-chip">COMMUNICATION QUESTION</span><p style="font-size:17px;line-height:1.55;margin:10px 0"><b>${escape(q.q || 'Speak your answer clearly and explain your reasoning.')}</b></p><p class="small">Speak for about 30–45 seconds. Explain your answer clearly and stay on the question.</p><button class="secondary" data-action="recordVoice">${done ? '✓ Response captured' : '🎙 Start speaking'}</button><p id="voiceStatus" class="small" style="margin-top:10px">${done ? 'Speech response captured. You can continue to the next question.' : 'Not started.'}</p></div>`;
        };
        /* Do not disable Next while the camera check is still running. */
        const previousFrameCheck = checkCameraFrame;
        checkCameraFrame = async function () {
            await previousFrameCheck();
            const next = root.querySelector('[data-action="nextAssessment"]');
            if (next && !state.assessment.focusLocked && !state.assessment.backgroundSpeech)
                next.disabled = false;
        };
        const previousAction = action;
        action = function (a) {
            if (a === 'recordVoice' && state.screen === 'assessment') {
                const q = currentQ();
                if (isVoiceQuestion(q)) {
                    startVoiceCapture();
                    return;
                }
            }
            if (a === 'nextAssessment' && state.screen === 'assessment') {
                const q = currentQ();
                if (isVoiceQuestion(q) && !state.assessment.voiceCompleted) {
                    showToast('Please record your spoken response before moving to the next question.');
                    return;
                }
                if (!isVoiceQuestion(q)) {
                    const ms = state.assessment.micStream;
                    if (ms?.getTracks)
                        ms.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
                    state.assessment.micStream = null;
                    state.assessment.mic = false;
                }
            }
            previousAction(a);
        };
        /* Camera preview uses only the camera track; microphone never feeds the video element. */
        attachVideo = function () {
            const video = document.getElementById('camVideo');
            const cs = state.assessment.cameraStream;
            if (video && cs?.getVideoTracks?.().length) {
                video.srcObject = new MediaStream(cs.getVideoTracks());
                video.muted = true;
                video.play?.().catch(() => { });
            }
        };
    })();
    addStudyStyles();
    clearLegacyAccountsOnce();
    render();
    const finalActionGuard = action;
    action = function (a) {
        if (a === 'resumeExam') {
            if (state.screen !== 'assessment')
                return;
            state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
            render();
            showToast('Assessment resumed. Stay on this tab until the exam is completed.');
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'logoutDuringAssessment' && state.screen === 'assessment' && state.assessment.mode === 'diagnostic') {
            showToast('You cannot exit the diagnostic before completing it.');
            return;
        }
        finalActionGuard(a);
    };
    window.addEventListener('blur', () => {
        if (state.screen === 'assessment') {
            state.assessment.focusLocked = true;
            p360Persist();
        }
    });
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
        if (state.screen === 'assessment' && document.hidden) {
            state.assessment.focusLocked = true;
            p360Persist();
        }
    });
    document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', () => {
        if (state.screen === 'assessment' && !document.fullscreenElement) {
            state.assessment.focusLocked = true;
            p360Persist();
            showToast('Fullscreen was exited. Resume the assessment to continue.');
        }
    });
    const finalWelcomeGateCleanup = enhanceWelcome;
    enhanceWelcome = function () {
        finalWelcomeGateCleanup();
        const n = document.getElementById('diagnosticGateNote');

            n.remove();
    };
    addStudyStyles();
    clearLegacyAccountsOnce();
    render();
    /* === USER REQUEST PATCH: Python study material + 30-question checkbox lab + remove Ask AI === */
    (function () {
        const PYTHON_PRACTICE_QUESTIONS = [
            {
                level: 'Basic (Variables, Control Flow, and Loops)', items: [
                    'Write a program to swap two variables without using a temporary third variable.',
                    'Take an integer input from the user and determine whether the number is odd or even.',
                    'Create a program that checks if a user-inputted year is a leap year.',
                    'Write a program to find the factorial of a given number using a while loop.',
                    'Use a for loop to print the multiplication table of a number given by the user.',
                    'Calculate and display the sum of all natural numbers up to a specified number N.',
                    'Take a string from the user and count the total number of vowels present in it.',
                    'Write a program that reverses a given string using slicing.',
                    'Generate and print the first N numbers of the Fibonacci sequence.',
                    'Check if a given string is a palindrome (reads the same backward as forward).'
                ]
            },
            {
                level: 'Intermediate (Data Structures, Functions, and Core Modules)', items: [
                    'Write a function that takes a list and returns a new list containing only unique elements.',
                    'Merge two dictionaries into one; if a key exists in both, combine their values into a list.',
                    'Given a list of integers, find the second largest number without using built-in sorting methods.',
                    'Implement a function to find the Greatest Common Divisor (GCD) of two numbers using Euclidean algorithm logic.',
                    'Write a list comprehension that extracts all words starting with a vowel from a given sentence.',
                    'Write a function to check if a given number is a prime number.',
                    'Create a program that reads a text file and counts the frequency of each word inside it.',
                    'Flatten a deeply nested list of integers (e.g., [1, [2, 3], [[4], 5]]) into a single flat list.',
                    'Use the datetime module to calculate the exact number of days remaining until a specific future date.',
                    'Write a program that validates a password string based on criteria (min length, uppercase, number, special character) using the re (regex) module.'
                ]
            },
            {
                level: 'Advanced (OOP, Functional Programming, and System Design)', items: [
                    'Create a BankAccount class with private attributes for balance, and safe methods for deposit and withdrawal.',
                    'Implement a multi-level inheritance structure where a subclass overrides a parent method but still invokes it using super().',
                    'Write a custom decorator named @timer that calculates and logs the execution time of any function it wraps.',
                    'Build a custom generator function that mimics the behavior of the built-in range() function but accepts float steps.',
                    'Create a custom context manager class using __enter__ and __exit__ to manage a database connection or file lock safely.',
                    'Write a script that uses the threading or multiprocessing module to download multiple mock files concurrently.',
                    'Implement a property decorator (@property, @setter) inside a class to perform type validation on an attribute during assignment.',
                    'Write a program that handles custom nested exceptions, ensuring resource cleanup happens in the finally block even when an unexpected error occurs.',
                    'Implement a basic Abstract Base Class (ABC) using the abc module to enforce structural design constraints across multiple subclasses.',
                    'Create an implementation of a Singleton design pattern in Python using the __new__ dunder method.'
                ]
            }
        ];
        const PYTHON_FLAT_QUESTIONS = PYTHON_PRACTICE_QUESTIONS.flatMap(g => g.items);
        state.pythonLabChecks = Array.isArray(state.pythonLabChecks) ? state.pythonLabChecks : Array(PYTHON_FLAT_QUESTIONS.length).fill(false);
        if (state.pythonLabChecks.length !== PYTHON_FLAT_QUESTIONS.length)
            state.pythonLabChecks = Array(PYTHON_FLAT_QUESTIONS.length).fill(false);
        function pythonLabChecklistView() {
            const done = state.pythonLabChecks.filter(Boolean).length;
            const pct = Math.round(done / PYTHON_FLAT_QUESTIONS.length * 100);
            let n = 0;
            const groups = PYTHON_PRACTICE_QUESTIONS.map(g => {
                const start = n;
                const html = g.items.map((q, i) => {
                    const idx = start + i;
                    n++;
                    return '<label class="python-practice-check" style="display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start;padding:13px 14px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;background:#fff;margin:8px 0;cursor:pointer">'
                        + '<input type="checkbox" data-python-lab-check="' + idx + '" ' + (state.pythonLabChecks[idx] ? 'checked' : '') + ' style="width:18px;height:18px;margin-top:2px;accent-color:var(--purple);flex:0 0 auto">'
                        + '<span><b style="display:block;margin-bottom:3px">Q' + (idx + 1) + '</b><span class="small">' + escape(q) + '</span></span></label>';
                }).join('');
                return '<section style="margin-top:20px"><div class="panel-head"><div><span class="eyebrow">' + escape(g.level) + '</span><h3>' + escape(g.level.split(' (')[0]) + ' Practice</h3></div><span class="tag">' + g.items.length + ' questions</span></div>' + html + '</section>';
            }).join('');
            return '<div class="panel lab-session"><div class="panel-head"><div><span class="eyebrow">PYTHON FUNDAMENTALS · PRACTICE LAB</span><h2>Python Practice Checklist</h2><p class="muted">Tick each question after you solve or practise it. Your checklist stays available when you return to the lab.</p></div><span class="tag">' + done + '/' + PYTHON_FLAT_QUESTIONS.length + ' completed</span></div>'
                + '<div class="panel" style="margin-top:16px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbf9ff,#f1f9ff)"><div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;align-items:center"><div><b>Practice progress</b><div class="small">Complete all 30 questions for a full checklist.</div></div><strong>' + pct + '%</strong></div><div class="skillbar" style="margin-top:10px"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div></div>'
                + groups
                + '<div style="display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:20px"><button class="secondary" data-action="resetPythonLab">Reset checklist</button><button class="secondary" data-view="studyMaterials">Back to study materials</button></div></div>';
        }
        const previousLabViewForPythonChecklist = labView;
        labView = function () {
            const topic = String(state.activeLab || state.activeStudyTopic || '');
            if (topic.toLowerCase() === 'python fundamentals')
                return pythonLabChecklistView();
            return previousLabViewForPythonChecklist();
        };
        /* Remove Ask AI from the top navigation while retaining the rest of the navigation. */
        topbar = function () {
            const showBack = state.screen !== 'landing';
            return '<header class="topbar">'
                + '<div class="brand"><div class="mascot">' + mascot + '<span class="spark">✦</span></div><div><div>PLACEMENT<span style="color:var(--purple)">360</span></div><small>AI placement companion</small></div></div>'
                + '<nav class="nav">'
                + (showBack ? '<button class="ghost" data-action="back">← Back</button>' : '')
                + '<button data-action="home">Home</button>'
                + '</nav></header>';
        };
        const previousBindForPythonChecklist = bind;
        bind = function () {
            previousBindForPythonChecklist();
            root.querySelectorAll('[data-python-lab-check]').forEach(box => {
                box.onchange = () => {
                    const idx = Number(box.dataset.pythonLabCheck);
                    if (Number.isInteger(idx) && idx >= 0 && idx < PYTHON_FLAT_QUESTIONS.length) {
                        state.pythonLabChecks[idx] = box.checked;
                        p360Persist();
                        render();
                    }
                };
            });
            root.querySelectorAll('[data-action="resetPythonLab"]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
                state.pythonLabChecks = Array(PYTHON_FLAT_QUESTIONS.length).fill(false);
                p360Persist();
                showToast('Python practice checklist reset.');
                render();
            });
        };
        /* Persist checklist with the existing user-progress storage. */
        const previousPersistForPythonChecklist = p360Persist;
        p360Persist = function () {
            try {
                state.pythonLabChecks = state.pythonLabChecks || Array(PYTHON_FLAT_QUESTIONS.length).fill(false);
            }
            catch (e) { }
            previousPersistForPythonChecklist();
            if (!state.userEmail)
                return;
            try {
                const key = p360UserKey(state.userEmail), u = p360LoadUser(state.userEmail) || {};
                u.pythonLabChecks = state.pythonLabChecks;
                localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify({
                    ...u, email: state.userEmail
                }));
            }
            catch (e) { }
        };
        const previousApplyUserForPythonChecklist = p360ApplyUser;
        p360ApplyUser = function (u) {
            previousApplyUserForPythonChecklist(u);
            state.pythonLabChecks = Array.isArray(u.pythonLabChecks) && u.pythonLabChecks.length === PYTHON_FLAT_QUESTIONS.length
                ? u.pythonLabChecks : Array(PYTHON_FLAT_QUESTIONS.length).fill(false);
        };
    })();
    /* ---------- Job-specific preparation route after diagnostic ---------- */
    const JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY = [
        {
            id: 'software-developer', job: 'Software Developer',
            tagline: 'Build strong software engineering fundamentals, coding ability and production habits.',
            modules: [

                    ], ans: 0

            practical: 'Solve one practical problem from your selected role direction and explain your assumptions and test cases.',
            assignment: 'Create a small placement-ready project or coding feature and document the problem, approach, result and lessons learned.'
        }
    ];
    function resolveCareerRoute() {
        const b = String(state.branch || '').trim(), r = String(state.role || '').trim();
        const rb = (pattern) => new RegExp(pattern, 'i').test(b);
        const rr = (pattern) => new RegExp(pattern, 'i').test(r);
        if (rb('Civil') && rr('Structural'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'civil-structural');
        if (rb('Civil') && rr('Site'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'civil-site');
        if (rb('Civil') && rr('Design'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'civil-design');
        if (rb('Civil') && rr('Construction|Project'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'civil-project');
        if (rb('Mechanical') && rr('Design'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'mech-design');
        if (rb('Mechanical') && rr('Manufacturing'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'manufacturing-engineer');
        if (rb('Mechanical') && rr('Automotive'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'automotive-engineer');
        if (rb('Mechanical') && rr('Production'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'production-engineer');
        if (rb('Robotics|Mechatronics') && rr('Robotics'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'robotics-engineer');
        if (rb('Robotics|Mechatronics') && rr('Automation'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'automation-engineer');
        if (rb('Robotics|Mechatronics') && rr('Embedded'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'embedded-engineer');
        if (rb('Robotics|Mechatronics') && rr('Controls'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'controls-engineer');
        if (rb('EEE|Electrical') && rr('Electrical|Power'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'electrical-power');
        if (rb('EEE|Electrical') && rr('Embedded'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'embedded-engineer');
        if (rb('EEE|Electrical') && rr('Controls'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'controls-engineer');
        if (rb('EEE|Electrical') && rr('Core'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'core-eee');
        if (rb('ECE') && rr('Embedded'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'embedded-engineer');
        if (rb('ECE') && rr('VLSI'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'vlsi-engineer');
        if (rb('ECE') && rr('Electronics|Core'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'electronics-core');
        if (rb('ECE') && rr('IoT'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'iot-engineer');
        if (rr('Python'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'python-developer');
        if (rr('Java'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'java-developer');
        if (rr('Full Stack'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'full-stack-developer');
        if (rr('Data Analyst'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'data-analyst');
        if (rr('Data Scientist'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'data-scientist');
        if (rr('GenAI'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'genai-engineer');
        if (rr('AI|ML'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'ai-ml-engineer');
        if (rr('Cloud|DevOps'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'cloud-devops');
        if (rr('Software Developer'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'software-developer');
        if (rb('CSE|Computer Science'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'software-developer');
        if (rr('Common Graduate|discover'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'general-graduate');
        if (rb('ECE'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'electronics-core');
        if (rb('EEE'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'core-eee');
        if (rb('Mechanical'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'production-engineer');
        if (rb('Civil'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'civil-project');
        if (rb('Robotics|Mechatronics'))
            return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'robotics-engineer');
        return JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'general-graduate');
    }
    function activeCareerRoute() {
        if (!state.diagnosis)
            return null;
        const route = resolveCareerRoute();
        if (route && state.studyRouteId !== route.id) {
            state.studyRouteId = route.id;
            state.studyProgress = {};
            state.labDone = {};
            state.activeStudyTopic = '';
            state.activeLab = '';
            state.labAnswer = undefined;
            state.labResult = '';
            state.companyMock = null;
            state.companyMockResult = null;
            state.studyRouteChanged = true;
        }
        return route;
    }
    const previousStudyCatalogForJob = studyCatalog;
    studyCatalog = function () {
        const route = activeCareerRoute();
        return route ? route.modules : previousStudyCatalogForJob();
    };
    const previousStudyInitForJob = studyInit;
    studyInit = function () {
        previousStudyInitForJob();
        activeCareerRoute();
    };
