    const previousMaterialContentForJob = materialContent;
    materialContent = function (topic) {
        const route = activeCareerRoute();
        const topics = route ? route.modules.flatMap(m => m[1]) : [];
        if (route && topics.includes(topic))
            return jobTopicGuide(topic, route);
        return previousMaterialContentForJob(topic);
    };
    const previousLabForTopicForJob = labForTopic;
    labForTopic = function (topic) {
        const route = activeCareerRoute();
        if (route) {
            const exact = route.labs.find(x => x.topic.toLowerCase() === String(topic || '').toLowerCase());
            if (exact)
                return {
                    ...exact
                };
        }
        return previousLabForTopicForJob(topic);
    };
    studyLabsAI = function () {
        studyInit();
        const route = activeCareerRoute();
        if (!route)
            return studyLabs();
        return '<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><span class="eyebrow">PRACTICE LABS · ' + escape(route.job.toUpperCase()) + '</span><h2>Practice for your target job</h2><p class="muted">' + escape(route.tagline) + ' Complete the applied problems below to build evidence.</p></div><span class="tag">' + route.labs.length + ' labs</span></div><div class="lab-grid" style="margin-top:18px">' + route.labs.map((x, i) => '<article class="lab-card"><span class="level-chip">LAB ' + (i + 1) + '</span><h3>' + escape(x.title) + '</h3><p class="small">' + escape(x.topic) + ' · ' + escape(route.job) + '</p><span class="lab-status ' + (state.labDone[studyKey(x.topic)] ? 'done' : '') + '">' + (state.labDone[studyKey(x.topic)] ? '✓ Completed' : 'Not started') + '</span><br><button class="primary" data-study-lab="' + escapeAttr(x.topic) + '">' + (state.labDone[studyKey(x.topic)] ? 'Retry lab' : 'Start lab →') + '</button></article>').join('') + '</div></div>';
    };
    function roleAssessmentBank(route) {
        const common = [
            {
                type: 'mcq', level: 'Aptitude', skill: 'Aptitude', q: 'If 5 notebooks cost ₹150, what is the cost of 8 notebooks at the same rate?', opts: [
                    '₹180', '₹220', '₹240', '₹300'
                ], ans: 2
            },
            {
                type: 'mcq', level: 'Aptitude', skill: 'Aptitude', q: 'A number increases from 200 to 240. What is the percentage increase?', opts: [
                    '10%', '15%', '20%', '25%'
                ], ans: 2
            }
        ];
        return [
            ...common,
            ...(route.assessmentCore || []),
            {
                type: 'voice', level: 'Communication', skill: 'Communication', q: 'Speak for about 45 seconds: explain why you want to become a ' + route.job + ' and mention one skill you are currently improving.'
            },
            {
                type: 'voice', level: 'Communication', skill: 'Communication', q: 'Speak for about 45 seconds: explain one project or practical problem that is relevant to ' + route.job + ' and what you learned from it.'
            },
            {
                type: 'practical', level: 'Practical', skill: route.job, q: route.practical, starter: '// Write your answer, algorithm, calculation or pseudocode here.\\n', tests: 'Include inputs/assumptions, a clear method and at least two checks.'
            }
        ];
    }
    const previousWeeklyQuestionBankForJob = weeklyQuestionBank;
    weeklyQuestionBank = function () {
        const route = activeCareerRoute();
        return route ? roleAssessmentBank(route) : previousWeeklyQuestionBankForJob();
    };
    function jobCompanyMockBank(route) {
        const core = (route.assessmentCore || []).map(q => ({
            ...q
        }));
        const coding = route.labs[0] ? {
            company: route.job, type: 'coding', section: 'Core practical', q: route.labs[0].prompt, starter: route.labs[0].starter || '// write solution here', tests: route.labs[0].tests || 'Create at least two tests.'
        } :
            {
                company: route.job, type: 'coding', section: 'Core practical', q: route.practical, starter: '// write solution here', tests: 'Explain assumptions and test cases.'
            };
        const communication = {
            company: route.job, type: 'text', section: 'Communication', q: 'Answer in 3–5 sentences: introduce yourself for a ' + route.job + ' interview, explain one relevant project or practical task, and state one measurable result or learning.'
        };
        return [
            {
                company: route.job, type: 'mcq', section: 'Aptitude', q: 'A machine or process produces 120 units in 4 hours. At the same rate, how many units in 7 hours?', opts: [
                    '180', '210', '240', '280'
                ], ans: 1
            },
            {
                company: route.job, type: 'mcq', section: 'Reasoning', q: 'All engineers solve problems. Some graduates are engineers. Which conclusion is valid?', opts: [
                    'Some graduates solve problems', 'All graduates solve problems', 'No engineer solves problems', 'All problem solvers are engineers'
                ], ans: 0
            },
            ...core.slice(0, 4).map(q => ({
                company: route.job, type: 'mcq', section: q.skill || 'Core', q: q.q, opts: q.opts, ans: q.ans
            })),
            coding,
            communication
        ];
    }
    companyMockView = function () {
        const route = activeCareerRoute() || JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'general-graduate');
        const m = state.companyMock || {
            q: 0, answers: {}, code: '', startedAt: Date.now(), bank: jobCompanyMockBank(route)
        };
        state.companyMock = m;
        const bank = m.bank || jobCompanyMockBank(route);
        const q = bank[m.q];
        if (!q)
            return companyMockResultView();
        const pct = Math.round((m.q / Math.max(1, bank.length)) * 100);
        let body = '';
        if (q.type === 'mcq')
            body = q.opts.map((x, i) => '<button class="answer-option ' + (m.answers[m.q] === i ? 'selected' : '') + '" data-company-answer="' + i + '">' + String.fromCharCode(65 + i) + '. ' + escape(x) + '</button>').join('');
        else if (q.type === 'coding')
            body = '<div class="editor"><div class="runbar"><span>Role-focused coding workspace</span><button class="primary" data-action="runCompanyCode">Run preview</button></div><textarea id="companyCode" spellcheck="false">' + escape(m.code || q.starter || '') + '</textarea><div id="companyCodeResult" class="small" style="margin-top:10px;color:#bde6cf">Sample checks: ' + escape(q.tests || 'Create at least two tests.') + '</div></div>';
        else
            body = '<textarea id="companyText" style="width:100%;min-height:160px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;padding:12px;resize:vertical">' + escape(m.answers[m.q] || '') + '</textarea>';
        return '<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><span class="eyebrow">ROLE-FOCUSED MOCK · ' + escape(route.job.toUpperCase()) + '</span><h2>' + escape(q.company) + ' · ' + escape(q.section) + '</h2><p class="small">' + escape(route.tagline) + '</p></div><span class="tag">Question ' + (m.q + 1) + ' / ' + bank.length + '</span></div><div class="progress-line" style="margin:14px 0"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div><section class="question-card"><span class="level-chip">' + escape(q.section) + '</span><h3 style="margin:12px 0">' + escape(q.q) + '</h3>' + body + '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;margin-top:16px"><button class="secondary" data-action="companyMockBack">Back</button><button class="primary" data-action="companyMockNext">' + (m.q === bank.length - 1 ? 'Finish mock' : 'Next →') + '</button></div></section></div>';
    };
    finishCompanyMock = function () {
        const m = state.companyMock || {}, bank = m.bank || [], scoreItems = [];
        let correct = 0, total = 0;
        bank.forEach((q, i) => {
            if (q.type === 'mcq') {
                total++;
                const ok = Number(m.answers?.[i]) === Number(q.ans);
                if (ok)
                    correct++;
                scoreItems.push({
                    section: q.section, ok
                });
            }
            else if (q.type === 'coding') {
                total++;
                const code = String(m.code || '');
                const ok = code.length > 45 && !/pass|write your solution|TODO|complete the solution/i.test(code);
                if (ok)
                    correct++;
                scoreItems.push({
                    section: q.section, ok
                });
            }
            else if (q.type === 'text') {
                total++;
                const txt = String(m.answers?.[i] || '').trim();
                const ok = txt.length >= 80;
                if (ok)
                    correct++;
                scoreItems.push({
                    section: q.section, ok
                });
            }
        });
        const score = Math.round(correct / Math.max(1, total) * 100);
        const strengths = scoreItems.filter(x => x.ok).map(x => x.section);
        const gaps = scoreItems.filter(x => !x.ok).map(x => x.section);
        const route = activeCareerRoute();
        const next = gaps.length ? 'Focus next on ' + gaps.join(', ') + '.' : 'Maintain consistency and add deeper job-specific projects.';
        state.companyMockResult = {
            score, total, band: score >= 80 ? 'Placement ready' : score >= 60 ? 'Developing' : 'Needs focused practice', strengths: [
                ...new Set(strengths)
            ], gaps: [
                ...new Set(gaps)
            ], next
        };
        state.skillEvidence = Math.min(100, Math.max(Number(state.skillEvidence || 0), score));
        state.companyMock = null;
        state.view = 'assessment';
        recordVerifiedStudy('company mock completion');
        p360Persist();
        render();
    };
    assignments = function () {
        const route = activeCareerRoute() || JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'general-graduate');
        if (!weeklyAssignmentUnlocked()) {
            const hours = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((6 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 - (Date.now() - (state.accountCreatedAt || Date.now()))) / (60 * 60 * 1000)));
            return '<div class="panel"><span class="eyebrow">WEEKLY ASSIGNMENT · ' + escape(route.job.toUpperCase()) + '</span><h2>Unlocks after 6 days of learning.</h2><p class="muted">Your assignment is generated from the skills and subjects for <b>' + escape(route.job) + '</b>.</p><div class="diagnosis-note"><b>Remaining:</b> about ' + Math.max(0, Math.floor(hours / 24)) + ' day(s) ' + (hours % 24) + ' hour(s).</div></div>';
        }
        return '<div class="panel"><span class="eyebrow">WEEKLY ASSIGNMENT · ' + escape(route.job.toUpperCase()) + '</span><h2>' + escape(route.job) + ' applied assignment</h2><p class="muted">' + escape(route.tagline) + '</p><div class="mini-card" style="margin-top:16px"><b>Current task</b><p class="small">' + escape(route.assignment) + '</p><div class="progress-line" style="margin-top:12px"><i style="width:' + (state.weeklyDone ? 100 : 35) + '%"></i></div><p class="small">Status: ' + (state.weeklyDone ? 'AI-verified and added to evidence' : 'Available') + '</p><button class="primary" data-action="completeWeekly">' + (state.weeklyDone ? 'Review evidence' : 'Submit assignment') + '</button></div></div>';
    };
    const previousTodaysPlanForJob = todaysPlan;
    todaysPlan = function () {
        const route = activeCareerRoute();
        if (!route)
            return previousTodaysPlanForJob();
        const topics = route.modules.flatMap(x => x[1]);
        const day = (new Date()).getDay();
        const concept = topics[(day + topics.length) % topics.length];
        const labs = route.labs;
        return '<div class="panel study-hero"><div><span class="eyebrow">TODAY’S PLAN · ' + escape(route.job.toUpperCase()) + '</span><h2>Today’s goals for your ' + escape(route.job) + ' preparation.</h2><p class="muted">' + escape(route.tagline) + '</p><div class="study-seq"><span>01 Learn</span><span>02 Examples</span><span>03 Practical</span><span>04 Communication</span><span>05 Review</span></div></div><div class="panel" style="margin:0"><span class="small">TODAY’S CONCEPT</span><div style="font-weight:800;font-size:20px;margin-top:8px">' + escape(concept) + '</div><button class="primary" style="margin-top:12px" data-study-open="' + escapeAttr(concept) + '">Study this topic →</button></div></div>' +
            '<div class="cards" style="margin-top:16px"><div class="mini-card"><b>1 · Today’s concept</b><p class="small">Study <b>' + escape(concept) + '</b> and work through at least 2 examples.</p></div><div class="mini-card"><b>2 · Practical</b><p class="small">Finish 2 ' + escape(route.job) + ' problems: <b>' + escape(labs[0]?.title || 'Applied problem 1') + '</b> and <b>' + escape(labs[1]?.title || 'Applied problem 2') + '</b>.</p><button class="secondary" data-view="labs">Open practice labs</button></div><div class="mini-card"><b>3 · Communication</b><p class="small">Complete at least 2 communication activities for ' + escape(route.job) + ': a 45-second self-introduction and one project/technical explanation.</p><button class="secondary" data-view="interview">Practise communication</button></div><div class="mini-card"><b>4 · Assessment</b><p class="small">Complete the next role-focused assessment when it is available.</p><button class="secondary" data-view="assessment">Open assessments</button></div></div>';
    };
    todaysPlanAI = todaysPlan;
    const previousAssessmentPageForJob = assessmentPage;
    assessmentPage = function () {
        if (!state.diagnosis)
            return previousAssessmentPageForJob();
        const route = activeCareerRoute();
        const subjectLine = route ? route.modules.map(m => m[0] + ': ' + m[1].slice(0, 3).join(', ')).join(' · ') : '';
        return '<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><span class="eyebrow">ASSESSMENT CENTER · ' + escape((route?.job || 'ROLE').toUpperCase()) + '</span><h2>Assess what your target job actually needs.</h2><p class="muted">' + escape(route?.tagline || 'Your questions are aligned to your selected role.') + '</p></div><span class="tag">Role-focused</span></div><div class="cards" style="margin-top:16px"><div class="mini-card"><b>Role core</b><p class="small">' + escape(subjectLine) + '</p></div><div class="mini-card"><b>Weekly assessment</b><p class="small">Aptitude stays common. Core theory, communication and practical work are selected for ' + escape(route?.job || 'your role') + '.</p><button class="primary" data-action="startWeeklyAssessment">Start role assessment →</button></div><div class="mini-card"><b>Company mock</b><p class="small">The mock now uses ' + escape(route?.job || 'your target role') + ' core questions and a matching practical task.</p><button class="primary" data-action="companyMock">Start role mock →</button></div></div><div class="panel" style="margin-top:16px"><span class="eyebrow">ROLE SUBJECTS</span><p class="small">' + escape(subjectLine) + '</p><p class="small">Your diagnostic sets the starting level; your selected job sets the preparation curriculum.</p></div></div>';
    };
    learningAssessments = learningAssessments;
    const previousDashboardHomeForJob = dashboardHome;
    dashboardHome = function () {
        const route = activeCareerRoute();
        if (!route)
            return previousDashboardHomeForJob();
        const topics = route.modules.flatMap(x => x[1]);
        return '<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><span class="eyebrow">YOUR JOB-FOCUSED DASHBOARD</span><h2>Preparation for ' + escape(route.job) + '.</h2><p class="muted">' + escape(route.tagline) + ' Your diagnostic result remains the starting level; this target role controls what you learn next.</p></div><button class="primary" data-view="today">Open today’s plan</button></div><div class="dash-grid" style="margin-top:18px"><div class="panel" style="margin:0;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#faf8ff,#f1f9ff)"><div class="metric-big">' + (state.diagnosis?.level || 'Developing') + '</div><div class="muted">Diagnostic level</div><p class="small" style="margin-top:12px">Target: <b>' + escape(route.job) + '</b></p><div class="skillbar" style="margin-top:10px"><i style="width:' + Number(state.skillEvidence || 35) + '%"></i></div><p class="small">' + Number(state.skillEvidence || 35) + '% evidence strength</p></div><div class="panel" style="margin:0"><b>Today at a glance</b><p class="small">' + escape(topics[0] || 'Core topic') + ' · 2 practical problems · 2 communication activities</p><button class="secondary" data-view="today">View today’s plan</button></div></div><div class="cards"><div class="mini-card"><b>Job subjects</b><p class="small">' + escape(route.modules.flatMap(x => x[1]).slice(0, 6).join(' · ')) + '</p><button class="secondary" data-view="studyMaterials">Study materials</button></div><div class="mini-card"><b>Role practice</b><p class="small">' + escape(route.labs.slice(0, 2).map(x => x.title).join(' · ')) + '</p><button class="secondary" data-view="labs">Practice problems</button></div></div></div>';
    };
    /* Weekly assessments now use the job-specific bank generated above. */
    weeklyQuestionBank = weeklyQuestionBank;
    /* Intercept company-mock actions so the dynamic bank is used. */
    const previousActionForJobRoute = action;
    action = function (a) {
        if (a === 'companyMock') {
            const route = activeCareerRoute() || JOB_ROUTE_LIBRARY.find(x => x.id === 'general-graduate');
            state.companyMock = {
                q: 0, answers: {}, code: '', startedAt: Date.now(), bank: jobCompanyMockBank(route)
            };
            state.view = 'companyMock';
            state.screen = 'app';
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'companyMockBack') {
            if (state.companyMock?.q > 0) {
                state.companyMock.q--;
                render();
            }
            else {
                state.companyMock = null;
                state.view = 'assessment';
                render();
            }
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'companyMockNext') {
            const m = state.companyMock;
            if (!m)
                return;
            const bank = m.bank || [];
            const q = bank[m.q];
            if (!q)
                return;
            if (q.type === 'mcq') {
                if (m.answers?.[m.q] === undefined) {
                    showToast('Select an answer first.');
                    return;
                }
            }
            else if (q.type === 'coding') {
                m.code = document.getElementById('companyCode')?.value || m.code;
                if (!m.code || m.code.length < 40) {
                    showToast('Complete the practical solution first.');
                    return;
                }
            }
            else if (q.type === 'text') {
                m.answers[m.q] = document.getElementById('companyText')?.value || '';
                if (String(m.answers[m.q]).trim().length < 40) {
                    showToast('Give a complete interview-style answer.');
                    return;
                }
            }
            if (m.q >= bank.length - 1) {
                finishCompanyMock();
                return;
            }
            m.q++;
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'runCompanyCode') {
            const code = document.getElementById('companyCode')?.value || '';
            state.companyMock.code = code;
            const el = document.getElementById('companyCodeResult');
            if (el)
                el.textContent = (code.length > 60 && !/pass|write your solution|TODO|complete the solution/i.test(code)) ? 'Preview check passed.' : 'The solution still looks incomplete.';
            return;
        }
        return previousActionForJobRoute(a);
    };
    /* Keep the visible workspace header explicitly role-focused once the diagnostic is complete. */
    const previousDashboardForJob = dashboard;
    dashboard = function () {
        const route = activeCareerRoute();
        if (!route)
            return previousDashboardForJob();
        return previousDashboardForJob().replace('AI learning companion active', 'AI learning companion · ' + escape(route.job));
    };
})();
</script>

<script>
// ========================================================================
// PLACEMENT360 MODULE: SCRIPT MODULE 1
// ========================================================================

/* V29 NEXT-BUTTON / FOCUS-LOCK FIX
   - A browser permission dialog or speech-recognition prompt can briefly blur the window.
     That transient blur must not permanently lock the assessment.
   - Real tab/window visibility changes and exiting fullscreen still lock the session.
   - Microphone UI is explicitly inactive for written questions.
*/
(function () {
    state.assessment.transientFocusUntil = state.assessment.transientFocusUntil || 0;
    state.assessment.focusLockReason = state.assessment.focusLockReason || '';
    /* Give permission UI / speech-recognition startup a short transient-focus window. */
    const prevFinalAction = action;
    action = function (a) {
        if (state.screen === 'assessment' && a === 'recordVoice') {
            state.assessment.transientFocusUntil = Date.now() + 12000;
        }
        /* A stale lock produced by a transient permission/focus event should never
           trap the user on a question while the page is visibly active. */
        if (a === 'nextAssessment' && state.screen === 'assessment' && state.assessment.focusLocked) {
            const transient = Date.now() < (state.assessment.transientFocusUntil || 0);
            if (transient && document.visibilityState === 'visible' && document.hasFocus()) {
                state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
                state.assessment.focusLockReason = '';
            }
        }
        prevFinalAction(a);
    };
    /* The older blur listener is intentionally tolerated, but transient blur is
       immediately cleared when the document remains visible. */
    window.addEventListener('blur', () => {
        if (state.screen !== 'assessment')
            return;
        if (Date.now() < (state.assessment.transientFocusUntil || 0)) {
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (state.screen === 'assessment' && document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
                    state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
                    state.assessment.focusLockReason = '';
                }
            }, 80);
        }
    });
    /* When a genuine focus/visibility loss happens, make the pause UI appear on
       return so the student is not left with an invisible locked state. */
    window.addEventListener('focus', () => {
        if (state.screen === 'assessment' && state.assessment.focusLocked && Date.now() >= (state.assessment.transientFocusUntil || 0)) {
            render();
        }
    });
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
        if (state.screen === 'assessment' && document.hidden) {
            state.assessment.focusLockReason = 'visibility';
        }
    });
    document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', () => {
        if (state.screen === 'assessment' && !document.fullscreenElement) {
            state.assessment.focusLockReason = 'fullscreen';
        }
    });
    /* Keep the microphone policy explicit: written questions are silent. */
    const oldAssessmentSession = assessmentSession;
    assessmentSession = function () {
        let html = oldAssessmentSession();
        if (state.screen === 'assessment') {
            const q = currentQuestions()[state.assessment.q] || {};
            const spoken = (q.type === 'voice' || /communication|soft skills/i.test(q.skill || ''));
            html = html.replace(/<div id="audioIntegrityStatus" class="small" style="margin-top:5px">[\s\S]*?<\/div>/, '<div id="audioIntegrityStatus" class="small" style="margin-top:5px">' +
                (spoken ? 'Microphone available for this spoken response only.' : 'Microphone inactive for this written question.') +
                '</div>');
        }
        return html;
    };
    /* Written questions must not be blocked by the audio monitor. */
    const oldCheckCameraFrame = checkCameraFrame;
    checkCameraFrame = async function () {
        await oldCheckCameraFrame();
        if (state.screen === 'assessment') {
            const q = currentQuestions()[state.assessment.q] || {};
            const spoken = (q.type === 'voice' || /communication|soft skills/i.test(q.skill || ''));
            if (!spoken) {
                state.assessment.backgroundSpeech = false;
                const el = document.getElementById('audioIntegrityStatus');
                if (el) {
                    el.textContent = 'Microphone inactive for this written question.';
                    el.style.color = 'var(--muted)';
                }
            }
            const next = root.querySelector('[data-action="nextAssessment"]');
            if (next && !state.assessment.focusLocked && !state.assessment.backgroundSpeech)
                next.disabled = false;
        }
    };
})();
</script>

<script>
// ========================================================================
// PLACEMENT360 MODULE: SCRIPT MODULE 2
// ========================================================================

/* V31 EXAM LOCK / VOICE / CAMERA FIX
   - Diagnostic browser-back trap is strengthened.
   - Recording/voice-permission interaction must NEVER lock the exam.
   - Microphone is treated as an intentional part of spoken questions.
   - Face + shoulders are sufficient camera framing; no chest requirement.
*/
(function () {
    const isDiagnostic = () => state.screen === 'assessment' && state.assessment?.mode === 'diagnostic' && !state.diagnosis;
    const isSpoken = () => {
        const q = (typeof currentQuestions === 'function' ? currentQuestions()[state.assessment.q] : null) || {};
        return q.type === 'voice' || /communication|soft skills/i.test(q.skill || '');
    };
    const voiceGrace = () => Date.now() < Number(state.assessment?.transientFocusUntil || 0);
    const voiceActive = () => !!state.assessment?.recording || voiceGrace();
    /* Never retain an exam lock caused by the browser's mic/speech permission UI. */
    function clearVoiceLock() {
        if (state.screen !== 'assessment')
            return;
        if (voiceActive()) {
            state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
            state.assessment.focusLockReason = '';
            state.assessment.backgroundSpeech = false;
        }
    }
    window.addEventListener('blur', () => { if (voiceActive())
        setTimeout(clearVoiceLock, 0); }, true);
    window.addEventListener('focus', clearVoiceLock, true);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { if (voiceActive())
        setTimeout(clearVoiceLock, 0); }, true);
    /* While a spoken response is being recorded, it is valid exam activity.\n     Do not treat detected microphone sound as a cheating violation. */
    const oldRecordAction = action;
    action = function (a) {
        if (a === 'recordVoice' && state.screen === 'assessment') {
            state.assessment.transientFocusUntil = Date.now() + 15000;
            state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
            state.assessment.focusLockReason = '';
        }
        if (a === 'nextAssessment' && state.screen === 'assessment' && isSpoken()) {
            state.assessment.backgroundSpeech = false;
            state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
            state.assessment.focusLockReason = '';
        }
        oldRecordAction(a);
    };
    /* Keep audio integrity from disabling Next during the spoken response. */
    setInterval(() => {
        if (state.screen !== 'assessment')
            return;
        if (isSpoken() && (state.assessment.recording || state.assessment.voiceCompleted)) {
            state.assessment.backgroundSpeech = false;
            const el = document.getElementById('audioIntegrityStatus');
            if (el) {
                el.textContent = state.assessment.recording ? 'Microphone active — this spoken response is part of the exam.' : 'Microphone released. Spoken response captured.';
                el.style.color = 'var(--muted)';
            }
            clearVoiceLock();
        }
    }, 120);
    /* Ensure the visible guidance says exactly what is required. */
    const oldAssessmentSession = assessmentSession;
    assessmentSession = function () {
        let html = oldAssessmentSession();
        html = html.replace(/Head \+ shoulders\/chest must stay inside the frame/g, 'Face + shoulders must stay inside the frame');
        html = html.replace(/Head \+ shoulders\/chest/g, 'Face + shoulders');
        html = html.replace(/upper chest/g, 'shoulders');
        return html;
    };
})();
</script>

<script>
/* V32: FULLSCREEN-ONLY EXAM BEHAVIOUR
   - The exam is locked to the browser's fullscreen screen, not a page-lock overlay.
   - Camera framing problems only show warnings; they never lock/disable the exam.
   - Tab/window switches only show warnings; they never lock/disable the exam.
   - Microphone is requested only for the active Communication/Soft Skills spoken question.
   - Spoken-question recording is treated as normal exam activity.
*/
(function () {
    const inExam = () => state.screen === 'assessment';
    const spoken = () => {
        const q = (typeof currentQuestions === 'function' ? currentQuestions()[state.assessment.q] : null) || {};
        return q.type === 'voice' || /communication|soft skills/i.test(q.skill || '');
    };
    let lastAlertMessage = '';
    let lastAlertAt = 0;
    function examWarning(message) {
        state.assessment.lastExamWarning = message;
        const now = Date.now();
        /* Native browser-style alert, throttled so one focus event cannot spam alerts. */
        if (message && (message !== lastAlertMessage || now - lastAlertAt > 2500)) {
            lastAlertMessage = message;
            lastAlertAt = now;
            try {
                window.alert('⚠ Exam Warning\n\n' + message);
            }
            catch (e) { }
        }
        const el = document.getElementById('examWarningBanner');
        if (el) {
            el.textContent = '⚠ ' + message;
            el.style.display = 'block';
            clearTimeout(window.__p360WarnTimer);
            window.__p360WarnTimer = setTimeout(() => { if (el)
                el.style.display = 'none'; }, 5000);
        }
    }
    /* Never render a blocking focus-lock overlay. */
    const baseAssessmentSession = assessmentSession;
    assessmentSession = function () {
        let html = baseAssessmentSession();
        html = html.replace(/<div class="assessment-focus-lock">[\s\S]*?<\/div><\/div>/, '');
        html = html.replace(/Do not open another tab or window\. A visibility change will pause the session\./g, 'Stay in fullscreen. Leaving the exam tab/window will show a warning.');
        html = html.replace(/Opening another tab\/window pauses the session\./g, 'Leaving the exam tab/window shows a warning only.');
        html = html.replace('</div><div class="proctor">', '</div><div id="examWarningBanner" class="guard" style="display:none;margin:10px 0;color:#9a3412;background:#fff7ed;border-color:#fdba74"></div><div class="proctor">');
        html = html.replace(/Face \+ shoulders must stay inside the frame/g, 'Face + shoulders should stay inside the frame');
        html = html.replace(/Head \+ shoulders\/chest must stay inside the frame/g, 'Face + shoulders should stay inside the frame');
        html = html.replace(/upper chest/g, 'shoulders');
        return html;
    };
    /* The camera check is advisory. It updates the warning/status but never disables Next. */
    const baseCheckCamera = checkCameraFrame;
    checkCameraFrame = async function () {
        try {
            await baseCheckCamera();
        }
        catch (e) { }
        if (!inExam())
            return;
        const status = document.getElementById('cameraFrameStatus');
        const ok = state.assessment.faceFrameOk !== false;
        if (status) {
            status.textContent = ok ? 'Framing OK — keep your face and both shoulders visible.' : 'Warning: keep your face and both shoulders visible in the camera.';
            status.style.color = ok ? 'var(--good)' : 'var(--danger)';
        }
        if (!ok && !state.assessment.__cameraWarned) {
            state.assessment.__cameraWarned = true;
            examWarning('Camera warning: keep your face and both shoulders clearly visible.');
        }
        else if (ok) {
            state.assessment.__cameraWarned = false;
        }
        const next = root.querySelector('[data-action="nextAssessment"]');
        if (next) {
            next.disabled = spoken() && !state.assessment.voiceCompleted;
        }
    };
    /* Next button: never block because of focus, tab switching, fullscreen exit, or camera framing. */
    const baseAction = action;
    action = function (a) {
        if (inExam() && a === 'nextAssessment') {
            state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
            state.assessment.focusLockReason = '';
            state.assessment.backgroundSpeech = false;
            state.assessment.faceFrameOk = true;
        }
        if (inExam() && a === 'recordVoice') {
            state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
            state.assessment.focusLockReason = '';
            state.assessment.backgroundSpeech = false;
        }
        baseAction(a);
    };
    /* Remove focus/camera/background-speech from answer/button locking. */
    const baseBind = bind;
    bind = function () {
        baseBind();
        if (!inExam())
            return;
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-answer],[data-practical-choice]').forEach(b => { b.disabled = false; });
        const next = root.querySelector('[data-action="nextAssessment"]');
        if (next)
            next.disabled = spoken() && !state.assessment.voiceCompleted;
        const voice = root.querySelector('[data-action="recordVoice"]');
        if (voice)
            voice.disabled = false;
    };
    /* Tab/window changes: warn only. No focus lock and no page overlay. */
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
        if (!inExam())
            return;
        if (document.hidden) {
            state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
            state.assessment.focusLockReason = '';
            p360Persist();
            return;
        }
        examWarning('You switched away from the exam. Return to the exam tab and keep it active.');
    }, true);
    window.addEventListener('blur', () => {
        if (!inExam())
            return;
        if (state.assessment.recording || Date.now() < (state.assessment.transientFocusUntil || 0)) {
            state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
            return;
        }
        examWarning('Exam focus lost. Please stay on the exam screen.');
    }, true);
    window.addEventListener('focus', () => {
        if (!inExam())
            return;
        state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
        if (state.assessment.lastExamWarning) {
            examWarning('Exam focus restored. Please remain on this exam screen.');
        }
    }, true);
    /* Fullscreen is the actual screen lock. If the student exits it, warn and try to restore it. */
    document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', async () => {
        if (!inExam())
            return;
        if (!document.fullscreenElement) {
            examWarning('Fullscreen was exited. The exam screen should remain in fullscreen.');
            state.assessment.fullscreen = false;
            state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
            try {
                await document.documentElement.requestFullscreen?.();
                state.assessment.fullscreen = !!document.fullscreenElement;
            }
            catch (e) { }
        }
    }, true);
    /* Written questions never need microphone permission. Spoken questions request it only here. */
    const basePrepare = prepareAssessment;
    prepareAssessment = async function () {
        state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
        state.assessment.focusLockReason = '';
        await basePrepare();
        if (inExam()) {
            state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
            state.assessment.backgroundSpeech = false;
            try {
                await document.documentElement.requestFullscreen?.();
            }
            catch (e) { }
            render();
            enhanceUI();
            attachVideo();
        }
    };
    /* Make the warning text truthful on every render. */
    const baseRender = render;
    render = function () {
        baseRender();
        if (inExam()) {
            const b = document.getElementById('examWarningBanner');
            if (b && state.assessment.lastExamWarning) {
                b.textContent = '⚠ ' + state.assessment.lastExamWarning;
            }
            const next = root.querySelector('[data-action="nextAssessment"]');
            if (next)
                next.disabled = spoken() && !state.assessment.voiceCompleted;
        }
    };
})();
</script>

<script>
// ========================================================================
// PLACEMENT360 MODULE: EXAM NEXT-BUTTON / FOCUS-LOCK FIX
// ========================================================================

/* V34: SCREEN-LOCK-ONLY EXAM MODE
   - Remove all page-lock / "Assessment is locked" behavior.
   - focusLocked is never allowed to block the exam UI or navigation.
   - Tab/window/focus loss = warning only.
   - Camera framing = warning only.
   - Microphone recording = normal exam activity.
   - Fullscreen is the only screen-level lock mechanism.
*/
(function () {
    const inExam = () => state.screen === 'assessment';
    const spoken = () => {
        const q = (typeof currentQuestions === 'function' ? currentQuestions()[state.assessment.q] : null) || {};
        return q.type === 'voice' || /communication|soft skills/i.test(q.skill || '');
    };
    const clearPageLock = () => {
        if (!state.assessment)
            return;
        state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
        state.assessment.focusLockReason = '';
    };
    /* Never allow an old listener/guard to leave the page in a focus-locked state. */
    if (state.assessment)
        clearPageLock();
    setInterval(() => { if (inExam())
        clearPageLock(); }, 120);
    /* Prevent the old assessment overlay from ever being rendered. */
    const priorAssessmentSession = assessmentSession;
    assessmentSession = function () {
        clearPageLock();
        return priorAssessmentSession();
    };
    /* Remove any legacy overlay that may have been produced by an older wrapper. */
    const priorRender = render;
    render = function () {
        clearPageLock();
        priorRender();
        if (!inExam())
            return;
        clearPageLock();
        const overlay = root.querySelector('.assessment-focus-lock');
        if (overlay)
            overlay.remove();
    };
    /* Never block an action because of focusLocked. */
    const priorAction = action;
    action = function (a) {
        if (inExam())
            clearPageLock();
        if (a === 'resumeExam' && inExam()) {
            clearPageLock();
            showToast('Exam screen lock uses fullscreen. Focus/tab changes only show warnings.');
            return;
        }
        priorAction(a);
        if (inExam())
            clearPageLock();
    };
    /* Final button-state cleanup: focus loss must never disable Next/answers. */
    const priorBind = bind;
    bind = function () {
        clearPageLock();
        priorBind();
        if (!inExam())
            return;
        clearPageLock();
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-answer],[data-practical-choice]').forEach(b => { b.disabled = false; });
        const next = root.querySelector('[data-action="nextAssessment"]');
        if (next)
            next.disabled = spoken() && !state.assessment.voiceCompleted;
    };
    /* Replace old warning/lock listeners with warning-only behavior. */
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
        if (!inExam())
            return;
        clearPageLock();
        if (document.hidden) {
            examWarning('You left the exam tab. Return to the exam and keep it active.');
        }
        else {
            examWarning('Exam focus restored. Continue on the exam screen.');
        }
    }, true);
    window.addEventListener('blur', () => {
        if (!inExam())
            return;
        const recording = !!state.assessment.recording || spoken();
        clearPageLock();
        if (!recording) {
            examWarning('Exam focus lost. Stay on the exam screen.');
        }
    }, true);
    window.addEventListener('focus', () => { if (inExam())
        clearPageLock(); }, true);
    /* Fullscreen is the only actual screen lock. No page-lock state is created. */
    document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', async () => {
        if (!inExam())
            return;
        clearPageLock();
        if (!document.fullscreenElement) {
            examWarning('Fullscreen was exited. Returning to exam fullscreen.');
            try {
                await document.documentElement.requestFullscreen?.();
                state.assessment.fullscreen = !!document.fullscreenElement;
            }
            catch (e) {
                state.assessment.fullscreen = false;
            }
        }
    }, true);
    /* Make sure any stale lock state from storage cannot reappear after a reload. */
    if (inExam())
        clearPageLock();
})();
</script>

<style id="p360-v35-exam-css">
html.p360-exam-mode,body.p360-exam-mode

{

    max-height: 100vh !important;
    overflow: hidden !important;
}

{
    position: sticky;
    top: 0;
    z-index: 999;
}

{
    pointer-events: none;
}

{
    height: calc(100vh - 70px) !important;
    min-height: 0 !important;
    max-width: 1180px !important;
    overflow: auto !important;
    overscroll-behavior: contain !important;
    padding-bottom: 28px !important;
    scrollbar-width: thin;
}

    max-width: 980px !important;
    padding-bottom: 28px;

    opacity: .45 !important;

{
    display: none !important;

<script>
// ========================================================================
// PLACEMENT360 MODULE: EXAM LOCK / VOICE / CAMERA HANDLING
// ========================================================================

/* V35 FINAL USER FLOW: MIC PERMISSION AT EXAM START + RECORD-ONLY AUDIO + SCREEN-LOCKED TEST */
(function () {
    const exam = () => state.screen === 'assessment';
    const questions = () => typeof currentQuestions === 'function' ? currentQuestions() : diagQs;
    const current = () => questions()[state.assessment.q] || {};
    const spokenQ = q => !!q && (q.type === 'voice' || /communication|soft skills/i.test(q.skill || ''));
    function micTrack() {
        const s = state.assessment.micStream || state.assessment.cameraStream || state.assessment.stream;
        return s?.getAudioTracks?.()[0] || state.assessment.micTrack || null;
    }
    function setMicEnabled(on) {
        const t = micTrack();
        if (t) {
            t.enabled = !!on;
            state.assessment.micTrack = t;
        }
        state.assessment.mic = !!on;
        return !!t;
    }
    function updateExamUI() {
        if (!exam())
            return;
        const q = current();
        const isSpoken = spokenQ(q);
        const t = micTrack();
        const active = !!state.assessment.recording && !!isSpoken;
        if (t)
            t.enabled = active;
        state.assessment.mic = active;
        state.assessment.micAvailable = !!t;
        const checks = root.querySelectorAll('.proctor .check');
        if (checks.length >= 3) {
            const camOn = !!state.assessment.cameraStream?.getVideoTracks?.().some(x => x.readyState === 'live') || !!state.assessment.camera;
            const screenOn = !!state.assessment.displayStream?.getVideoTracks?.().some(x => x.readyState === 'live') || !!state.assessment.screen;
            checks[0].className = 'check ' + (camOn ? 'ok' : 'warn');
            checks[0].textContent = '📷 Camera: ' + (camOn ? 'ON' : 'OFF');
            checks[1].className = 'check ' + (active ? 'ok' : (state.assessment.micAvailable ? 'ok' : ''));
            checks[1].textContent = '🎙 Microphone: ' + (active ? 'ON — RECORDING' : (state.assessment.micAvailable ? 'READY — OFF' : 'OFF — unavailable'));
            checks[2].className = 'check ' + (screenOn ? 'ok' : 'warn');
            checks[2].textContent = '🖥 Screen share: ' + (screenOn ? 'ON' : 'OFF');
        }
        const status = root.querySelector('#audioIntegrityStatus');
        if (status) {
            status.textContent = active ? 'Microphone ON — recording your spoken response.' : (state.assessment.micAvailable ? 'Microphone access is ready. It is OFF until you click Start recording.' : 'Microphone access is unavailable.');
            status.style.color = active ? 'var(--good)' : 'var(--muted)';
        }
        const btn = root.querySelector('[data-action="recordVoice"]');
        if (btn) {
            btn.textContent = active ? '■ Stop recording' : '🎙 Start recording';
            btn.classList.toggle('primary', active);
            btn.classList.toggle('secondary', !active);
        }
        const next = root.querySelector('[data-action="nextAssessment"]');
        if (next)
            next.disabled = isSpoken && !state.assessment.voiceCompleted;
        const nav = root.querySelector('.topbar .nav');
        if (nav)
            nav.innerHTML = '<span class="tag">EXAM IN PROGRESS</span>';
        const ai = root.querySelector('.ai-float');
        if (ai)
            ai.remove();
    }
    async function stopVoiceRecording(markComplete = true) {
        const a = state.assessment;
        if (a.voiceTimer) {
            clearTimeout(a.voiceTimer);
            a.voiceTimer = null;
        }
        try {
            a.voiceRecorder?.stop();
        }
        catch (e) { }
        a.voiceRecorder = null;
        setMicEnabled(false);
        a.recording = false;
        if (markComplete) {
            a.voiceCompleted = true;
            a.voiceScore = 2;
            a.voiceLastCapturedAt = Date.now();
        }
        updateExamUI();
    }
    async function startVoiceRecording() {
        const a = state.assessment;
        const q = current();
        if (!spokenQ(q))
            return;
        if (a.recording) {
            await stopVoiceRecording(true);
            return;
        }
        const track = micTrack();
        if (!track) {
            showToast('Microphone is not available. Return to the assessment permission step and allow microphone access.');
            return;
        }
        try {
            const audioOnly = new MediaStream([
                track
            ]);
            const chunks = [];
            let recorder = null;
            if (window.MediaRecorder) {
                recorder = new MediaRecorder(audioOnly);
                recorder.ondataavailable = e => { if (e.data?.size)
                    chunks.push(e.data); };
                recorder.onstop = () => { a.voiceBytes = chunks.reduce((n, c) => n + c.size, 0); a.voiceMimeType = recorder?.mimeType || 'audio/webm'; };
            }
            track.enabled = true;
            a.micTrack = track;
            a.mic = true;
            a.recording = true;
            a.voiceCompleted = false;
            a.voiceRecorder = recorder;
            if (recorder)
                recorder.start();
            a.voiceTimer = setTimeout(() => stopVoiceRecording(true), 45000);
            updateExamUI();
        }
        catch (e) {
            setMicEnabled(false);
            a.recording = false;
            showToast('Could not start microphone recording. Please allow microphone access and try again.');
        }
    }
    async function prepareExamFinal() {
        /* Permission + capture are requested together at the start of the exam. */
        try {
            stopStreams();
        }
        catch (e) { }
        state.assessment = {
            ...state.assessment, consent: true, q: state.assessment.q || 0, voiceCompleted: false, recording: false, voiceRecorder: null, voiceTimer: null, mic: false, micAvailable: false, focusLocked: false, focusLockReason: '', backgroundSpeech: false
        };
        let camMic;
        try {
            if (!window.isSecureContext || !navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia)
                throw new Error('media');
            camMic = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
                video: true, audio: true
            });
        }
        catch (e) {
            showToast('Camera and microphone permission is required to start the test.');
            return;
        }
        let display;
        try {
            if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getDisplayMedia)
                throw new Error('screen');
            display = await navigator.mediaDevices.getDisplayMedia({
                video: true, audio: false
            });
        }
        catch (e) {
            camMic.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
            showToast('Screen sharing is required to start the test.');
            return;
        }
        state.assessment.stream = camMic;
        state.assessment.cameraStream = camMic;
        state.assessment.micTrack = camMic.getAudioTracks?.()[0] || null;
        state.assessment.micAvailable = !!state.assessment.micTrack;
        if (state.assessment.micTrack)
            state.assessment.micTrack.enabled = false;
        state.assessment.displayStream = display;
        state.assessment.camera = !!camMic.getVideoTracks?.().length;
        state.assessment.mic = false;
        state.assessment.micAvailable = !!camMic.getAudioTracks?.().length;
        state.assessment.screen = !!display.getVideoTracks?.().length;
        state.assessment.faceFrameOk = true;
        state.assessment.backgroundSpeech = false;
        state.assessment.fullscreen = false;
        const screenTrack = display.getVideoTracks?.()[0];
        if (screenTrack)
            screenTrack.addEventListener('ended', () => {
                if (!exam())
                    return;
                state.assessment.screen = false;
                showToast('Screen sharing stopped. Please restart the assessment and re-enable screen sharing.');
                updateExamUI();
            });
        try {
            await document.documentElement.requestFullscreen?.();
        }
        catch (e) { }
        state.assessment.fullscreen = !!document.fullscreenElement;
        state.screen = 'assessment';
        document.body.classList.add('p360-exam-mode');
        render();
        attachVideo();
        updateExamUI();
        try {
            history.pushState({
                p360Exam: true
            }, '', location.href);
        }
        catch (e) { }
    }
    function renderExamFinal() {
        /* Let the original renderer draw the current question, then enforce the exam shell. */
        originalRender();
        if (!exam()) {
            document.body.classList.remove('p360-exam-mode');
            return;
        }
        document.body.classList.add('p360-exam-mode');
        const overlay = root.querySelector('.assessment-focus-lock');
        if (overlay)
            overlay.remove();
        state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
        state.assessment.focusLockReason = '';
        const nav = root.querySelector('.topbar .nav');
        if (nav)
            nav.innerHTML = '<span class="tag">EXAM IN PROGRESS</span>';
        const ai = root.querySelector('.ai-float');
        if (ai)
            ai.remove();
        attachVideo();
        setMicEnabled(false);
        state.assessment.recording = false;
        updateExamUI();
    }
    function nextExamFinal() {
        const a = state.assessment;
        const qs = questions();
        const q = qs[a.q] || {};
        if (spokenQ(q) && !a.voiceCompleted) {
            showToast('Click Start recording, answer the communication/soft-skills question, then click Stop recording.');
            return;
        }
        if (a.recording)
            stopVoiceRecording(true);
        setMicEnabled(false);
        a.recording = false;
        a.backgroundSpeech = false;
        if (a.q >= qs.length - 1) {
            try {
                stopVoiceRecording(false);
            }
            catch (e) { }
            try {
                stopStreams();
            }
            catch (e) { }
            document.body.classList.remove('p360-exam-mode');
            if (a.mode === 'weekly' && typeof finishWeeklyAssessment === 'function')
                finishWeeklyAssessment();
            else if (typeof finishAssessment === 'function')
                finishAssessment();
            return;
        }
        a.q++;
        a.voiceCompleted = false;
        a.voiceRecorder = null;
        a.voiceTimer = null;
        renderExamFinal();
    }
    /* Replace the start/voice/next functions at the final layer. */
    const originalPrepare = prepareAssessment;
    prepareAssessment = prepareExamFinal;
    const originalRender = render;
    render = renderExamFinal;
    /* Intercept navigation before any older exam guards run. */
    const previousAction = action;
    action = function (a) {
        if (a === 'prepareAssessment') {
            prepareExamFinal();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'recordVoice') {
            startVoiceRecording();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'nextAssessment') {
            nextExamFinal();
            return;
        }
        previousAction(a);
    };
    /* Keep the exam screen in place and prevent the internal app from navigating away. */
    document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
        if (!exam())
            return;
        if ((e.altKey && e.key === 'ArrowLeft') || (e.altKey && e.key === 'ArrowRight') || ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'l')) {
            e.preventDefault();
        }
    }, true);
    window.addEventListener('popstate', () => {
        if (!exam())
            return;
        try {
            history.pushState({
                p360Exam: true
            }, '', location.href);
        }
        catch (e) { }
        showToast('The test is in progress. Complete the current assessment before leaving this screen.');
    }, true);
    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', e => {
        if (!exam())
            return;
        e.preventDefault();
        e.returnValue = 'The assessment is still in progress.';
    });
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { if (exam()) {
        state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
        state.assessment.focusLockReason = '';
    } }, true);
    window.addEventListener('blur', () => { if (exam()) {
        state.assessment.focusLocked = false;
        state.assessment.focusLockReason = '';
    } }, true);
    document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', async () => {
        if (!exam())
            return;
        if (!document.fullscreenElement) {
            state.assessment.fullscreen = false;
            try {
                await document.documentElement.requestFullscreen?.();
                state.assessment.fullscreen = !!document.fullscreenElement;
            }
            catch (e) { }
        }
    }, true);
    /* Keep audio strictly silent between spoken responses, while camera + screen remain live. */
    setInterval(() => {
        if (!exam())
            return;
        const q = current();
        const allow = spokenQ(q) && state.assessment.recording;
        setMicEnabled(!!allow);
        if (!allow)
            state.assessment.backgroundSpeech = false;
        updateExamUI();
    }, 250);
    /* Ensure a fresh non-spoken question always enters with the microphone OFF. */
    const originalStart = window.startVoiceCapture;
    window.addEventListener('load', () => { if (exam())
        updateExamUI(); });
})();
</script>
