<script>
// ========================================================================
// PLACEMENT360 MODULE: FULLSCREEN-ONLY EXAM BEHAVIOUR
// ========================================================================

/* V35 ADAPTIVE CODING CHALLENGE
   Coding questions are selected from branch + target job + selected company(ies).
   The student chooses a language when the coding question appears.
   The in-browser Run button performs a safe completeness/structure preview; a real
   Python/Java/C/C++ compiler should be connected to a sandboxed backend in production.
*/
(function () {
    const priorPracticalBlock = practicalBlock;
    const priorAssessmentSession = assessmentSession;
    const priorAction = action;
    const priorBind = bind;
    const priorRender = render;
    const norm = s => String(s || '').toLowerCase();
    const roleText = () => String(state.role || state.jobGoals?.join(', ') || 'Software Developer');
    const branchText = () => String(state.branch || 'CSE');
    const companyList = () => Array.isArray(state.companies) && state.companies.length ? state.companies.map(String) : (state.company && state.company !== 'Not sure yet' ? [
        String(state.company)
    ] : []);
    const companyFocus = {
        'TCS': [
            'arrays / strings', 'SQL / enterprise logic'
        ],
        'Infosys': [
            'problem solving', 'Java / Python fundamentals'
        ],
        'Accenture': [
            'logic + data handling', 'Python / SQL fundamentals'
        ],
        'IBM': [
            'data structures', 'enterprise application thinking'
        ],
        'Deloitte': [
            'data handling', 'SQL / Python reasoning'
        ],
        'Amazon': [
            'algorithmic efficiency', 'arrays / hash maps'
        ],
        'Microsoft': [
            'data structures', 'algorithmic reasoning'
        ],
        'Wipro': [
            'problem solving', 'programming fundamentals'
        ],
        'Cognizant': [
            'Java / Python fundamentals', 'SQL / data handling'
        ],
        'Capgemini': [
            'problem solving', 'OOP / SQL fundamentals'
        ],
        'HCLTech': [
            'programming fundamentals', 'OOP / problem solving'
        ],
        'Tech Mahindra': [
            'logic + data handling', 'programming fundamentals'
        ]
    };
    function targetCompanyFocus() {
        const companies = companyList();
        if (!companies.length)
            return {
                label: 'General placement', topics: [
                    'problem solving', 'programming fundamentals'
                ]
            };
        const found = companies.find(c => companyFocus[c]);
        const topics = companyFocus[found] || [
            'problem solving', 'programming fundamentals'
        ];
        return {
            label: found || companies[0], topics
        };
    }
    function isCodingTarget() {
        const r = norm(roleText()), b = norm(branchText());
        return /software|python|java|full stack|web|developer|data analyst|data scientist|ai|ml|genai|cloud|devops|embedded|vlsi|iot|robot|automation|controls|electronics|electrical|mechanical|engineering|programming|technical/.test(r + ' ' + b);
    }
    function languageOptions() {
        const r = norm(roleText()), b = norm(branchText());
        if (/data analyst|data scientist/.test(r))
            return [
                'Python', 'SQL', 'R'
            ];
        if (/java developer/.test(r))
            return [
                'Java', 'Python', 'C++'
            ];
        if (/python developer|ai|ml|genai/.test(r))
            return [
                'Python', 'SQL', 'C++'
            ];
        if (/full stack|web/.test(r))
            return [
                'JavaScript', 'Python', 'Java'
            ];
        if (/cloud|devops/.test(r))
            return [
                'Python', 'JavaScript', 'Java', 'Bash'
            ];
        if (/embedded|vlsi|iot|electronics|electrical/.test(r + ' ' + b))
            return [
                'C', 'C++', 'Python'
            ];
        if (/robot|automation|controls/.test(r + ' ' + b))
            return [
                'C++', 'Python', 'C'
            ];
        if (/mechanical/.test(b))
            return [
                'Python', 'C++', 'C'
            ];
        if (/civil/.test(b))
            return [
                'Python', 'C'
            ];
        return [
            'Python', 'Java', 'C++', 'JavaScript', 'C'
        ];
    }
    function taskFor(language) {
        const r = norm(roleText()), b = norm(branchText()), focus = targetCompanyFocus();
        const lang = language || 'Python';
        if (/data analyst|data scientist/.test(r)) {
            if (lang === 'SQL') {
                return {
                    title: 'SQL challenge: find the top 3 categories by average result',
                    prompt: 'You have a table placement_results(category, student_id, score). Write one SQL query that returns the top 3 categories by average score, highest average first.',
                    starter: 'SELECT category, AVG(score) AS avg_score\nFROM placement_results\nGROUP BY category\nORDER BY avg_score DESC\nLIMIT 3;',
                    tests: 'Test data: Placement, 70 · Placement, 90 · DSA, 80 · DSA, 60 · SQL, 95\nExpected ordering: SQL → Placement → DSA',
                    focus: focus.topics[0]
                };
            }
            return {
                title: 'Data challenge: summarize placement scores by category',
                prompt: 'Given an array/list of records with category and score fields, calculate each category average and return the highest-scoring category. Handle an empty input cleanly.',
                starter: lang === 'Python' ? 'def best_category(records):\n    # records: [{"category":"DSA", "score":80}, ...]\n    pass' : lang === 'R' ? 'best_category <- function(records) {\n  # implement here\n}' : 'function bestCategory(records) {\n  // implement here\n}',
                tests: 'Input: DSA 80, DSA 60, SQL 90, SQL 70\nExpected best category: SQL',
                focus: focus.topics[0]
            };
        }
        if (/ai|ml|genai/.test(r)) {
            return {
                title: 'Model-evaluation challenge: calculate classification accuracy',
                prompt: 'Given predicted labels and actual labels, return the accuracy as the fraction of matching labels. Handle empty input without dividing by zero.',
                starter: lang === 'Python' ? 'def accuracy(predicted, actual):\n    # return a value between 0 and 1\n    pass' : lang === 'C++' ? 'double accuracy(const vector<int>& predicted, const vector<int>& actual) {\n    // implement here\n}' : 'public class Main {\n    // implement the accuracy method here\n}',
                tests: 'Predicted: [1, 0, 1, 1]\nActual:    [1, 1, 1, 0]\nExpected accuracy: 0.5',
                focus: focus.topics[0]
            };
        }
        if (/java developer/.test(r)) {
            return {
                title: 'Java problem: find the first non-repeating character',
                prompt: 'Write a method that returns the first character that appears exactly once in a string. Return a suitable empty result when no such character exists.',
                starter: 'import java.util.*;\n\nclass Main {\n    static char firstUnique(String s) {\n        // implement here\n        return \'\0\';\n    }\n}',
                tests: 'Input: "swiss"\nExpected: w\nInput: "aabb"\nExpected: no unique character',
                focus: focus.topics[0]
            };
        }
        if (/full stack|web/.test(r)) {
            return {
                title: 'Web/API challenge: count failed requests by endpoint',
                prompt: 'Given request records containing an endpoint and HTTP status code, count how many 4xx/5xx responses belong to each endpoint and return the endpoint with the most failures.',
                starter: lang === 'JavaScript' ? 'function busiestFailingEndpoint(requests) {\n  // requests: [{endpoint:"/login", status:500}, ...]\n  // implement here\n}' : lang === 'Java' ? 'class Main {\n    // implement busiestFailingEndpoint here\n}' : 'def busiest_failing_endpoint(requests):\n    # implement here\n    pass',
                tests: 'Input: /login 200, /login 500, /users 404, /users 404\nExpected: /users',
                focus: focus.topics[0]
            };
        }
        if (/cloud|devops/.test(r)) {
            return {
                title: 'Cloud/DevOps challenge: retry-safe error counter',
                prompt: 'Given deployment event records with service and status, count failed events per service and return the service with the highest failure count. Ignore successful events.',
                starter: lang === 'Python' ? 'def top_failed_service(events):\n    pass' : lang === 'Bash' ? '# Read events from stdin and summarize failed counts\n# example line: api,FAILED\n' : 'function topFailedService(events) {\n  // implement here\n}',
                tests: 'api FAILED, api OK, worker FAILED, worker FAILED\nExpected: worker',
                focus: focus.topics[0]
            };
        }
        if (/embedded|electronics|electrical|iot|vlsi/.test(r + ' ' + b)) {
            return {
                title: 'Embedded challenge: detect sensor threshold crossings',
                prompt: 'Given ordered sensor readings and a threshold, count how many times the readings move from at/below the threshold to above it. Return the crossing count.',
                starter: lang === 'C' ? 'int countCrossings(const int readings[], int n, int threshold) {\n    // implement here\n}' : lang === 'C++' ? 'int countCrossings(const vector<int>& readings, int threshold) {\n    // implement here\n}' : 'def count_crossings(readings, threshold):\n    pass',
                tests: 'Readings: [10, 12, 20, 18, 25], threshold: 18\nExpected crossings: 1',
                focus: focus.topics[0]
            };
        }
        if (/robot|automation|controls/.test(r + ' ' + b)) {
            return {
                title: 'Robotics challenge: choose the next safe state from sensor input',
                prompt: 'Given a sequence of integer sensor states where 0 means clear and 1 means obstacle, return the first index at which the system must stop because an obstacle is detected.',
                starter: lang === 'C++' ? 'int firstObstacle(const vector<int>& states) {\n    // implement here\n}' : lang === 'C' ? 'int firstObstacle(const int states[], int n) {\n    // implement here\n}' : 'def first_obstacle(states):\n    pass',
                tests: 'States: [0, 0, 0, 1, 0]\nExpected index: 3',
                focus: focus.topics[0]
            };
        }
        if (/civil/.test(b)) {
            return {
                title: 'Engineering data challenge: find the highest-load reading',
                prompt: 'Given measured load values from a simple engineering check, return the maximum load and its position. Handle an empty input clearly.',
                starter: lang === 'C' ? '/* return the maximum value and its index */\nint maxLoad(const int loads[], int n) {\n    // implement here\n}' : 'def max_load(loads):\n    # return value + index\n    pass',
                tests: 'Loads: [42, 55, 49, 61, 58]\nExpected max: 61 at index 3',
                focus: focus.topics[0]
            };
        }
        if (/mechanical/.test(b)) {
            return {
                title: 'Mechanical-data challenge: moving average of measurements',
                prompt: 'Given a list of machine measurements and a window size k, return the moving-average values. State how you handle invalid window sizes.',
                starter: lang === 'C++' ? 'vector<double> movingAverage(const vector<double>& values, int k) {\n    // implement here\n}' : 'def moving_average(values, k):\n    pass',
                tests: 'Values: [10, 20, 30, 40], k = 2\nExpected: [15, 25, 35]',
                focus: focus.topics[0]
            };
        }
        return {
            title: 'Programming challenge: find the first non-repeating character',
            prompt: 'Write a function that returns the first character that appears exactly once. Handle an empty string and the case where every character repeats.',
            starter: lang === 'Python' ? 'def first_unique(s):\n    pass' : lang === 'Java' ? 'import java.util.*;\n\nclass Main {\n    static char firstUnique(String s) {\n        // implement here\n        return \'\0\';\n    }\n}' : lang === 'C++' ? 'char firstUnique(const string& s) {\n    // implement here\n    return \'\0\';\n}' : lang === 'C' ? 'char firstUnique(const char* s) {\n    // implement here\n    return \'\0\';\n}' : lang === 'JavaScript' ? 'function firstUnique(s) {\n  // implement here\n}' : '# implement the requested function here',
            tests: 'Input: "swiss"\nExpected: w\nInput: "aabbcc"\nExpected: no unique character',
            focus: focus.topics[0]
        };
    }
    function codingMeta() {
        const focus = targetCompanyFocus();
        return {
            branch: branchText(), role: roleText(), company: focus.label, topics: focus.topics
        };
    }
    function codingBlock() {
        const a = state.assessment;
        const lang = a.codingLanguage || '';
        const meta = codingMeta();
        const langs = languageOptions();
        if (!lang) {
            return '<div class="coding-language-wrap">'
                + '<div class="coding-language-title">Choose your coding language</div>'
                + '<div class="coding-language-sub">This coding question is tailored to your selected branch, target job and company target. Choose the language you are most comfortable solving in.</div>'
                + '<div class="coding-context">'
                + '<span><b>Branch</b>' + escape(meta.branch) + '</span>'
                + '<span><b>Target job</b>' + escape(meta.role) + '</span>'
                + '<span><b>Company target</b>' + escape(meta.company) + '</span>'
                + '</div>'
                + '<div class="coding-language-list">' + langs.map(x => '<button type="button" class="coding-language" data-coding-language="' + escapeAttr(x) + '">' + escape(x) + '</button>').join('') + '</div>'
                + '<div class="coding-note">The company emphasis shown here is a practice preset for this prototype; it is not a claim about a company’s current hiring test.</div>'
                + '</div>';
        }
        const task = taskFor(lang);
        a.codingTask = task;
        a.codingRun = false;
        return '<div class="coding-language-wrap">'
            + '<div class="coding-language-title">Language: ' + escape(lang) + '</div>'
            + '<div class="coding-language-sub">Branch: ' + escape(meta.branch) + ' · Job: ' + escape(meta.role) + ' · Company target: ' + escape(meta.company) + '</div>'
            + '<div class="coding-language-list">' + langs.map(x => '<button type="button" class="coding-language ' + (x === lang ? 'selected' : '') + '" data-coding-language="' + escapeAttr(x) + '">' + escape(x) + '</button>').join('') + '</div>'
            + '</div>'
            + '<div class="coding-task">'
            + '<h3>' + escape(task.title) + '</h3>'
            + '<p>' + escape(task.prompt) + '</p>'
            + '<div class="coding-tests"><b>Sample test cases</b>\n' + escape(task.tests) + '</div>'
            + '</div>'
            + '<div class="editor" style="margin-top:14px"><div class="runbar"><span>' + escape(lang) + ' coding workspace</span><div class="coding-actions"><button class="secondary" type="button" data-action="resetCode">Reset starter</button><button class="primary" type="button" data-action="runCode">Run preview</button></div></div>'
            + '<textarea id="codeBox" spellcheck="false">' + escape(task.starter) + '</textarea>'
            + '<div id="codeResult" class="small" style="margin-top:10px;color:#bde6cf">Ready. Complete the solution and run the preview before moving to the next question.</div></div>'
            + '<p class="coding-note">Preview mode checks that you entered a substantive solution and records your submission. A production version should send the code to a sandboxed language runner for real compilation and test execution.</p>';
    }
    practicalBlock = function () {
        if (isCodingTarget())
            return codingBlock();
        return priorPracticalBlock();
    };
    assessmentSession = function () {
        const qs = currentQuestions();
        const q = qs[state.assessment.q];
        if (q && q.type === 'practical' && isCodingTarget()) {
            const task = taskFor(state.assessment.codingLanguage || 'Python');
            q.q = task.title + ' — ' + task.prompt;
        }
        return priorAssessmentSession();
    };
    function codingQuestionActive() {
        const q = (currentQuestions && currentQuestions()[state.assessment.q]) || {};
        return !!q && q.type === 'practical' && isCodingTarget();
    }
    function updateCodingNext() {
        if (!codingQuestionActive())
            return;
        const next = root.querySelector('[data-action="nextAssessment"]');
        if (!next)
            return;
        next.disabled = !(state.assessment.codingLanguage && state.assessment.codingRun);
    }
    action = function (a) {
        if (a === 'selectCodingLanguage') {
            const language = state.assessment.pendingCodingLanguage || '';
            if (language) {
                state.assessment.codingLanguage = language;
                state.assessment.codingRun = false;
                state.assessment.codingTask = taskFor(language);
                render();
            }
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'resetCode') {
            if (!codingQuestionActive() || !state.assessment.codingLanguage)
                return;
            const task = taskFor(state.assessment.codingLanguage);
            const box = document.getElementById('codeBox');
            const result = document.getElementById('codeResult');
            if (box)
                box.value = task.starter;
            state.assessment.codingRun = false;
            if (result)
                result.textContent = 'Starter code restored. Complete the task and run the preview.';
            updateCodingNext();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'runCode') {
            if (codingQuestionActive()) {
                const box = document.getElementById('codeBox');
                const result = document.getElementById('codeResult');
                const code = (box?.value || '').trim();
                const lang = state.assessment.codingLanguage || '';
                if (!lang) {
                    showToast('Choose a coding language first.');
                    return;
                }
                const incomplete = !code || /\bpass\b|implement here|write your solution|TODO|your solution here/i.test(code) || code.length < 40;
                if (incomplete) {
                    state.assessment.codingRun = false;
                    if (result) {
                        result.textContent = 'Preview check: the solution still looks incomplete. Replace the placeholder with your logic, then run again.';
                        result.style.color = 'var(--danger)';
                    }
                    updateCodingNext();
                    return;
                }
                state.assessment.codingRun = true;
                state.assessment.codingSubmission = code;
                state.assessment.codingSubmittedAt = Date.now();
                if (result) {
                    result.textContent = 'Preview check passed: your code is captured and ready for sandbox evaluation. Sample tests are shown above.';
                    result.style.color = 'var(--good)';
                }
                updateCodingNext();
                showToast(lang + ' solution captured for evaluation.');
                return;
            }
        }
        if (a === 'nextAssessment' && codingQuestionActive() && !state.assessment.codingRun) {
            showToast('Complete the coding solution and click Run preview before continuing.');
            return;
        }
        priorAction(a);
    };
    bind = function () {
        priorBind();
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-coding-language]').forEach(btn => {
            btn.onclick = () => { state.assessment.pendingCodingLanguage = btn.dataset.codingLanguage || ''; action('selectCodingLanguage'); };
        });
        updateCodingNext();
    };
    render = function () {
        priorRender();
        if (state.screen === 'assessment' && codingQuestionActive()) {
            updateCodingNext();
        }
    };
})();
/* === PLACEMENT360 FINAL PRODUCT PATCH: Firebase + AI workflow + labs + resume + interview === */
(function () {
    /* ---------- Firebase ---------- */
    const P360_FIREBASE_CONFIG = {
        apiKey: 'AIzaSyBpTdxOMqrufj7tOmKbNbVFOXQGTDoId6s',
        authDomain: 'placement360-6c615.firebaseapp.com',
        projectId: 'placement360-6c615',
        storageBucket: 'placement360-6c615.firebasestorage.app',
        messagingSenderId: '946132750860',
        appId: '1:946132750860:web:1a056fd39f7e7e08b31429',
        measurementId: 'G-X8T4CJEVRL'
    };
    let P360_FB_APP = null, P360_FB_AUTH = null, P360_FB_DB = null, P360_FIREBASE_STATUS = 'Local fallback';
    try {
        if (window.firebase) {
            P360_FB_APP = window.firebase.apps?.length ? window.firebase.app() : window.firebase.initializeApp(P360_FIREBASE_CONFIG);
            P360_FB_AUTH = window.firebase.auth();
            P360_FB_DB = window.firebase.firestore?.() || null;
            P360_FIREBASE_STATUS = 'Firebase connected';
        }
    }
    catch (e) {
        P360_FIREBASE_STATUS = 'Firebase unavailable — local mode';
    }
    state.firebaseUid = state.firebaseUid || '';
    state.accountCreatedAt = Number(state.accountCreatedAt || 0);
    state.firebaseReady = !!P360_FB_AUTH;
    state.resumeReview = state.resumeReview || null;
    state.companyMock = state.companyMock || null;
    state.companyMockResult = state.companyMockResult || null;
    state.chatMessages = state.chatMessages || [
        {
            who: 'ai', text: 'Hi! I’m your PLACEMENT360 interviewer/coach. Ask me what to study, what to improve, or what to do next.'
        }
    ];
    state.studyEngagement = state.studyEngagement || {};
    state.studySessionStartedAt = 0;
    state.studySessionLastTick = Date.now();
    state.interview = state.interview || null;
    state.activeLab = state.activeLab || null;
    state.todayDone = !!state.todayDone;
    /* ---------- Helpers ---------- */
    const dayNames = [
        'Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'
    ];
    function todayKey() { return dayNames[new Date().getDay()]; }
    function daysSinceAccount() {
        if (!state.accountCreatedAt)
            return 0;
        return Math.max(0, Math.floor((Date.now() - state.accountCreatedAt) / (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)));
    }
    function weeklyAssignmentUnlocked() { return daysSinceAccount() >= 6; }
    function isSunday() { return new Date().getDay() === 0; }
    function nextSundayText() {
        const d = new Date();
        const n = (7 - d.getDay()) % 7 || 7;
        d.setDate(d.getDate() + n);
        return d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
            weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'short'
        });
    }
    function queueFirebaseSave() {
        if (!P360_FB_DB || !state.firebaseUid)
            return;
        clearTimeout(queueFirebaseSave.t);
        queueFirebaseSave.t = setTimeout(async () => {
            try {
                await P360_FB_DB.collection('users').doc(state.firebaseUid).set({
                    email: state.userEmail || '', studentName: state.studentName || '', branch: state.branch || '', role: state.role || '', company: state.company || '',
                    companyNoIdea: !!state.companyNoIdea, accountCreatedAt: Number(state.accountCreatedAt || Date.now()), diagnosis: state.diagnosis || null,
                    weeklyAssessmentDone: !!state.weeklyAssessmentDone, weeklyDone: !!state.weeklyDone, todayDone: !!state.todayDone, todayCarry: !!state.todayCarry,
                    studyProgress: state.studyProgress || {}, studyEngagement: state.studyEngagement || {}, studyAttendance: state.studyAttendance || {},
                    resumeReview: state.resumeReview || null, companyMockResult: state.companyMockResult || null, skillEvidence: Number(state.skillEvidence || 0),
                    lastUpdated: Date.now()
                }, {
                    merge: true
                });
            }
            catch (e) { /* Firebase rules/offline can be handled by local fallback. */ }
        }, 700);
    }
    const priorPersistFinal = p360Persist;
    p360Persist = function () { studyInit(); if (!state.accountCreatedAt)
        state.accountCreatedAt = Date.now(); priorPersistFinal(); queueFirebaseSave(); };
    async function firebaseAuth(mode, email, password, name) {
        if (!P360_FB_AUTH)
            return {
                ok: false, unavailable: true
            };
        try {
            try {
                await P360_FB_AUTH.setPersistence(window.firebase.auth.Auth.Persistence.LOCAL);
            }
            catch (_e) { }
            let cred;
            if (mode === 'signup') {
                cred = await P360_FB_AUTH.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(email, password);
                try {
                    if (name)
                        await cred.user.updateProfile({
                            displayName: name
                        });
                }
                catch (_e) { }
                state.firebaseUid = cred.user.uid;
                state.accountCreatedAt = Date.now();
                try {
                    if (P360_FB_DB)
                        await P360_FB_DB.collection('users').doc(cred.user.uid).set({
                            email, name, studentName: name, accountCreatedAt: state.accountCreatedAt, createdAt: Date.now()
                        }, {
                            merge: true
                        });
                }
                catch (_e) { /* Authentication has already succeeded; Firestore profile sync can retry later. */ }
            }
            else {
                cred = await P360_FB_AUTH.signInWithEmailAndPassword(email, password);
                state.firebaseUid = cred.user.uid;
                try {
                    if (P360_FB_DB) {
                        const snap = await P360_FB_DB.collection('users').doc(cred.user.uid).get();
                        if (snap.exists) {
                            const u = snap.data() || {};
                            state.accountCreatedAt = Number(u.accountCreatedAt || 0);
                            if (u.studentName)
                                state.studentName = u.studentName;
                            p360ApplyUser(u);
                        }
                    }
                }
                catch (_e) { /* Firebase Auth login still succeeded even when profile read is blocked. */ }
                if (!state.accountCreatedAt)
                    state.accountCreatedAt = Date.now();
            }
            return {
                ok: true, user: cred.user
            };
        }
        catch (error) {
            return {
                ok: false, error
            };
        }
    }
    /* ---------- Top navigation / back ---------- */
    const priorTopbarFinal = topbar;
    topbar = function () {
        const showBack = state.screen !== 'landing';
        return '<header class="topbar">'
            + '<div class="brand"><div class="mascot">' + mascot + '<span class="spark">✦</span></div><div><div>PLACEMENT<span style="color:var(--purple)">360</span></div><small>AI placement companion</small></div></div>'
            + '<nav class="nav">'
            + (showBack ? '<button class="ghost" data-action="back">← Back</button>' : '')
            + '<button data-action="home">Home</button>'
            + '</nav></header>';
    };
    function backAction() {
        if (state.screen === 'auth') {
            state.screen = 'landing';
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (state.screen === 'welcome') {
            state.screen = 'auth';
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (state.screen === 'diagIntro') {
            state.screen = 'welcome';
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (state.screen === 'loading') {
            state.screen = 'diagIntro';
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (state.screen === 'report') {
            state.screen = 'diagIntro';
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (state.screen === 'assessment') {
            if (state.assessment?.mode === 'diagnostic') {
                state.screen = 'welcome';
                render();
                return;
            }
            if (state.assessment?.mode === 'weekly') {
                state.screen = 'app';
                state.view = 'assessment';
                render();
                return;
            }
        }
        if (state.screen === 'app') {
            if (state.view === 'studyTopic') {
                state.activeStudyTopic = '';
                state.view = 'studyMaterials';
                render();
                return;
            }
            if (state.view === 'labSession') {
                state.activeLab = null;
                state.view = 'labs';
                render();
                return;
            }
            if (state.view === 'companyMock') {
                state.companyMock = null;
                state.view = 'assessment';
                render();
                return;
            }
            if (state.view === 'interviewSession') {
                state.interview = null;
                state.view = 'interview';
                render();
                return;
            }
            if (state.view === 'resume') {
                state.view = 'dashboard';
                render();
                return;
            }
            if (state.view === 'studyGuide' || state.view === 'studyMaterials' || state.view === 'labs' || state.view === 'attendance' || state.view === 'assignments' || state.view === 'progress' || state.view === 'chat' || state.view === 'today' || state.view === 'interview' || state.view === 'concepts') {
                state.view = 'dashboard';
                render();
                return;
            }
            state.view = 'dashboard';
            render();
            return;
        }
        state.screen = 'landing';
        render();
    }
    /* ---------- Remove student profile from the UI ---------- */
    const studentDashboard = function () {
        studyInit();
        return topbar() + '<div class="workspace"><aside class="sidebar">'
            + '<div class="side-label">AI Workspace</div>'
            + '<button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'dashboard' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="dashboard">◉ Dashboard</button>'
            + '<button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'today' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="today">☀ Today’s AI Plan</button>'
            + '<button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'chat' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="chat">✦ AI Conversation</button>'
            + '<div class="side-label">Study</div>'
            + '<button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'studyGuide' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="studyGuide">▤ Learning Path</button>'
            + '<button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'studyMaterials' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="studyMaterials">📚 Study Materials</button>'
            + '<button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'labs' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="labs">⌘ Practice Labs</button>'
            + '<div class="side-label">AI Evidence</div>'
            + '<button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'assessment' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="assessment">⌁ Assessments</button>'
            + '<button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'assignments' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="assignments">✓ Weekly Assignment</button>'
            + '<button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'attendance' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="attendance">◷ AI Attendance</button>'
            + '<button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'progress' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="progress">↗ Progress & Evidence</button>'
            + '<div class="side-label">Career</div>'
            + '<button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'resume' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="resume">▤ Resume AI Review</button>'
            + '<button class="side-btn ' + (state.view === 'interview' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-view="interview">◉ AI Interview Practice</button>'
            + '<button class="side-btn" data-action="signout">← Sign out</button>'
            + '</aside><main class="mainspace"><div class="workspace-head"><div><b>PLACEMENT360</b><div class="small">' + escape(state.branch || 'Branch pending') + ' · ' + escape(state.role || 'Target role pending') + ' · ' + escape(state.companyNoIdea ? 'Common placement route' : state.company || 'Company pending') + '</div></div><span class="tag">AI systems active</span></div><div class="content ' + (state.view === 'studyMaterials' ? 'content-study-materials' : '') + '">' + viewContent() + '</div></main></div>' + shellFloat();
    };
    dashboard = studentDashboard;
    /* ---------- AI attendance / engagement ---------- */
    function initEngagement() { state.studyEngagement = state.studyEngagement || {}; state.studyAttendance = state.studyAttendance || {}; }
    function markVerifiedStudy(reason) {
        initEngagement();
        const k = todayKey();
        const rec = state.studyEngagement[k] || {
            minutes: 0, verified: false, sources: []
        };
        rec.verified = true;
        rec.sources = Array.from(new Set([
            ...(rec.sources || []), reason
        ]));
        state.studyEngagement[k] = rec;
        state.studyAttendance[k] = true;
        p360Persist();
    }
    function tickStudyEngagement() {
        if (!state.authed || state.screen !== 'app')
            return;
        const studying = [
            'studyGuide', 'studyMaterials', 'studyTopic', 'labs', 'labSession'
        ].includes(state.view);
        if (!studying || document.visibilityState !== 'visible') {
            state.studySessionLastTick = Date.now();
            return;
        }
        initEngagement();
        const now = Date.now();
        const delta = Math.min(30, Math.max(0, (now - state.studySessionLastTick) / 1000));
        state.studySessionLastTick = now;
        const k = todayKey();
        const rec = state.studyEngagement[k] || {
            minutes: 0, verified: false, sources: []
        };
        rec.minutes = Number(rec.minutes || 0) + delta / 60;
        state.studyEngagement[k] = rec;
        if (rec.minutes >= 2)
            markVerifiedStudy('AI study-time verification');
        if (rec.minutes >= 15) {
            state.studyAttendance[k] = true;
            rec.sources = Array.from(new Set([
                ...(rec.sources || []), '15+ minute study block'
            ]));
        }
        if (Math.floor(rec.minutes) % 5 === 0)
            p360Persist();
    }
    if (!state.studyEngagementTimer) {
        state.studyEngagementTimer = setInterval(tickStudyEngagement, 15000);
    }
    function learningAttendanceAI() {
        initEngagement();
        const days = [
            'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat', 'Sun'
        ];
        const rows = days.map(d => { const r = state.studyEngagement[d] || {
            minutes: 0, verified: false, sources: []
        }; return {
            d, verified: !!r.verified || !!state.studyAttendance[d], minutes: Math.round(Number(r.minutes || 0)), sources: r.sources || []
        }; });
        const present = rows.filter(r => r.verified).length, pct = Math.round(present / 7 * 100);
        return '<div class="panel attendance-hero"><div><span class="eyebrow">AI ATTENDANCE</span><h2>Attendance is earned from study activity.</h2><p class="muted">There are no Present/Absent buttons. PLACEMENT360 records participation from visible study time, completed labs and completed assessment evidence.</p></div><div><div class="metric-big">' + pct + '%</div><span class="small">AI-verified weekly participation</span></div></div>'
            + '<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><span class="eyebrow">WEEK VIEW</span><h3>Study engagement</h3></div><span class="tag">' + present + '/7 AI verified</span></div><div class="attendance-week">' + rows.map(r => '<div class="attendance-day ' + (r.verified ? 'present' : '') + '"><b>' + r.d + '</b><span>' + (r.verified ? '●' : '○') + '</span><small>' + (r.verified ? 'Present' : 'No verified study') + '</small><small style="display:block;margin-top:5px">' + r.minutes + ' min</small></div>').join('') + '</div></div>'
            + '<div class="cards"><div class="mini-card"><b>Today</b><p class="small">' + (rows.find(r => r.d === todayKey())?.verified ? 'AI detected active study today.' : 'No verified study block detected yet today.') + '</p></div><div class="mini-card"><b>How it is recorded</b><p class="small">Study materials, labs and assessment work are the evidence sources. The student cannot manually change attendance.</p></div></div>';
    }
    learningAttendance = learningAttendanceAI;
    function todaysPlanAI() {
        const next = typeof studyNext === 'function' ? studyNext() : 'Your next weak topic';
        const rec = state.studyEngagement?.[todayKey()] || {
            minutes: 0, verified: false
        };
        return '<div class="panel study-hero"><div><span class="eyebrow">TODAY’S AI PLAN</span><h2>' + escape(state.studentName || 'Student') + ', your next best action is ' + escape(next) + '.</h2><p class="muted">The AI creates today’s route from your diagnostic, target role, weak areas and recent evidence. You do not manually mark attendance or absence.</p><div class="study-seq"><span>01 Learn</span><span>02 Examples</span><span>03 Lab</span><span>04 Quick check</span><span>05 Reflect</span></div><button class="primary" data-study-open="' + escapeAttr(next) + '">Start today’s study →</button></div><div class="panel" style="margin:0"><span class="small">AI engagement</span><div class="metric-big">' + Math.round(rec.minutes || 0) + ' min</div><span class="small">' + (rec.verified ? 'Verified study activity detected.' : 'Waiting for verified study activity.') + '</span><div class="skillbar" style="margin-top:12px"><i style="width:' + Math.min(100, Math.round((rec.minutes || 0) / 30 * 100)) + '%"></i></div></div></div>'
            + '<div class="cards"><div class="mini-card"><b>Carry-over</b><p class="small">' + (state.todayCarry ? 'AI moved an unfinished task here as a priority.' : 'No carry-over is currently waiting.') + '</p></div><div class="mini-card"><b>Attendance</b><p class="small">The AI will mark today only when verified learning activity is detected.</p></div></div>';
    }
    todaysPlan = todaysPlanAI;

    const pythonCompleteNotes = "1. Python Basics\nWhat is Python?\nPython is a high-level, interpreted, general-purpose programming language. It emphasizes readable\nsyntax and supports procedural, object-oriented and functional programming.\nKey features\nPython is easy to read, dynamically typed, cross-platform, supported by a large standard library, and\nhas a large ecosystem of third-party packages.\nFirst program\nprint(\"Hello, Python!\")\nComments\n# This is a single-line comment\nprint(\"Hello\")\nImportant beginner rules\nPython uses indentation to define blocks. Usually four spaces are used. Python is case-sensitive: name\nand Name are different identifiers.\n2. Variables, Data Types & Type Conversion\nVariables\nA variable is a name referring to a value. Python does not require a separate declaration of the variable\ntype.\nage = 20\nname = \"Anu\"\nprice = 99.5\npassed = True\nMain data types\nint: whole numbers float: decimal numbers complex: numbers such as 2+3j bool: True or False str: text\nNoneType: None\nType checking and conversion\nUse type(value) to inspect a type. Common conversions are int(), float(), str() and bool().\nx = \"25\"\nn = int(x)\nprint(n + 5)\nprint(type(n))\n3. Operators\nArithmetic\n+, -, *, /, //, %, **. Note that / gives ordinary division while // gives floor division.\nComparison\n==, !=, >, <, >=, <= produce Boolean results.\nLogical\n\nand, or, not combine or invert conditions.\nAssignment\n=, +=, -=, *=, /=, //=, %=, **= update variables.\nMembership and identity\nin / not in test membership. is / is not test object identity; use == when comparing values.\nBitwise\n&, |, ^, ~, <<, >> operate on integer bits.\n4. Input, Output & Formatting\nInput\ninput() always returns text. Convert it when numeric input is required.\nname = input(\"Name: \")\nage = int(input(\"Age: \"))\nprint(name, age)\nFormatted strings\nf-strings are a clear way to insert values into text.\nname = \"Ravi\"\nmarks = 85\nprint(f\"{name} scored {marks}\")\n5. Conditional Statements\nif / elif / else\nUse conditions to choose which block should run.\nmarks = 72\nif marks >= 90:\n    grade = \"A\"\nelif marks >= 60:\n    grade = \"B\"\nelse:\n    grade = \"C\"\nprint(grade)\nNested conditions\nAn if statement can appear inside another if statement. Keep nesting shallow when possible for\nreadability.\nTernary expression\nresult = \"Pass\" if marks >= 40 else \"Fail\"\n6. Loops\nfor loop\nUsed to iterate over an iterable.\nfor i in range(1, 6):\n    print(i)\n\nwhile loop\nRepeats while a condition remains true.\ni = 1\nwhile i <= 5:\n    print(i)\n    i += 1\nLoop controls\nbreak stops the loop. continue skips to the next iteration. pass does nothing and is useful as a\nplaceholder.\nNested loops\nA loop can contain another loop; common in matrix and pattern problems.\n7. Strings\nBasics\nStrings are immutable sequences of characters. Indexing starts at 0 and negative indices count from the\nend.\ns = \"Python\"\nprint(s[0])\nprint(s[-1])\nprint(s[1:4])\nImportant methods\nupper(), lower(), capitalize(), title(), strip(), replace(), split(), join(), find(), count(), startswith(),\nendswith(), isdigit(), isalpha(), isalnum().\nString formatting\nUse f-strings for readable formatting.\nImportant interview idea\nBecause strings are immutable, operations that appear to modify a string actually create a new string.\n8. Lists\nBasics\nLists are ordered, mutable collections and can contain different data types.\nnumbers = [10, 20, 30]\nnumbers.append(40)\nnumbers[0] = 5\nprint(numbers)\nImportant methods\nappend(), extend(), insert(), remove(), pop(), clear(), index(), count(), sort(), reverse(), copy().\nSlicing\nitems[start:stop:step] creates a slice.\nNested lists\nA list can contain other lists, which is useful for representing matrices.\n\n9. Tuples\nBasics\nTuples are ordered and immutable. They are useful for fixed collections of values.\npoint = (10, 20)\nx, y = point\nImportant ideas\nTuple packing groups values into a tuple; tuple unpacking assigns its elements to variables. Main\nmethods are count() and index().\n10. Sets\nBasics\nA set is an unordered collection of unique elements.\na = {1, 2, 3}\nb = {3, 4, 5}\nprint(a | b)\nprint(a & b)\nprint(a - b)\nOperations\nUnion |, intersection &, difference -, symmetric difference ^. Also learn issubset(), issuperset() and\nisdisjoint().\nUse case\nSets are excellent for removing duplicates and fast membership checks.\n11. Dictionaries\nBasics\nA dictionary stores key-value pairs.\nstudent = {\"name\": \"Riya\", \"marks\": 88}\nprint(student[\"name\"])\nstudent[\"marks\"] = 92\nImportant methods\nkeys(), values(), items(), get(), update(), pop(), popitem(), setdefault(), clear(), copy().\nIteration\nfor key, value in student.items(): print(key, value)\nNested dictionaries\nValues can themselves be dictionaries or lists, allowing structured data.\n12. Functions\nDefinition\nFunctions package reusable logic.\ndef add(a, b):\n    return a + b\n\nprint(add(3, 4))\nArguments\nKnow positional arguments, keyword arguments, default arguments, *args and **kwargs.\nScope\nVariables may be local or global. Understand LEGB lookup: Local, Enclosing, Global, Built-in.\nGood practice\nPrefer small functions with clear names, one main responsibility, and useful return values.\n13. Recursion\nDefinition\nRecursion occurs when a function calls itself. Every recursive solution needs a base case.\ndef factorial(n):\n    if n == 0:\n        return 1\n    return n * factorial(n - 1)\nUse cases\nTrees, divide-and-conquer, backtracking and certain mathematical problems.\nWarning\nDeep recursion can hit Python's recursion limit; iterative solutions are often more memory-efficient.\n14. Lambda & Functional Tools\nLambda\nA lambda is a small anonymous function.\nsquare = lambda x: x * x\nprint(square(5))\nmap / filter\nmap transforms items; filter keeps items satisfying a condition.\nnums = [1, 2, 3, 4]\ndoubled = list(map(lambda x: x*2, nums))\neven = list(filter(lambda x: x%2 == 0, nums))\nreduce\nfunctools.reduce() combines values cumulatively. Use it when it makes the logic clearer than an\nordinary loop.\n15. Comprehensions\nList comprehension\nCompactly create a list from an iterable.\nsquares = [x*x for x in range(1, 6)]\nevens = [x for x in range(10) if x % 2 == 0]\nOther comprehensions\n\nSet comprehensions use { ... }; dictionary comprehensions use {key: value for ...}.\nAdvice\nUse comprehensions for simple transformations. Prefer ordinary loops when the logic becomes difficult\nto read.\n16. Modules & Packages\nModules\nA module is a Python file containing reusable definitions.\nimport math\nprint(math.sqrt(25))\nfrom math import pi\nUseful standard modules\nmath, random, datetime, os, sys, statistics, collections, re, json.\nPackages and pip\nA package organizes modules. pip installs packages from package indexes. Use virtual environments to\nisolate project dependencies.\n17. Exception Handling\nWhy exceptions?\nExceptions allow a program to handle runtime problems without abruptly terminating.\ntry:\n    x = int(input(\"Number: \"))\n    print(10 / x)\nexcept ValueError:\n    print(\"Enter a valid integer\")\nexcept ZeroDivisionError:\n    print(\"Cannot divide by zero\")\nfinally:\n    print(\"Finished\")\nKeywords\ntry, except, else, finally and raise.\nGood practice\nCatch specific exceptions instead of using a broad except when possible.\n18. File Handling\nOpening files\nUse open() and preferably the with statement so the file closes automatically.\nwith open(\"data.txt\", \"r\") as f:\n    data = f.read()\nprint(data)\nModes\nr read, w write/overwrite, a append, x create, rb binary read, wb binary write.\n\nMethods\nread(), readline(), readlines(), write(), writelines().\nCommon formats\nText, CSV, JSON and binary files are frequently used in applications.\n19. Object-Oriented Programming\nClass and object\nA class defines a structure and behavior; an object is an instance of a class.\nclass Student:\n    def __init__(self, name, marks):\n        self.name = name\n        self.marks = marks\n    def display(self):\n        print(self.name, self.marks)\ns = Student(\"Anu\", 90)\ns.display()\nCore concepts\nEncapsulation, inheritance, polymorphism and abstraction.\nInheritance\nA child class can reuse or extend a parent class.\nsuper()\nUse super() to access parent-class behavior.\nSpecial methods\nExamples include __init__, __str__, __len__, __eq__ and other dunder methods.\n20. Iterators & Generators\nIterator\nAn iterator provides values one at a time through __iter__() and __next__().\nnums = [1, 2, 3]\nit = iter(nums)\nprint(next(it))\nGenerator\nA generator function uses yield and produces values lazily.\ndef numbers():\n    for i in range(3):\n        yield i\nBenefit\nGenerators can reduce memory use when processing large sequences.\n21. Decorators\n\nDefinition\nA decorator wraps a function and changes or extends its behavior.\ndef log_call(func):\n    def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):\n        print(\"Calling function\")\n        return func(*args, **kwargs)\n    return wrapper\n@log_call\ndef hello():\n    print(\"Hello\")\nUses\nLogging, timing, authentication checks, caching and reusable cross-cutting behavior.\n22. Regular Expressions\nModule\nPython's re module provides regular-expression operations.\nimport re\ntext = \"My marks are 85\"\nprint(re.findall(r\"\\d+\", text))\nImportant functions\nsearch(), match(), findall(), finditer(), sub().\nImportant pattern concepts\nCharacter classes, anchors, groups, quantifiers and escaping.\n23. Date & Time\ndatetime\nUse the datetime module for dates, times and time differences.\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\nnow = datetime.now()\ntomorrow = now + timedelta(days=1)\nprint(now)\nprint(tomorrow)\nStudy\ndate, time, datetime, timedelta, formatting with strftime(), and parsing with strptime().\n24. Collections Module\nCounter\nCounts hashable objects.\nfrom collections import Counter\nprint(Counter(\"banana\"))\ndefaultdict\nProvides a default value for missing keys.\ndeque\n\nA double-ended queue optimized for adding/removing from both ends.\nOther tools\nnamedtuple, OrderedDict and ChainMap are useful in specific situations.\n25. JSON\nPurpose\nJSON is a common format for exchanging structured data.\nimport json\nobj = {\"name\": \"Ravi\", \"age\": 20}\ntext = json.dumps(obj)\nback = json.loads(text)\nFunctions\ndumps()/loads() convert between Python objects and JSON strings. dump()/load() work with files.\n26. Useful Built-in Functions\nMust know\nlen(), type(), id(), range(), sum(), min(), max(), abs(), round(), sorted(), reversed(), enumerate(), zip(),\nmap(), filter(), any(), all().\nenumerate\nAdds an index while iterating.\nfor i, value in enumerate([\"a\", \"b\"]):\n    print(i, value)\nzip\nCombines elements from multiple iterables.\nnames = [\"A\", \"B\"]\nmarks = [90, 80]\nfor name, mark in zip(names, marks):\n    print(name, mark)\n27. Sorting\nsort vs sorted\nlist.sort() changes the list in place. sorted(iterable) returns a new sorted list.\nnums = [4, 1, 3, 2]\nnums.sort()\nprint(nums)\nprint(sorted(nums, reverse=True))\nkey function\nThe key argument controls what value is used for comparison.\nwords = [\"cat\", \"elephant\", \"dog\"]\nprint(sorted(words, key=len))\n28. Database & SQL Basics\nDatabase concepts\n\nA database stores structured information in tables. A row is a record and a column is an attribute.\nSQLite\nPython includes sqlite3 for lightweight local databases.\nimport sqlite3\ncon = sqlite3.connect(\"school.db\")\ncur = con.cursor()\ncur.execute(\"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS students(name TEXT, marks INTEGER)\")\ncur.execute(\"INSERT INTO students VALUES (?, ?)\", (\"Anu\", 90))\ncon.commit()\ncon.close()\nSQL basics\nLearn SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE, CREATE TABLE, WHERE, ORDER BY, GROUP BY, JOIN and\naggregate functions.\n29. NumPy\nPurpose\nNumPy provides efficient multidimensional arrays and numerical operations.\nimport numpy as np\na = np.array([1, 2, 3])\nprint(a * 2)\nStudy\narray creation, dimensions, indexing, slicing, reshape, broadcasting, aggregation and vectorized\noperations.\n30. Pandas\nPurpose\nPandas provides Series and DataFrame structures for data analysis.\nimport pandas as pd\ndf = pd.DataFrame({\"Name\": [\"A\", \"B\"], \"Marks\": [90, 80]})\nprint(df.head())\nStudy\nread_csv(), read_excel(), head(), info(), describe(), filtering, sorting, groupby(), merge(), join(), handling\nmissing values and exporting data.\n31. Matplotlib\nPurpose\nMatplotlib creates charts and visualizations.\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nx = [1, 2, 3]\ny = [2, 4, 3]\nplt.plot(x, y)\nplt.xlabel(\"X\")\nplt.ylabel(\"Y\")\nplt.title(\"Example\")\nplt.show()\nStudy\n\nLine, bar, scatter, histogram, labels, titles, legends, axes and saving figures.\n32. APIs & Web Basics\nHTTP\nLearn requests and responses, status codes, headers, URLs, query parameters and common methods\nGET, POST, PUT and DELETE.\nRequests\nThe requests package is commonly used to call web APIs.\nJSON APIs\nMany APIs return JSON. Convert the response into Python data and validate errors/status codes.\nWeb basics\nFor web development, understand HTML, CSS, JavaScript basics, HTTP and REST concepts before\nlearning Flask or Django.\n33. Testing & Debugging\nTesting\nTests verify that code behaves as expected.\ndef add(a, b):\n    return a + b\nassert add(2, 3) == 5\nTools\nLearn unittest and basic pytest usage.\nDebugging\nUnderstand syntax errors, runtime exceptions and logical errors. Read tracebacks from the bottom\nupward to find the immediate cause.\n34. Virtual Environments & pip\nWhy environments?\nThey keep project dependencies isolated.\npython -m venv venv\nCommon commands\nCreate an environment, activate it, install packages with pip, and record dependencies in\nrequirements.txt.\nBest practice\nUse one environment per project when dependencies differ.\n35. Advanced Python\nTopics\n\nClosures, decorators in depth, context managers, __enter__/__exit__, dunder methods, properties,\ndataclasses, type hints, pattern matching, asyncio, threading and multiprocessing.\nContext manager\nThe with statement commonly relies on context-manager behavior to acquire and release resources\nsafely.\nAsync basics\nasync/await and asyncio support concurrent I/O-oriented programs.\n36. Data Structures & Algorithms\nCore structures\nArrays/lists, strings, stacks, queues, linked lists, hash tables/dictionaries, sets, trees, heaps and graphs.\nSearching\nLinear search scans items. Binary search works on sorted data and repeatedly halves the search space.\nSorting\nKnow bubble, selection, insertion, merge and quick sort conceptually, plus Python's built-in sorting.\nGraph/tree\nLearn BFS, DFS, tree traversal and basic binary-search-tree concepts.\nComplexity\nLearn Big-O notation and compare time and space requirements.\n37. Placement Problem-Solving Patterns\nFrequency counting\nUse dictionaries or Counter to count occurrences.\nTwo pointers\nUse two indices to solve many sorted-array and string problems efficiently.\nSliding window\nMaintain a moving range for subarray/substring problems.\nPrefix sum\nPrecompute cumulative sums to answer range-sum queries efficiently.\nHashing\nUse sets/dictionaries for fast membership and lookup.\nStack pattern\nUseful for parentheses, next-greater-element and monotonic-stack problems.\nBinary search\nUse whenever the search space is ordered or a monotonic condition can be tested.\n\nBacktracking\nBuild a candidate solution step by step and undo choices when necessary.\nGreedy / DP\nGreedy makes locally optimal choices; dynamic programming stores overlapping subproblem results.\n38. Common Coding Questions\nBeginner\nReverse a string, palindrome, factorial, Fibonacci, prime number, Armstrong number, sum of digits,\nlargest/second-largest element.\nStrings\nCharacter frequency, anagram, duplicate characters, first non-repeating character, substring problems.\nArrays/lists\nRemove duplicates, missing number, two sum, rotate array, merge sorted arrays, maximum subarray.\nSearching/sorting\nLinear search, binary search, sorting a list, kth largest/smallest.\nData structures\nImplement stack/queue, linked-list operations, tree traversal and BFS/DFS.\nTip\nFor every problem: understand input/output, write a simple approach, test edge cases, then optimize.\n39. Study Plan & Revision Checklist\nWeek 1\nBasics, variables, types, operators, input/output and conditions. Solve 15–20 small problems.\nWeek 2\nLoops, strings, lists, tuples, sets and dictionaries. Solve pattern and collection problems.\nWeek 3\nFunctions, recursion, lambda, comprehensions, modules, exceptions and files.\nWeek 4\nOOP, iterators, generators, decorators, regex, JSON and datetime.\nWeek 5\nSQL, databases, NumPy/Pandas basics and APIs.\nWeek 6+\nData structures, algorithms, placement problems and mock interviews.\nRevision checklist\n\nBe able to explain each topic in your own words, write a small example without notes, identify common\nerrors, and solve at least 3–5 problems per major topic.\n\nQuick Python Cheat Sheet\nCore syntax\n# variable\nx = 10\n# condition\nif x > 5:\n    print(\"yes\")\n# loop\nfor i in range(5):\n    print(i)\n# function\ndef add(a, b):\n    return a + b\n# list\nitems = [1, 2, 3]\n# dictionary\ndata = {\"name\": \"A\", \"marks\": 90}\n# exception\ntry:\n    value = int(input())\nexcept ValueError:\n    print(\"Invalid input\")\nImportant collection choices\nList → ordered and mutable. Tuple → ordered and immutable. Set → unique elements. Dictionary →\nkey-value mapping.\nPlacement checklist\n• Master strings, lists, dictionaries and sets.\n• Practice loops and functions until syntax becomes automatic.\n• Learn time and space complexity.\n• Practice searching, sorting, hashing, two pointers and sliding window.\n• Solve problems regularly rather than only reading solutions.\n• After solving, explain your approach aloud as interview practice.";
    studyTopicDetail = function (t) {

    function labForTopic(topic) {
        const exact = LAB_BANK[topic];
        if (exact)
            return exact;
        const key = Object.keys(LAB_BANK).find(k => topic.toLowerCase().includes(k.toLowerCase()) || k.toLowerCase().includes(topic.toLowerCase()));
        if (key)
            return LAB_BANK[key];
        return {
            title: topic + ' practical challenge', prompt: 'Complete one applied task for ' + topic + '. Explain your approach and edge cases.', starter: 'def solve(data):\n    # write your solution here\n    pass', tests: 'Create at least 2 test cases and verify your output.'
        };
    }
    function labView() {

'selected' : '') + '" data-lab-answer="' + i + '">' + String.fromCharCode(65 + i) + '. ' + escape(x) + '</button>').join('') + '<button class="primary" style="margin-top:10px" data-action="submitLab">Submit lab</button><div class="small" style="margin-top:10px" id="labResult">' + (state.labResult || 'Choose an answer and submit when ready.') + '</div></div></div>';

    function companyMockView() {
        const m = state.companyMock || {
            q: 0, answers: {}, code: '', startedAt: Date.now()
        };
        state.companyMock = m;
        const q = COMPANY_MOCK_BANK[m.q];
        if (!q)
            return companyMockResultView();
        const pct = Math.round((m.q / COMPANY_MOCK_BANK.length) * 100);
        let body = '';
        if (q.type === 'mcq')
            body = q.opts.map((x, i) => '<button class="answer-option ' + (m.answers[m.q] === i ? 'selected' : '') + '" data-company-answer="' + i + '">' + String.fromCharCode(65 + i) + '. ' + escape(x) + '</button>').join('');
        else if (q.type === 'coding')
            body = '<div class="editor"><div class="runbar"><span>Python coding workspace</span><button class="primary" data-action="runCompanyCode">Run preview</button></div><textarea id="companyCode" spellcheck="false">' + escape(m.code || q.starter) + '</textarea><div id="companyCodeResult" class="small" style="margin-top:10px;color:#bde6cf">Sample tests: ' + escape(q.tests) + '</div></div>';
        else
            body = '<textarea id="companyText" style="width:100%;min-height:160px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;padding:12px;resize:vertical">' + escape(m.answers[m.q] || '') + '</textarea>';
        return '<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><span class="eyebrow">COMPANY MOCK TEST</span><h2>' + escape(q.company) + ' · ' + escape(q.section) + '</h2></div><span class="tag">Question ' + (m.q + 1) + ' / ' + COMPANY_MOCK_BANK.length + '</span></div><div class="progress-line" style="margin:14px 0"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div><section class="question-card"><span class="level-chip">' + escape(q.section) + '</span><h3 style="margin:12px 0">' + escape(q.q) + '</h3>' + body + '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;margin-top:16px"><button class="secondary" data-action="companyMockBack">Back</button><button class="primary" data-action="companyMockNext">' + (m.q === COMPANY_MOCK_BANK.length - 1 ? 'Finish mock' : 'Next →') + '</button></div></section></div>';
    }
    function companyMockResultView() {
        const r = state.companyMockResult || {
            score: 0, total: 0, band: 'Needs work', strengths: [], gaps: []
        };
        return '<div class="panel"><span class="eyebrow">AI MOCK EVALUATION</span><h2>Mock completed · ' + r.score + '%</h2><p class="muted">The result has been added to your dashboard evidence and used to choose your next study priorities.</p><div class="cards"><div class="mini-card"><b>Strengths</b><p class="small">' + r.strengths.map(escape).join(' · ') + '</p></div><div class="mini-card"><b>Priority gaps</b><p class="small">' + r.gaps.map(escape).join(' · ') + '</p></div></div><div class="diagnosis-note" style="margin-top:16px"><b>AI next step:</b> ' + escape(r.next || 'Continue with the highest-priority weak topic, then retry the mock after new evidence is earned.') + '</div><button class="primary" style="margin-top:14px" data-view="dashboard">Back to dashboard</button></div>';
    }
    function finishCompanyMock() {
        const m = state.companyMock;
        let correct = 0, total = 0;
        const sectionScores = {};
        COMPANY_MOCK_BANK.forEach((q, i) => {
            if (q.type === 'mcq') {
                total++;
                if (m.answers[i] === q.ans) {
                    correct++;
                    sectionScores[q.section] = (sectionScores[q.section] || 0) + 1;
                }
                else
                    sectionScores[q.section] = sectionScores[q.section] || 0;
            }
            if (q.type === 'coding') {
                total++;
                if (m.code && m.code.length > 60 && !/pass|write your solution|TODO/i.test(m.code)) {
                    correct++;
                    sectionScores[q.section] = 1;
                }
                else
                    sectionScores[q.section] = 0;
            }
            if (q.type === 'text') {
                total++;
                const txt = String(m.answers[i] || '').trim();
                if (txt.length >= 80) {
                    correct++;
                    sectionScores[q.section] = 1;
                }
                else
                    sectionScores[q.section] = 0;
            }
        });
        const score = Math.round(correct / Math.max(1, total) * 100);
        const strengths = [];
        const gaps = [];
        Object.entries(sectionScores).forEach(([k, v]) => { if (v >= .7)
            strengths.push(k);
        else
            gaps.push(k); });
        const next = gaps.length ? 'Focus next on ' + gaps.join(', ') + '.' : 'Maintain consistency and increase coding depth.';
        state.companyMockResult = {
            score, total, band: score >= 80 ? 'Placement ready' : score >= 60 ? 'Developing' : 'Needs focused practice', strengths, gaps, next
        };
        state.skillEvidence = Math.min(100, Math.max(Number(state.skillEvidence || 0), score));
        state.companyMock = null;
        state.view = 'assessment';
        recordVerifiedStudy('company mock completion');
        p360Persist();
        render();
    }
    /* ---------- Adaptive AI interview ---------- */
    const ADAPTIVE_INTERVIEW_FLOWS = {
