    function interviewFlow(key) { return ADAPTIVE_INTERVIEW_FLOWS[key] || ADAPTIVE_INTERVIEW_FLOWS.behavioral; }
    function interviewStep(key, id) { const f = interviewFlow(key); return f.steps.find(s => s.id === id) || f.steps[0]; }
    function interviewCategoryButton(key) {
        const f = interviewFlow(key), done = !!(state.interviewResults && state.interviewResults[key]);
        return '<button class="interview-category ' + (state.interview?.category === key ? 'selected' : '') + '" data-action="selectInterviewCategory" data-interview-category="' + key + '"><span class="interview-category-icon">' + f.icon + '</span><span><b>' + escape(f.title) + '</b><small>' + (done ? '✓ Completed · practice again' : 'Start adaptive questions') + '</small></span><span class="interview-category-arrow">→</span></button>';
    }
    function interviewPanel() {
        return '<div class="panel adaptive-interview-panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><span class="eyebrow">ADAPTIVE INTERVIEW</span><h2>Choose the interview area</h2><p class="muted">Select a category. The next question changes based on your answer, including Yes/No branching where appropriate.</p></div><span class="tag">Nova · AI interviewer</span></div><div class="interview-category-grid">' + Object.keys(ADAPTIVE_INTERVIEW_FLOWS).map(interviewCategoryButton).join('') + '</div><div class="mini-card" style="margin-top:16px"><b>How it works</b><p class="small">Choose a category → answer the question → choose Yes/No when shown → receive the next follow-up → finish the section → select another category.</p></div></div>';
    }
    function interviewSession() {
        const it = state.interview || {}, f = interviewFlow(it.category || 'behavioral'), step = interviewStep(it.category || 'behavioral', it.stepId || f.steps[0].id);
        let answerUi = '';
        if (step.type === 'yesno')
            answerUi = '<div class="interview-yesno"><button class="interview-choice yes" data-action="interviewChoice" data-interview-choice="yes">Yes</button><button class="interview-choice no" data-action="interviewChoice" data-interview-choice="no">No</button></div>';
        else if (step.type === 'choice')
            answerUi = '<div class="interview-yesno"><button class="interview-choice" data-action="interviewChoice" data-interview-choice="C">C</button><button class="interview-choice" data-action="interviewChoice" data-interview-choice="Java">Java</button></div>';
        else if (step.type === 'end')
            answerUi = '<div class="diagnosis-note"><b>Section complete.</b><p class="small">Your answers were recorded for interview coaching.</p></div><button class="primary" data-action="finishInterviewCategory">Finish ' + escape(f.title) + ' →</button>';
        else
            answerUi = '<div class="conversation-input"><input id="interviewInput" placeholder="Type your answer…"><button class="secondary" data-action="interviewVoice">🎙 Voice</button><button class="primary" data-action="submitInterview">Send</button></div><div id="interviewVoiceStatus" class="small" style="margin-top:8px">Text or voice answer accepted.</div>';
        const transcript = (it.messages || []).slice(-20).map(m => '<div class="chat-msg ' + m.who + '">' + escape(m.text) + '</div>').join('');
        return '<div class="panel adaptive-interview-session"><div class="panel-head"><div><span class="eyebrow">' + escape(f.title.toUpperCase()) + '</span><h2>Nova · adaptive interviewer</h2></div><span class="tag">' + escape(f.title) + '</span></div><div class="chat-stream" style="margin-top:16px">' + transcript + '</div><div class="question-card"><span class="level-chip">CURRENT QUESTION</span><h3 style="margin:12px 0">' + escape(step.q) + '</h3>' + answerUi + '</div><div style="display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:12px"><button class="secondary" data-action="backToInterviewCategories">← Categories</button></div></div>';
    }
    interview = interviewPanel;
    function evaluateInterview(text) {
        const clean = text.trim(), len = clean.length, hasExample = /example|project|built|developed|result|because|measured|%|number|challenge|team|responsibility/i.test(clean), hasStructure = /first|second|then|finally|situation|task|action|result|because/i.test(clean);
        let score = 45 + Math.min(25, Math.round(len / 15)) + (hasExample ? 18 : 0) + (hasStructure ? 12 : 0);
        score = Math.min(100, score);
        return {
            score, feedback: score >= 80 ? 'Strong answer: clear, relevant and evidence-based.' : score >= 65 ? 'Good answer. Add one concrete example or measurable result to make it stronger.' : 'Answer is too brief or generic. Add context, your action and the result.'
        };
    }
    function addInterviewUserMessage(text) { state.interview.messages.push({
        who: 'user', text
    }); }
    function addInterviewAIMessage(text) { state.interview.messages.push({
        who: 'ai', text
    }); }
    function startInterviewCategory(key) {
        const f = interviewFlow(key), first = f.steps[0];
        state.interview = {
            category: key, stepId: first.id, messages: [
                {
                    who: 'ai', text: 'Let’s begin ' + f.title + '. I’ll adapt the next question based on your answer.'
                }, {
                    who: 'ai', text: first.q
                }
            ], answers: [], scores: []
        };
        state.view = 'interviewSession';
        state.screen = 'app';
        render();
    }
    function advanceInterviewFromAnswer(value) {
        const it = state.interview, cur = interviewStep(it.category, it.stepId);
        let next = 'end';
        const v = String(value || '').trim();
        if (cur.id === 'languages') {
            const hasC = /C|C language|C programming/i.test(v), hasJava = /Java|Java programming/i.test(v);
            next = hasC && hasJava ? 'languageChoice' : hasC ? 'cProject' : hasJava ? 'javaProject' : 'difficultPart';
        }
        else if (cur.type === 'yesno')
            next = value === 'yes' ? cur.yes : cur.no;
        else if (cur.id === 'languageChoice')
            next = value === 'C' ? 'cProject' : 'javaProject';
        else if (cur.id === 'cProject' || cur.id === 'javaProject' || cur.id === 'otherProject')
            next = 'difficultPart';
        else if (cur.id === 'difficultPart')
            next = 'end';
        else if (cur.id === 'problem')
            next = 'technicalProblem';
        else if (cur.id === 'cause' || cur.id === 'team')
            next = 'different';
        else if (cur.id === 'different')
            next = 'end';
        else if (cur.id === 'led')
            next = value === 'yes' ? 'teamSize' : 'responsibility';
        else if (cur.id === 'teamSize')
            next = 'disagree';
        else if (cur.id === 'disagree')
            next = value === 'yes' ? 'disagreeHow' : 'end';
        else if (cur.id === 'disagreeHow')
            next = 'end';
        else if (cur.id === 'responsibility')
            next = value === 'yes' ? 'responsibilityHow' : 'end';
        else if (cur.id === 'responsibilityHow')
            next = 'end';
        else if (cur.id === 'explain')
            next = 'clearExample';
        else if (cur.id === 'clearExample')
            next = value === 'yes' ? 'anotherExample' : 'methods';
        else if (cur.id === 'anotherExample' || cur.id === 'methods')
            next = 'end';
        else if (cur.id === 'finalProject')
            next = 'role';
        else if (cur.id === 'role')
            next = 'challenge';
        else if (cur.id === 'challenge')
            next = value === 'yes' ? 'overcome' : 'effort';
        else if (cur.id === 'overcome' || cur.id === 'effort')
            next = 'end';
        else if (cur.id === 'deadlines')
            next = 'planAhead';
        else if (cur.id === 'planAhead')
            next = value === 'yes' ? 'prioritize' : 'pressureExample';
        else if (cur.id === 'prioritize' || cur.id === 'pressureExample')
            next = 'end';
        it.answers.push({
            step: cur.id, answer: v
        });
        it.stepId = next;
        if (next === 'end')
            addInterviewAIMessage('Thank you. That section is complete. Click Finish to save this section, or go back to Categories to choose another area.');
        else
            addInterviewAIMessage(interviewStep(it.category, next).q);
    }
    /* ---------- AI conversation must stay in the chat box ---------- */
    chatPanel = function () {
        const msgs = state.chatMessages || [];
        const history = msgs.slice(-30).map(m => '<div class="chat-msg ' + m.who + '">' + escape(m.text) + '</div>').join('');
        return '<div class="panel"><span class="eyebrow">AI CONVERSATION</span><h2>Ask your coach anything.</h2><p class="muted">Every question and AI answer stays inside this conversation box.</p><div class="chat-stream" id="workspaceChatStream">' + history + '</div><div class="conversation-input"><input id="workspaceChat" placeholder="Ask PLACEMENT360 anything…"><button class="primary" data-action="workspaceSend">Send</button></div></div>';
    };
    /* ---------- Resume AI review ---------- */
    resumePanel = function () {
        const r = state.resumeReview;
        return '<div class="panel"><span class="eyebrow">AI RESUME REVIEW</span><h2>Upload your resume and get specific improvements.</h2><p class="muted">The reviewer checks your target role, projects, skills, evidence, measurable impact, links and ATS-friendly structure.</p><div class="mini-card" style="margin-top:14px"><b>Target context</b><p class="small">' + escape(state.branch || 'Branch pending') + ' · ' + escape(state.role || 'Target role pending') + ' · ' + escape(state.companyNoIdea ? 'Common placement patterns' : state.company || 'Company pending') + '</p></div><label class="secondary" style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-top:15px;cursor:pointer">Choose PDF / DOCX / TXT<input id="resumeInputFinal" type="file" accept=".pdf,.docx,.txt" style="display:none"></label>'
            + (r ? '<div class="diagnosis-note" style="margin-top:18px"><b>AI review for ' + escape(r.name) + '</b><p class="small">Extracted text: ' + r.chars + ' characters · ' + r.coverage + '</p><h3 style="margin:12px 0 7px">Add / strengthen</h3><ul style="margin:0;padding-left:19px">' + r.suggestions.map(s => '<li class="small" style="margin:6px 0">' + escape(s) + '</li>').join('') + '</ul><h3 style="margin:14px 0 7px">Detected strengths</h3><ul style="margin:0;padding-left:19px">' + r.strengths.map(s => '<li class="small" style="margin:6px 0">' + escape(s) + '</li>').join('') + '</ul></div>' : '<div class="diagnosis-note" style="margin-top:18px"><b>What the AI will check</b><p class="small">Summary · role keywords · technical skills · projects · quantified impact · links · certifications · education · formatting signals.</p></div>') + '</div>';
    };
    async function loadExternal(url, ready) {
        if (window[ready])
            return window[ready];
        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => { const s = document.createElement('script'); s.src = url; s.onload = () => resolve(window[ready]); s.onerror = reject; document.head.appendChild(s); });
    }
    async function extractResumeText(file) {
        const ext = (file.name.split('.').pop() || '').toLowerCase();
        if (ext === 'txt')
            return await file.text();
        if (ext === 'docx') {
            try {
                const mammoth = await loadExternal('https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/mammoth@1.8.0/mammoth.browser.min.js', 'mammoth');
                const buf = await file.arrayBuffer();
                const out = await mammoth.extractRawText({
                    arrayBuffer: buf
                });
                return out.value || '';
            }
            catch (e) {
                return '';
            }
        }
        if (ext === 'pdf') {
            try {
                const pdfjs = await loadExternal('https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.min.js', 'pdfjsLib');
                pdfjs.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js';
                const buf = await file.arrayBuffer();
                const pdf = await pdfjs.getDocument({
                    data: buf
                }).promise;
                let text = '';
                for (let i = 1; i <= pdf.numPages; i++) {
                    const page = await pdf.getPage(i);
                    const c = await page.getTextContent();
                    text += c.items.map(x => x.str).join(' ') + '\n';
                }
                return text;
            }
            catch (e) {
                return '';
            }
        }
        return '';
    }
    function buildResumeReview(name, text, file) {
        const t = (text || '').toLowerCase(), suggestions = [], strengths = [];
        const role = state.role || 'target role';
        const checks = [
            [
                /@/.test(text || ''), 'Contact email is present.', 'Add a professional email address if missing.'
            ],
            [
                /\d{10}/.test((text || '').replace(/\D/g, '')), 'Phone/contact number is visible.', 'Add a reachable phone number if it is missing.'
            ],
            [
                /summary|profile|objective/.test(t), 'A summary/profile section is visible.', 'Add a 2–3 line role-targeted professional summary.'
            ],
            [
                /project|built|developed|implemented/.test(t), 'Project evidence is visible.', 'Add 2–4 strong projects with problem, your action, tech stack and result.'
            ],
            [
                /%|increased|reduced|improved|saved|users|accuracy/.test(t), 'Some measurable impact language is present.', 'Quantify results wherever possible: %, time saved, users, accuracy, throughput or scale.'
            ],
            [
                /skills|technical skills|technologies/.test(t), 'A skills section is visible.', 'Create a clean technical-skills section aligned to ' + role + '.'
            ],
            [
                /github|linkedin|portfolio/.test(t), 'A professional link appears.', 'Add GitHub/LinkedIn/portfolio links and make sure they are clickable.'
            ],
            [
                /certif|course/.test(t), 'Certifications/courses are mentioned.', 'Add relevant certifications only when you can explain or prove them.'
            ],
            [
                new RegExp(role.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&'), 'i').test(text || ''), 'The target-role wording appears.', 'Use keywords from the target role “' + role + '” naturally in summary, skills and project bullets.'
            ]
        ];
        checks.forEach(c => c[0] ? strengths.push(c[1]) : suggestions.push(c[2]));
        if (!suggestions.length)
            suggestions.push('Tighten every bullet to action + technology + result and remove repeated or generic wording.');
        suggestions.push('Keep the most relevant projects, skills and evidence in the top half of the first page for quick recruiter scanning.');
        suggestions.push('For ' + role + ', tailor 6–10 keywords to the exact job description rather than copying a generic skill list.');
        return {
            name, chars: (text || '').length, coverage: (text ? 'Document text extracted for AI-style review.' : 'File uploaded; text extraction was limited, so review used available file metadata plus your placement context.'), suggestions, strengths
        };
    }
    async function reviewResumeFile(file) {
        showToast('Reading resume and preparing AI review…');
        const text = await extractResumeText(file);
        state.resumeReview = buildResumeReview(file.name, text, file);
        state.resumeUploaded = true;
        state.resumeName = file.name;
        p360Persist();
        state.view = 'resume';
        state.screen = 'app';
        render();
    }
    /* ---------- Firebase/auth UI ---------- */
    auth = function () {
        return topbar() + '<main class="auth-wrap"><div class="auth-card"><span class="eyebrow">' + (state.authMode === 'login' ? 'WELCOME BACK' : 'CREATE ACCOUNT') + '</span><h2>' + (state.authMode === 'login' ? 'Continue your placement journey.' : 'Create your Placement360 account.') + '</h2><p class="muted">Your account is backed by Firebase Authentication when Firebase is available.</p><div class="toggle"><button class="' + (state.authMode === 'login' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-action="loginMode">Login</button><button class="' + (state.authMode === 'signup' ? 'active' : '') + '" data-action="signupMode">Create account</button></div>'
            + (state.authMode === 'signup' ? '<label class="small">Full name</label><input id="name" placeholder="Your name" required>' : '')
            + '<label class="small">Email</label><input id="authEmail" type="email" placeholder="you@example.com" required><label class="small">Password</label><input id="authPass" type="password" placeholder="At least 6 characters" required><button class="primary" style="width:100%" data-action="authSubmit">' + (state.authMode === 'login' ? 'Login →' : 'Create account →') + '</button><div class="small" style="margin-top:14px">' + escape(P360_FIREBASE_STATUS) + ' · Account creation date controls weekly-assignment access.</div></div></main>';
    };
    /* ---------- View routing additions ---------- */
    const priorViewContentFinal = viewContent;
    viewContent = function () {
        studyInit();
        if (state.view === 'today')
            return todaysPlanAI();
        if (state.view === 'resume')
            return resumePanel();
        if (state.view === 'labSession')
            return labView();
        if (state.view === 'companyMock')
            return state.companyMock ? companyMockView() : companyMockResultView();
        if (state.view === 'interviewSession')
            return interviewSession();
        return priorViewContentFinal();
    };
    /* ---------- Final event binding ---------- */
    const priorBindFinal = bind;
    bind = function () {
        priorBindFinal();
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-action="selectInterviewCategory"]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => startInterviewCategory(b.dataset.interviewCategory || ''));
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-action="interviewChoice"]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { const v = b.dataset.interviewChoice || ''; if (!state.interview)
            return; addInterviewUserMessage(v === 'yes' ? 'Yes' : v === 'no' ? 'No' : v); advanceInterviewFromAnswer(v); p360Persist(); render(); });
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-action="finishInterviewCategory"]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => action('finishInterviewCategory'));
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-action="backToInterviewCategories"]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => action('backToInterviewCategories'));
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-action="back"]').forEach(b => b.onclick = backAction);
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-action="home"]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { if (state.authed) {
            state.screen = 'app';
            state.view = 'dashboard';
            render();
        }
        else {
            state.screen = 'landing';
            render();
        } });
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-action="signout"]').forEach(b => b.onclick = async () => { try {
            await P360_FB_AUTH?.signOut();
        }
        catch (e) { } stopStreams(); state.authed = false; state.firebaseUid = ''; state.userEmail = ''; state.screen = 'landing'; state.aiOpen = false; render(); });
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-study-lab]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.activeLab = b.dataset.studyLab || ''; state.activeStudyTopic = state.activeLab; state.view = 'labSession'; state.screen = 'app'; markStudySessionStart(); render(); });
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-study-open]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.activeStudyTopic = b.dataset.studyOpen || ''; state.view = 'studyTopic'; state.screen = 'app'; markStudySessionStart(); render(); });
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-study-review]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.activeStudyTopic = b.dataset.studyReview || ''; state.view = 'studyTopic'; state.screen = 'app'; markStudySessionStart(); render(); });
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-company-answer]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.companyMock.answers[state.companyMock.q] = Number(b.dataset.companyAnswer); render(); });
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-resume-input]').forEach(b => b.onchange = async () => { const f = b.files?.[0]; if (f)
            await reviewResumeFile(f); });
        const ri = root.querySelector('#resumeInputFinal');
        if (ri)
            ri.onchange = async () => { const f = ri.files?.[0]; if (f)
                await reviewResumeFile(f); };
        const wi = root.querySelector('#workspaceChat');
        if (wi)
            wi.onkeydown = e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                e.preventDefault();
                action('workspaceSend');
            } };
        const ii = root.querySelector('#interviewInput');
        if (ii)
            ii.onkeydown = e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                e.preventDefault();
                action('submitInterview');
            } };
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-lab-answer]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { state.labAnswer = Number(b.dataset.labAnswer); render(); });
        if (state.view === 'labSession')
            markStudySessionStart();
        if (state.view === 'studyTopic')
            markStudySessionStart();
    };
    /* ---------- Final actions ---------- */
    const priorActionFinal = action;
    action = function (a) {
        if (a === 'back') {
            backAction();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'home') {
            if (state.authed) {
                state.screen = 'app';
                state.view = 'dashboard';
            }
            else
                state.screen = 'landing';
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'signout') {
            Promise.resolve(P360_FB_AUTH?.signOut()).catch(() => { }).finally(() => { stopStreams(); state.authed = false; state.firebaseUid = ''; state.userEmail = ''; state.screen = 'landing'; state.aiOpen = false; render(); });
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'authSubmit') {
            const email = (document.getElementById('authEmail')?.value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
            const pass = document.getElementById('authPass')?.value || '';
            const name = (document.getElementById('name')?.value || '').trim();
            if (!email || !pass) {
                showToast('Enter email and password.');
                return;
            }
            if (pass.length < 6) {
                showToast('Password must be at least 6 characters.');
                return;
            }
            (async () => {
                const res = await firebaseAuth(state.authMode, email, pass, name);
                if (res.ok) {
                    state.authed = true;
                    state.userEmail = email;
                    state.studentName = state.studentName || (res.user.displayName || name || email.split('@')[0] || 'Student');
                    if (state.authMode === 'signup') {
                        state.accountCreatedAt = Date.now();
                        state.diagnosis = null;
                        state.diagnosticStarted = false;
                        state.step = 0;
                        state.branch = '';
                        state.role = '';
                        state.company = '';
                        state.companyNoIdea = false;
                        state.convo = [
                            {
                                who: 'ai', html: 'Welcome ' + escape(state.studentName) + '! 👋 First question: what branch are you studying?'
                            }
                        ];
                        state.view = 'dashboard';
                        state.screen = 'welcome';
                    }
                    else {
                        state.accountCreatedAt = state.accountCreatedAt || Date.now();
                        state.screen = state.diagnosis ? 'app' : 'welcome';
                        state.view = 'dashboard';
                    }
                    p360Persist();
                    render();
                    return;
                }
                if (!res.unavailable) {
                    showToast(res.error?.message || 'Firebase authentication failed. Check your email/password.');
                    return;
                }
                /* Local fallback only when Firebase SDK cannot load. */
                if (state.authMode === 'signup') {
                    if (p360LoadUser(email)) {
                        showToast('An account already exists. Please use Login.');
                        return;
                    }
                    state.userEmail = email;
                    state.studentName = name || email.split('@')[0];
                    state.accountCreatedAt = Date.now();
                    state.authed = true;
                    state.diagnosis = null;
                    state.step = 0;
                    state.convo = [
                        {
                            who: 'ai', html: 'Welcome ' + escape(state.studentName) + '! 👋 First question: what branch are you studying?'
                        }
                    ];
                    state.screen = 'welcome';
                    p360Persist();
                    render();
                }
                else {
                    const u = p360LoadUser(email);
                    if (!u) {
                        showToast('No account found in this browser.');
                        return;
                    }
                    p360ApplyUser(u);
                    state.authed = true;
                    state.userEmail = email;
                    state.accountCreatedAt = Number(u.accountCreatedAt || Date.now());
                    state.screen = state.diagnosis ? 'app' : 'welcome';
                    state.view = 'dashboard';
                    render();
                }
            })();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'workspaceSend') {
            const inp = document.getElementById('workspaceChat');
            const v = (inp?.value || '').trim();
            if (!v)
                return;
            state.chatMessages = state.chatMessages || [];
            state.chatMessages.push({
                who: 'user', text: v
            });
            state.chatMessages.push({
                who: 'ai', text: aiAnswer(v)
            });
            state.view = 'chat';
            state.screen = 'app';
            p360Persist();

            requestAnimationFrame(() => {

                if (stream)
                    stream.scrollTop = stream.scrollHeight;
                const next = document.getElementById('workspaceChat');
                if (next)
                    next.focus();
            });
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'completeToday') {
            showToast('Today’s status is AI-recorded from learning activity. Continue studying instead of manually marking attendance.');
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'markAbsent' || a === 'markAttendance') {
            showToast('Manual attendance controls have been removed. PLACEMENT360 records attendance from verified learning activity.');
            return;

            state.view = 'labs';
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'companyMock') {
            state.companyMock = {
                q: 0, answers: {}, code: '', startedAt: Date.now()

            state.view = 'companyMock';
            state.screen = 'app';
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'companyMockBack') {
            if (state.companyMock.q > 0) {
                state.companyMock.q--;
                render();

            else {
                state.companyMock = null;
                state.view = 'assessment';
                render();
            }
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'companyMockNext') {
            const m = state.companyMock;
            if (!m)

            const q = COMPANY_MOCK_BANK[m.q];
            if (q.type === 'coding') {
                m.code = document.getElementById('companyCode')?.value || m.code;
                if (!m.code || m.code.length < 40) {
                    showToast('Complete the coding solution first.');
                    return;
                }
            }
            if (q.type === 'text') {
                m.answers[m.q] = document.getElementById('companyText')?.value || '';
                if (String(m.answers[m.q]).trim().length < 20) {
                    showToast('Give a complete interview-style answer.');
                    return;

            }
            if (m.q >= COMPANY_MOCK_BANK.length - 1) {
                finishCompanyMock();
                return;
            }
            m.q++;
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'runCompanyCode') {
            const code = document.getElementById('companyCode')?.value || '';
            state.companyMock.code = code;
            const el = document.getElementById('companyCodeResult');
            if (el)
                el.textContent = (code.length > 60 && !/pass|write your solution|TODO/i.test(code)) ? 'Preview check passed.' : 'The solution still looks incomplete.';
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'submitLab') {
            const l = labForTopic(state.activeLab || state.activeStudyTopic);
            if (l.mcq) {
                const ok = state.labAnswer === l.ans;
                state.labResult = ok ? 'Correct. The concept check is recorded as practice evidence.' : 'Not quite. Revisit the material and retry.';
                if (ok) {
                    state.labDone[studyKey(state.activeLab || state.activeStudyTopic)] = true;
                    markVerifiedStudy('lab completion');
                }
                render();
            }
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'runLab') {
            const box = document.getElementById('labCode'), r = document.getElementById('labCodeResult');
            const code = (box?.value || '').trim();
            const incomplete = !code || /pass|write your solution|TODO|implement here/i.test(code) || code.length < 35;
            if (incomplete) {
                if (r) {
                    r.textContent = 'Preview: solution still looks incomplete. Replace the placeholder and run again.';
                    r.style.color = 'var(--danger)';
                }
                return;
            }
            state.labCode = code;
            if (r) {
                r.textContent = 'Preview passed. Your solution is captured for the AI lab evaluator; complete a correct run to mark the lab done.';
                r.style.color = 'var(--good)';
            }
            state.labDone[studyKey(state.activeLab || state.activeStudyTopic)] = true;
            markVerifiedStudy('lab completion');
            p360Persist();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'resetLab') {
            const l = labForTopic(state.activeLab || state.activeStudyTopic);
            const box = document.getElementById('labCode'), r = document.getElementById('labCodeResult');
            if (box)
                box.value = l.starter;
            if (r)
                r.textContent = 'Starter restored.';

        if (a === 'startInterview') {
            state.interviewResults = state.interviewResults || {};
            state.interview = null;
            state.view = 'interview';
            state.screen = 'app';
            render();

                return;

            render();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'submitInterview') {
            const input = document.getElementById('interviewInput');
            const v = (input?.value || '').trim();
            if (!v) {
                showToast('Answer the interviewer first.');
                return;
            }
            const score = evaluateInterview(v);
            addInterviewUserMessage(v);
            addInterviewAIMessage('Feedback ' + score.score + '/100 — ' + score.feedback);
            state.interview.scores.push(score.score);
            advanceInterviewFromAnswer(v);
            p360Persist();
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'finishInterviewCategory') {
            const it = state.interview;
            if (!it)
                return;
            const f = interviewFlow(it.category), avg = it.scores.length ? Math.round(it.scores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / it.scores.length) : 0;
            state.interviewResults = state.interviewResults || {};
            state.interviewResults[it.category] = {
                title: f.title, score: avg, answers: it.answers || [], completedAt: Date.now()
            };
            markVerifiedStudy('interview ' + f.title + ' completion');
            p360Persist();
            state.interview = null;
            state.view = 'interview';
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'backToInterviewCategories') {
            state.interview = null;
            state.view = 'interview';
            render();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'interviewVoice') {
            const Recognition = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
            if (!Recognition) {
                showToast('Voice input is not available in this browser. Type your answer instead.');
                return;
            }
            const rec = new Recognition();
            rec.lang = 'en-IN';
            rec.interimResults = false;
            rec.maxAlternatives = 1;
            const status = document.getElementById('interviewVoiceStatus');
            if (status)
                status.textContent = 'Listening…';
            rec.onresult = e => { const text = e.results?.[0]?.[0]?.transcript || ''; const inp = document.getElementById('interviewInput'); if (inp)
                inp.value = text; if (status)
                status.textContent = 'Voice answer captured. Press Send.'; };
            rec.onerror = () => { if (status)
                status.textContent = 'Voice capture failed. Type your answer instead.'; };
            rec.start();
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'startWeeklyAssessment' && !isSunday()) {
            showToast('The weekly exam is available only on Sunday. Next availability: ' + nextSundayText());
            return;
        }
        if (a === 'completeWeekly' && !weeklyAssignmentUnlocked()) {
            showToast('Weekly assignment unlocks after 6 full days from account creation.');
            return;
        }
        priorActionFinal(a);
    };
    /* ---------- final render additions ---------- */
    const priorRenderFinal = render;

        priorRenderFinal();
        if (state.screen === 'app') {
            try {
                studyInit();
                addStudyStyles();
            }
            catch (e) { }
        }
        if (state.view === 'studyTopic' || state.view === 'studyMaterials' || state.view === 'labs' || state.view === 'labSession')
            markStudySessionStart();
    };
    /* ---------- Final persistence + dashboard enrichment ---------- */
    const priorApplyUserFinal = p360ApplyUser;
    p360ApplyUser = function (u) {
        priorApplyUserFinal(u);
        state.accountCreatedAt = Number(u.accountCreatedAt || state.accountCreatedAt || Date.now());
        state.diagnosticProgressAwarded = !!(u.diagnosticProgressAwarded ?? state.diagnosticProgressAwarded);
        state.diagnosticPreparationLevel = u.diagnosticPreparationLevel || state.diagnosticPreparationLevel || '';
        state.resumeReview = u.resumeReview || state.resumeReview || null;
        state.companyMockResult = u.companyMockResult || state.companyMockResult || null;
        state.studyEngagement = u.studyEngagement || state.studyEngagement || {};
        state.skillEvidence = Number(u.skillEvidence || state.skillEvidence || 0);
        state.weeklyDone = !!(u.weeklyDone ?? state.weeklyDone);
        state.todayDone = !!(u.todayDone ?? state.todayDone);
    };
    const currentPersistFinal = p360Persist;
    p360Persist = function () {
        studyInit();
        if (!state.accountCreatedAt && state.userEmail)
            state.accountCreatedAt = Date.now();
        currentPersistFinal();
        if (state.userEmail) {
            try {
                const key = p360UserKey(state.userEmail), old = p360LoadUser(state.userEmail) || {};
                localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify({
                    ...old,
                    email: state.userEmail, studentName: state.studentName || old.studentName || '', accountCreatedAt: state.accountCreatedAt,
                    branch: state.branch || '', role: state.role || '', company: state.company || '', companyNoIdea: !!state.companyNoIdea,
                    diagnosis: state.diagnosis || null, weeklyDone: !!state.weeklyDone, weeklyAssessmentDone: !!state.weeklyAssessmentDone,
                    todayDone: !!state.todayDone, todayCarry: !!state.todayCarry, studyEngagement: state.studyEngagement || {},
                    studyAttendance: state.studyAttendance || {}, resumeReview: state.resumeReview || null, companyMockResult: state.companyMockResult || null,
                    diagnosticProgressAwarded: !!state.diagnosticProgressAwarded, diagnosticPreparationLevel: state.diagnosticPreparationLevel || '', interviewResults: state.interviewResults || {},
                    skillEvidence: Number(state.skillEvidence || 0)
                }));
            }
            catch (e) { }
        }
        queueFirebaseSave();
    };
    studyDashboardHome = function () {
        studyInit();
        const all = studyCatalog().flatMap(c => c[1]);
        const done = all.filter(t => state.studyProgress[studyKey(t)] === 100).length;
        const next = studyNext();
        const mock = state.companyMockResult;
        const eng = state.studyEngagement?.[todayKey()] || {
            minutes: 0, verified: false
        };
        return '<div class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><span class="eyebrow">AI PLACEMENT DASHBOARD</span><h2>Here is what the AI wants you to do next.</h2><p class="muted">The dashboard updates from diagnostic results, study engagement, labs, weekly exams, assignments and company mock performance.</p></div><button class="primary" data-study-open="' + escapeAttr(next) + '">Continue: ' + escape(next) + ' →</button></div><div class="learning-stat-grid"><div class="mini-card"><span class="small">Learning path</span><div class="metric-big">' + done + '/' + all.length + '</div><span class="small">topics completed</span></div><div class="mini-card"><span class="small">Today’s engagement</span><div class="metric-big">' + Math.round(eng.minutes || 0) + 'm</div><span class="small">' + (eng.verified ? 'AI verified' : 'not yet verified') + '</span></div><div class="mini-card"><span class="small">Skill evidence</span><div class="metric-big">' + Number(state.skillEvidence || 0) + '%</div><span class="small">latest verified score</span></div><div class="mini-card"><span class="small">AI attendance</span><div class="metric-big">' + Object.values(state.studyAttendance || {}).filter(Boolean).length + '/7</div><span class="small">active days</span></div></div></div>'
            + '<div class="learning-grid"><div class="panel"><span class="eyebrow">TODAY</span><h3>' + escape(next) + '</h3><p class="muted">Study the material → solve the lab → build evidence.
