            + '<div class="panel"><span class="eyebrow">COMPANY MOCK</span><h3>' + (mock ? (mock.score + '% · ' + escape(mock.band)) : 'Not attempted yet') + '</h3><p class="small">' + (mock ? ('Strengths: ' + (mock.strengths || []).join(', ') + '. Gaps: ' + (mock.gaps || []).join(', ') + '.') : 'Take the current-pattern company mock to update this dashboard.') + '</p><button class="secondary" data-action="companyMock">' + (mock ? 'Retry company mock' : 'Start company mock') + '</button></div></div>'
            + '<div class="panel"><span class="eyebrow">THIS WEEK</span><div class="cards"><div class="mini-card"><b>Weekly assignment</b><p class="small">' + (weeklyAssignmentUnlocked() ? (state.weeklyDone ? 'AI-verified completed' : 'Available') : 'Unlocks after 6 full days') + '</p></div><div class="mini-card"><b>Sunday exam</b><p class="small">' + (isSunday() ? 'Available today' : 'Unavailable until Sunday · ' + nextSundayText()) + '</p></div></div></div>';
    };
    /* ---------- Hide obsolete manual controls / profile hooks from accidental old routes ---------- */
    const priorBaseViewForRemoval = viewContent;
    /* Make sure today/attendance/weekly routes above cannot be replaced by older wrappers. */
    /* ---------- Detailed diagnostic marking: real attempt-aware scoring ---------- */
    function diagnosticBlankCode(code) {
        const c = String(code || '').replace(/\/\/.*$/gm, '').replace(/#.*$/gm, '').trim();
        if (!c)
            return true;
        const stripped = c.replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
        return /pass\s*$/.test(stripped) || /TODO|implement your solution|write your solution here|your solution here|implement here/i.test(stripped) && !/(return|for\s*\(|while\s*\(|if\s*\(|System\.out|print\s*\()/i.test(stripped);
    }
    function evaluateDiagnosticCode(code) {
        const c = String(code || '').trim();
        if (!c)
            return {
                marks: 0, max: 45, status: 'Not attempted', remark: 'No practical response was submitted.', percent: 0, attempted: false
            };
        const lower = c.toLowerCase();
        const hasLogic = /(if|else|for|while|function|def |class |switch|case|return|=>|algorithm|step)/i.test(c);
        const hasInput = /(input|read|scanf|prompt|parameter|argument|accepts)/i.test(c);
        const hasOutput = /(print|output|display|console|return|result)/i.test(c);
        const hasValidation = /(validat|check|error|invalid|<=|>=|<=0|<0)/i.test(c);
        let marks = 12;
        const notes = [];
        if (hasLogic)
            marks += 12;
        else
            notes.push('Add clear processing logic or algorithm steps.');
        if (hasInput)
            marks += 7;
        else
            notes.push('Show how the required inputs are received.');
        if (hasOutput)
            marks += 7;
        else
            notes.push('Show a clear result or output.');
        if (hasValidation)
            marks += 4;
        else
            notes.push('Include a basic input/edge-case validation.');
        marks = Math.min(45, marks);
        const status = marks >= 40 ? 'Strong attempt' : marks >= 28 ? 'Good attempt' : marks >= 18 ? 'Partial attempt' : 'Limited attempt';
        return {
            marks, max: 45, status, remark: notes.slice(0, 3).join(' ') || 'The response contains the main practical structure and can be improved with testing.', percent: Math.round(marks / 45 * 100), attempted: true
        };
    }
    function recordDiagnosticResponse() {
        const a = state.assessment, qs = currentQuestions(), q = qs[a.q];
        if (!q)
            return;
        if (q.type === 'practical') {
            const editor = document.getElementById('codeBox');
            if (editor) {
                a.answers[a.q] = {
                    type: 'code', code: editor.value || '', attempted: !!(editor.value || '').trim()
                };
            }
            else {
                a.answers[a.q] = {
                    type: 'practicalChoice', attempted: !!a.practicalDone, correct: !!a.practicalDone
                };
            }
        }
        else if (q.type === 'voice') {
            a.answers[a.q] = {
                type: 'voice', attempted: !!a.voiceScore, score: Number(a.voiceScore || 0)
            };
        }
    }
    function evaluateDiagnosticDetailed() {
        const a = state.assessment || {}, qs = diagQs;
        const items = [];
        let total = 0, maxTotal = 0;
        qs.forEach((q, i) => {
            const max = q.type === 'mcq' ? 5 : q.type === 'voice' ? 10 : 45;
            maxTotal += max;
            let marks = 0, status = 'Not attempted', remark = 'Question was not attempted.';
            if (q.type === 'mcq') {
                const ans = a.answers?.[i];
                if (ans === undefined || ans === null) {
                    marks = 0;
                    status = 'Not attempted';
                    remark = 'No option was selected.';
                }
                else if (Number(ans) === Number(q.ans)) {
                    marks = max;
                    status = 'Correct';
                    remark = 'Correct answer.';
                }
                else {
                    marks = 0;
                    status = 'Incorrect';
                    remark = 'Answer selected was incorrect.';
                }
            }
            else if (q.type === 'voice') {
                const v = a.answers?.[i];
                if (v?.attempted || a.voiceScore) {
                    const raw = Number(v?.score || a.voiceScore || 0);
                    marks = raw >= 2 ? 7 : raw > 0 ? 4 : 0;
                    status = marks ? 'Attempted' : 'Not attempted';
                    remark = marks ? `Spoken response captured. ${marks < 10 ? 'Some marks are reduced because the response quality was only partially demonstrated.' : 'Good response quality.'}` : 'No spoken response was captured.';
                }
            }
            else {
                const v = a.answers?.[i];
                /* Practical marks can NEVER be awarded merely because the student clicked a
                   completion control. The submitted code is the only evidence for this item. */
                if (v?.type === 'code') {
                    const r = evaluateDiagnosticCode(v.code);
                    marks = r.marks;
                    status = r.status;
                    remark = r.remark;
                }
                else {
                    marks = 0;
                    status = 'Not attempted';
                    remark = 'No code was submitted. Practical evaluation is 0 and no diagnostic progress is awarded.';
                }
            }
            total += marks;
            items.push({
                i: i + 1, question: q.q, type: q.type, skill: q.skill, level: q.level, marks, max, status, remark
            });
        });
        const score = Math.round(total / maxTotal * 100);
        const commRaw = items[0].marks + items[1].marks, comm = Math.round(commRaw / 20 * 100);
        const aptitudeRaw = items[2].marks + items[3].marks, aptitude = Math.round(aptitudeRaw / 10 * 100);
        const coreRaw = items[4].marks + items[5].marks + items[6].marks, core = Math.round(coreRaw / 15 * 100);
        const fund = core;
        const problem = Math.round((aptitude + core) / 2);
        const practical = items[7].max ? Math.round(items[7].marks / items[7].max * 100) : 0;
        const foundationStart = items[7].marks === 0;
        /* Practical evidence is a progression gate. If no code was written, the student
           starts again from foundational preparation even when some MCQs were correct. */
        const level = foundationStart ? 'Needs Foundation' : (score >= 85 ? 'Placement Ready' : score >= 70 ? 'Foundation Strong' : score >= 50 ? 'Developing' : 'Needs Foundation');
        const summary = foundationStart
            ? 'No practical code was submitted. Practical evaluation is 0, no diagnostic learning progress is awarded, and preparation starts from the basic foundation.'
            : (score >= 85 ? 'Strong overall performance with only targeted polishing needed.' : score >= 70 ? 'Good foundation, with some areas that need focused improvement.' : score >= 50 ? 'You have a starting foundation, but several skills need more practice and evidence.' : 'The result shows that core preparation needs to be strengthened before placement-level readiness.');
        return {
            total, maxTotal, score, items, level, summary, fund, problem, comm, practical, foundationStart, diagnosticProgressAwarded: false,
            fundLabel: fund >= 80 ? 'Strong' : fund >= 60 ? 'Developing' : 'Needs reinforcement',
            problemLabel: problem >= 80 ? 'Strong' : problem >= 60 ? 'Developing well' : 'Priority focus',
            commLabel: comm >= 80 ? 'Clear and structured' : comm >= 60 ? 'Developing' : 'Needs speaking practice',
            practicalLabel: foundationStart ? '0% — code not submitted' : (practical >= 80 ? 'Strong practical attempt' : practical > 0 ? 'Partial practical attempt' : 'Not attempted'),
            evaluatedAt: Date.now()
        };
    }
    processAssessment = function () { return evaluateDiagnosticDetailed(); };
    const oldDetailedDiagnosisReport = diagnosisReport;
    diagnosisReport = function () {
        const d = state.diagnosis || evaluateDiagnosticDetailed();
        const rows = (d.items || []).map(x => `<tr><td><b>Q${x.i}</b><br><span class="small">${escape(x.skill)} · ${escape(x.level)}</span></td><td>${escape(x.type === 'mcq' ? 'MCQ' : x.type === 'voice' ? 'Communication' : 'Coding / Practical')}</td><td><b>${x.marks}/${x.max}</b></td><td><span class="tag ${x.marks === 0 ? 'warn' : ''}">${escape(x.status)}</span></td><td class="small">${escape(x.remark)}</td></tr>`).join('');
        const codeItem = (d.items || []).find(x => x.type === 'practical');
        const gateNote = d.foundationStart
            ? '<div class="diagnosis-note" style="margin-top:16px;background:#fff4f2;border-color:#f2d5cf"><b>Foundation gate activated:</b> the practical section received <b>0/45</b> because no practical response was submitted. The learning path will start from the basic preparation level. Diagnostic completion does not award study-topic progress.</div>'
            : '<div class="diagnosis-note" style="margin-top:16px"><b>Practical progression:</b> practical progress is based on submitted code evidence, not on clicking through the question.</div>';
        return `${topbar()}<main class="report-wrap"><section class="report-card"><span class="eyebrow">YOUR DIAGNOSIS REPORT</span><h2 style="font-size:38px;letter-spacing:-.05em;margin:12px 0 6px">Original evaluation with marks for every question.</h2><p class="muted">The score is based on what you actually submitted. Not attempted questions receive <b>0 marks</b>. Wrong answers receive <b>0</b> for that MCQ. Practical evaluation receives <b>0</b> when no response is submitted, and the preparation path then starts from the basic foundation.</p>${gateNote}<div class="report-top"><div class="evaluation"><span class="eyebrow">OVERALL SCORE</span><div class="level">${d.score}/${d.maxTotal}</div><p class="muted"><b>${escape(d.level)}</b> — ${escape(d.summary)}</p><div class="eval-grid"><div class="eval-box"><b>Fundamentals</b><div class="skillbar"><i style="width:${d.fund}%"></i></div><span class="small">${d.fundLabel}</span></div><div class="eval-box"><b>Problem solving</b><div class="skillbar"><i style="width:${d.problem}%"></i></div><span class="small">${d.problemLabel}</span></div><div class="eval-box"><b>Communication</b><div class="skillbar"><i style="width:${d.comm}%"></i></div><span class="small">${d.commLabel}</span></div><div class="eval-box"><b>Practical / Coding</b><div class="skillbar"><i style="width:${d.practical}%"></i></div><span class="small">${d.practicalLabel}</span></div></div></div><div><div class="panel" style="margin:0"><b>Marking rules</b><div style="margin-top:12px"><span class="tag">Correct → full marks</span><p class="small">A correct objective answer receives the full marks assigned to that question.</p><span class="tag warn">Not attempted → 0</span><p class="small">Skipping a question or leaving the coding area blank gives zero marks.</p><span class="tag warn">Mistakes → partial / lost marks</span><p class="small">Coding is checked for structure, logic, result handling and problem-solving patterns. Incomplete parts reduce the mark instead of giving full credit.</p></div></div><div class="diagnosis-note"><b>Coding result:</b> ${escape(codeItem?.marks + ' / ' + codeItem?.max || '0 / 45')} — ${escape(codeItem?.status || 'Not attempted')}. ${escape(codeItem?.remark || 'No code submitted.')}</div></div></div><div class="panel" style="margin-top:18px"><div class="panel-head"><div><span class="eyebrow">QUESTION-BY-QUESTION EVALUATION</span><h3>Exactly where marks were gained or lost</h3></div><span class="tag">${d.score}%</span></div><div style="overflow:auto"><table class="table"><tr><th>Question</th><th>Type</th><th>Marks</th><th>Result</th><th>Evaluation</th></tr>${rows}</table></div></div><div class="improve"><h3>What should you improve?</h3><p class="muted">The AI uses the lost marks to choose the next study topics, labs and reassessment areas. ${d.practical < 100 ? 'For the practical task, submit the full solution, pseudocode or algorithm rather than leaving placeholders.' : 'Your practical attempt is strong; focus next on the remaining weaker sections.'}</p><button class="primary" data-action="openPrep">Continue to your AI preparation →</button></div></section></main>${shellFloat()}`;
    };
    const previousDiagnosticAction = action;
    action = function (a) {
        if (a === 'nextAssessment' && state.assessment?.mode === 'diagnostic')
            recordDiagnosticResponse();
        previousDiagnosticAction(a);
    };
    const previousFinishAssessment = finishAssessment;
    finishAssessment = function () {
        recordDiagnosticResponse();
        stopStreams();
        state.screen = 'loading';
        render();
        setTimeout(() => {
            state.diagnosis = evaluateDiagnosticDetailed();
            state.diagnosticProgressAwarded = false;
            if (state.diagnosis.foundationStart) {
                state.diagnosticPreparationLevel = 'foundation';
                /* Diagnostic itself never completes a study topic. */
            }
            else {
                state.diagnosticPreparationLevel = 'targeted';
            }
            p360Persist?.();
            state.screen = 'report';
            render();
        }, 120);
    };
    /* ---------- Final Firebase authentication bridge ---------- */
    const p360ActionBeforeFirebaseBridge = action;
    action = async function (a) {
        if (a === 'authSubmit') {
            const email = (document.getElementById('authEmail')?.value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
            const pass = document.getElementById('authPass')?.value || '';
            const name = (document.getElementById('name')?.value || '').trim();
            if (!email || !pass) {
                showToast('Enter your email and password.');
                return;
            }
            if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) {
                showToast('Enter a valid email address.');
                return;
            }
            if (pass.length < 6) {
                showToast('Password must be at least 6 characters.');
                return;
            }
            if (state.authMode === 'signup' && !name) {
                showToast('Please enter your name first.');
                return;
            }
            const btn = root.querySelector('[data-action="authSubmit"]');
            if (btn) {
                btn.disabled = true;
                btn.textContent = state.authMode === 'signup' ? 'Creating account…' : 'Signing in…';
            }
            const res = await firebaseAuth(state.authMode, email, pass, name);
            if (res.ok) {
                state.authed = true;
                state.userEmail = email;
                state.studentName = state.studentName || (res.user?.displayName || name || email.split('@')[0] || 'Student');
                if (state.authMode === 'signup') {
                    state.accountCreatedAt = Date.now();
                    state.diagnosis = null;
                    state.diagnosticStarted = false;
                    state.assessmentLockUntil = 0;
                    state.step = 0;
                    state.branch = '';
                    state.role = '';
                    state.company = '';
                    state.companyNoIdea = false;
                    state.convo = [
                        {
                            who: 'ai', html: 'Welcome <b>' + escape(state.studentName) + '</b>! 👋 First question: what branch are you studying?'
                        }
                    ];
                    state.view = 'dashboard';
                    state.screen = 'welcome';
                }
                else {
                    state.accountCreatedAt = Number(state.accountCreatedAt || Date.now());
                    state.screen = state.diagnosis ? 'app' : 'welcome';
                    state.view = 'dashboard';
                }
                p360Persist();
                render();
                return;
            }
            let msg = 'Firebase authentication failed. Please try again.';
            const code = res.error?.code || '';
            if (code === 'auth/email-already-in-use')
                msg = 'This email is already registered. Please use Login.';
            else if (code === 'auth/invalid-credential' || code === 'auth/wrong-password' || code === 'auth/user-not-found')
                msg = 'Incorrect email or password.';
            else if (code === 'auth/invalid-email')
                msg = 'Enter a valid email address.';
            else if (code === 'auth/weak-password')
                msg = 'Use a password with at least 6 characters.';
            else if (code === 'auth/operation-not-allowed')
                msg = 'Firebase Email/Password sign-in is disabled. Enable Email/Password in Firebase Authentication → Sign-in method.';
            else if (code === 'auth/network-request-failed')
                msg = 'Firebase could not reach the authentication service. Check your internet connection and open the site from a web server (not file://).';
            else if (code === 'auth/unauthorized-domain')
                msg = 'This website domain is not authorized in Firebase Authentication. Add the domain under Firebase Authentication → Settings → Authorized domains.';
            else if (res.unavailable)
                msg = 'Firebase Authentication is not available because the Firebase SDK/config could not initialize.';
            showToast(msg);
            if (btn) {
                btn.disabled = false;
                btn.textContent = state.authMode === 'signup' ? 'Create account →' : 'Login →';
            }
            return;
        }
        return p360ActionBeforeFirebaseBridge(a);
    };
    /* AI is intentionally available only after sign-in. */
    shellFloat = function () {
        if (!state.authed)
            return '';
        return `<div class="ai-float ${state.aiOpen ? 'open' : ''}" id="aiFloat"><button class="ai-mini" data-action="toggleAI" aria-label="Open PLACEMENT360 AI">${mascot}</button><div class="ai-pop" style="padding:12px"><div class="title">✦ PLACEMENT360 AI</div><div class="small">Your coach stays here. No page change.</div><div class="ai-input"><input id="aiInput" placeholder="Message your coach…"><button data-action="sendAI">Send</button></div></div></div>`;
    };
    /* ---------- Initial account hydration from Firebase only on explicit login ---------- */
    try {
        clearLegacyAccountsOnce();
    }
    catch (e) { }
    p360Persist();
})();
</script>


<script id="p360AllSubjectNotesPatch">
// ========================================================================
// PLACEMENT360 MODULE: SCREEN-LOCK-ONLY EXAM MODE
// ========================================================================

(function () {
    const oldStudyTopicDetail = window.studyTopicDetail;
    function topicModule(route, topic) {
        if (!route || !Array.isArray(route.modules))
            return 'Learning Module';
        const found = route.modules.find(m => Array.isArray(m[1]) && m[1].some(x => String(x).toLowerCase() === String(topic).toLowerCase()));
        return found ? found[0] : 'Learning Module';
    }
    function uniq(arr) { return [
        ...new Set(arr.filter(Boolean))
    ]; }
    function richMaterial(topic, route) {
        const base = (typeof window.jobTopicGuide === 'function' && route)
            ? window.jobTopicGuide(topic, route)
            : (typeof window.materialContent === 'function' ? window.materialContent(topic) : null);
        const c = base || {
            summary: topic + ' is an important skill for ' + (route?.job || 'your target role') + '.',
            points: [],
            example: 'Start with a small ' + topic + ' example, identify the input and output, then verify each step.',
            practice: 'Solve one beginner problem and one job-related practical problem on ' + topic + '.'
        };
        const pts = Array.isArray(c.points) ? c.points : [];
        const job = route?.job || state.role || 'your target role';
        const moduleName = topicModule(route, topic);
        const applications = [
            'Use ' + topic + ' while building or explaining a small ' + job + ' project.',
            'Apply it to placement-style problems and practical tasks rather than only memorising definitions.',
            'Connect the topic with the tools and subjects listed elsewhere in your ' + job + ' learning path.',
            'Be ready to explain when you would choose this approach and what trade-offs it has.'
        ];
        const mistakes = [
            'Starting implementation without first identifying the inputs, outputs and constraints.',
            'Memorising syntax without understanding why the technique is used.',
            'Ignoring invalid input, edge cases or unusual real-world conditions.',
            'Being unable to explain the solution clearly after writing it.'
        ];
        const interviewQs = [
            'What is ' + topic + ' and why is it important for a ' + job + '?',
            'Explain one simple example of ' + topic + ' in your own words.',
            'When would you choose ' + topic + ' instead of another approach?',
            'What are the most common mistakes beginners make in ' + topic + '?',
            'How would you test or verify a solution involving ' + topic + '?'
        ];
        const revision = uniq([
            'Definition: ' + topic,
            ...pts.slice(0, 3),
            'Remember the worked example and be able to reproduce its main idea without notes.',
            'Practise the task once without looking at the solution.',
            'Explain one real job use case aloud.'
        ]).slice(0, 7);
        return {
            summary: c.summary, points: pts.length ? pts : [
                'Learn the definition, purpose and scope of ' + topic + '.',
                'Identify the core terms, steps, rules or formulas involved.',
                'Work through a simple example before attempting a placement-level problem.',
                'Understand common errors and how to test the solution.'
            ], example: c.example || 'Work through one small example step by step.', practice: c.practice || 'Solve one small problem and one job-related task.', applications, mistakes, interviewQs, revision, job, moduleName
        };
    }
    function bullet(items) {
        return '<ul style="margin:10px 0 0;padding-left:20px">' + items.map(x => '<li class="small" style="margin:7px 0">' + escape(String(x)) + '</li>').join('') + '</ul>';
    }
    function callout(title, text, klass) {
        return '<div class="panel" style="margin-top:14px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbf9ff,#f1f9ff)"><b>' + escape(title) + '</b><p class="muted" style="margin:8px 0 0;line-height:1.6">' + escape(text) + '</p></div>';
    }
    window.studyTopicDetail = function (t) {
        studyInit();
        state.activeStudyTopic = t;
        if (typeof markStudySessionStart === 'function')
            markStudySessionStart();
        // Keep the complete embedded Python material exactly as already provided.
        if (String(t).toLowerCase() === 'python fundamentals' && typeof pythonCompleteNotes !== 'undefined') {
            return oldStudyTopicDetail ? oldStudyTopicDetail(t) : '';
        }
        const route = typeof activeCareerRoute === 'function' ? activeCareerRoute() : null;
        const c = richMaterial(t, route);
        const job = route?.job || state.role || 'your target role';
        const mod = c.moduleName;
        const topicEsc = escape(t), jobEsc = escape(job);
        return '<div class="panel study-detail">'
            + '<span class="eyebrow">COMPLETE STUDY MATERIAL</span>'
            + '<h2>' + topicEsc + '</h2>'
            + '<p class="muted">Module: <b>' + escape(mod) + '</b> · Target job: <b>' + jobEsc + '</b></p>'
            + callout('1 · What is ' + t + '?', c.summary)
            + '<div class="cards">'
            + '<div class="mini-card"><b>2 · Core concepts</b>' + bullet(c.points) + '</div>'
            + '<div class="mini-card"><b>3 · Why it matters for your job</b>' + bullet(c.applications) + '</div>'
            + '</div>'
            + '<div class="panel" style="margin-top:14px">'
            + '<b>4 · Step-by-step way to learn</b>'
            + bullet([
                'First understand the purpose and terminology of ' + t + '.',
                'Read the worked example and trace every step from input to output.',
                'Try the practice task without copying the example.',
                'Test an edge case, explain your result, then move to the lab.'
            ])
            + '</div>'
            + '<div class="cards">'
            + '<div class="mini-card"><b>5 · Worked example</b><pre style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-family:ui-monospace,monospace;font-size:12px;background:#222033;color:#fff;border-radius:12px;padding:12px;margin-top:10px">' + escape(String(c.example)) + '</pre></div>'
            + '<div class="mini-card"><b>6 · Practice task</b><p class="small" style="margin-top:10px">' + escape(String(c.practice)) + '</p><p class="small"><b>Goal:</b> produce a working answer and be able to explain your approach.</p></div>'
            + '</div>'
            + '<div class="cards">'
            + '<div class="mini-card"><b>7 · Common mistakes to avoid</b>' + bullet(c.mistakes) + '</div>'
            + '<div class="mini-card"><b>8 · Interview questions</b>' + bullet(c.interviewQs) + '</div>'
            + '</div>'
            + '<div class="panel" style="margin-top:14px;background:#fffdf4;border-color:#eee4a8">'
            + '<b>9 · Quick revision</b>' + bullet(c.revision)
            + '</div>'
            + '<div class="diagnosis-note"><b>10 · Study flow:</b> Read these notes → understand the example → solve the practice task → open the lab → complete the quick check → revise before assessment. Opening the page alone does not mark the topic complete.</div>'
            + '<div style="display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:14px">'
            + '<button class="primary" data-study-lab="' + escapeAttr(t) + '">Start lab →</button>'
            + '<button class="secondary" data-view="studyMaterials">Back to materials</button>'
            + '</div>'
            + '</div>';
    };
    // Make the material library clearly advertise that every topic has full notes.
    const oldStudyMaterials = window.studyMaterials;
    window.studyMaterials = function () {
        const route = typeof activeCareerRoute === 'function' ? activeCareerRoute() : null;
        const cats = typeof studyCatalog === 'function' ? studyCatalog() : [];
        const topics = cats.flatMap(c => c[1].map(t => ({
            topic: t, module: c[0]
        })));
        return '<div class="study-materials-shell"><div class="panel">'
            + '<span class="eyebrow">STUDY MATERIALS</span>'
            + '<h2>Complete notes for every subject</h2>'
            + '<p class="muted">Every subject in every module has its own study page with definitions, core concepts, examples, applications, common mistakes, interview questions, revision points and a practical task. The material follows your selected branch and target job.</p>'
            + '<div class="material-grid" style="margin-top:18px">'
            + topics.map(x => {
                const c = richMaterial(x.topic, route);
                return '<article class="material-card"><span class="level-chip">' + escape(x.module) + '</span><h3>' + escape(x.topic) + '</h3><p class="small">' + escape(c.summary) + '</p><div class="coding-tests"><b>Included in notes</b><p class="small" style="margin:7px 0 0">Definition · key concepts · worked example · applications · mistakes · interview questions · revision · practice</p></div><button class="secondary" style="margin-top:12px" data-study-open="' + escapeAttr(x.topic) + '">Study complete notes →</button></article>';
            }).join('')
            + '</div></div></div>';
    };
})();
</script>


<script>
// ========================================================================
// PLACEMENT360 MODULE: MIC PERMISSION & RECORD-ONLY AUDIO FLOW
// ========================================================================

/* V35: FINAL DIAGNOSTIC NEXT-BUTTON FIX
   - Diagnostic Next is never disabled by camera/focus/audio monitors.
   - MCQ/voice/practical questions may be skipped; skipped answers receive 0.
   - Spoken responses are recorded when available; otherwise the voice question is simply unattempted.
   - Practical code is captured even when the student does not run the preview.
   - Every move resets per-question voice/coding state so one question cannot affect the next.
*/
(function () {
    const diagnosticMode = () => state.screen === 'assessment' && state.assessment?.mode === 'diagnostic' && !state.diagnosis;
    const diagnosticQuestions = () => typeof currentQuestions === 'function' ? currentQuestions() : (typeof diagQs !== 'undefined' ? diagQs : []);
    function captureDiagnosticQuestion() {
        if (!diagnosticMode())
            return;
        const a = state.assessment, qs = diagnosticQuestions(), q = qs[a.q];
        if (!q)
            return;
        /* MCQ answers are already written by the answer-button handler. */
        if (q.type === 'voice') {
            a.answers = a.answers || {};
            a.answers[a.q] = {
                type: 'voice',
                attempted: !!a.voiceCompleted,
                score: a.voiceCompleted ? Number(a.voiceScore || 0) : 0
            };
        }
        else if (q.type === 'practical') {
            a.answers = a.answers || {};
            const editor = document.getElementById('codeBox');
            const code = editor ? String(editor.value || '') : String(a.codingSubmission || '');
            if (code.trim()) {
                a.answers[a.q] = {
                    type: 'code', code, attempted: true
                };
            }
            else if (a.practicalDone) {
                a.answers[a.q] = {
                    type: 'practicalChoice', attempted: true, correct: true
                };
            }
            else {
                a.answers[a.q] = {
                    type: 'code', code: '', attempted: false
                };
            }
        }
    }
    function resetQuestionState() {
        const a = state.assessment;
        a.voiceCompleted = false;
        a.voiceScore = 0;
        a.recording = false;
        a.backgroundSpeech = false;
        a.focusLocked = false;
        a.focusLockReason = '';
        a.codingRun = false;
        a.codingSubmission = '';
        a.codingTask = null;
        a.transientFocusUntil = 0;
        try {
            a.voiceRecognition?.stop?.();
        }
        catch (e) { }
        a.voiceRecognition = null;
        if (a.micStream?.getTracks) {
            try {
                a.micStream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
            }
            catch (e) { }
        }
        a.micStream = null;
        a.mic = false;
    }
    const previousFinalAction = action;
    action = function (a) {
        if (a === 'nextAssessment' && diagnosticMode()) {
            const qs = diagnosticQuestions();
            if (!qs.length)
                return;
            captureDiagnosticQuestion();
            const last = state.assessment.q >= qs.length - 1;
            if (last) {
                /* finishAssessment performs the final evaluation/report transition. */
                finishAssessment();
                return;
            }
            state.assessment.q += 1;
            resetQuestionState();
            state.assessment.faceFrameOk = true;
            state.assessment.backgroundSpeech = false;
            p360Persist?.();
            render();
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (diagnosticMode()) {
                    try {
                        attachVideo?.();
                    }
                    catch (e) { }
                }
            }, 60);
            return;
        }
        previousFinalAction(a);
    };
    const previousFinalBind = bind;
    bind = function () {
        previousFinalBind();
        if (!diagnosticMode())
            return;
        /* The diagnostic is allowed to move forward even when the response is blank. */
        const next = root.querySelector('[data-action="nextAssessment"]');
        if (next) {
            next.disabled = false;
            next.removeAttribute('disabled');
            next.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
            next.style.opacity = '1';
            next.title = 'Move to the next diagnostic question. Unanswered questions receive 0.';
        }
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-answer],[data-practical-choice],[data-action="recordVoice"]').forEach(b => {
            b.disabled = false;
        });
    };
    const previousFinalRender = render;
    render = function () {
        previousFinalRender();
        if (!diagnosticMode())
            return;
        const next = root.querySelector('[data-action="nextAssessment"]');
        if (next) {
            next.disabled = false;
            next.removeAttribute('disabled');
            next.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
            next.style.opacity = '1';
        }
    };
    /* Catch any final interval/monitor that tries to disable the diagnostic Next button. */
    setInterval(() => {
        if (!diagnosticMode())
            return;
        const next = root.querySelector('[data-action="nextAssessment"]');
        if (next && next.disabled) {
            next.disabled = false;
            next.removeAttribute('disabled');
            next.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
            next.style.opacity = '1';
        }
    }, 200);
})();
</script>


<script>
// ========================================================================
// PLACEMENT360 MODULE: ADAPTIVE CODING CHALLENGE
// ========================================================================

/* V35: JOB/BRANCH-SPECIFIC PRACTICE CHECKLISTS
   Every non-Python career route uses its existing route.labs questions in the
   same checkbox style as the Python Fundamentals practice checklist.
*/
(function () {
    if (typeof state === 'undefined' || typeof root === 'undefined')
        return;
    state.jobPracticeChecks = (state.jobPracticeChecks && typeof state.jobPracticeChecks === 'object') ? state.jobPracticeChecks : {};
    function currentRoute() {
        try {
            return typeof activeCareerRoute === 'function' ? activeCareerRoute() : null;
        }
        catch (e) {
            return null;
        }
    }
    function routePracticeState(route) {
        if (!route)
            return {
                checks: [], key: ''
            };
        const key = String(route.id || route.job || 'general-graduate');
        const total = Array.isArray(route.labs) ? route.labs.length : 0;
        let checks = Array.isArray(state.jobPracticeChecks[key]) ? state.jobPracticeChecks[key].slice(0, total) : [];
        while (checks.length < total)
            checks.push(false);
        state.jobPracticeChecks[key] = checks;
        return {
            checks, key
        };
    }
    function jobPracticeChecklistView() {
        const route = currentRoute();
        if (!route || !Array.isArray(route.labs))
            return null;
        const data = routePracticeState(route);
        const labs = route.labs;
        const done = data.checks.filter(Boolean).length;
        const pct = Math.round(done / Math.max(1, labs.length) * 100);
        const rows = labs.map((lab, i) => {
            const checked = !!data.checks[i];
            const topic = lab.topic || lab.title || ('Problem ' + (i + 1));
            const prompt = lab.prompt || lab.title || 'Complete this practical task.';
            const tests = lab.tests || '';
            return '<label class="job-practice-check" style="display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start;padding:15px 14px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;background:#fff;margin:10px 0;cursor:pointer">'
                + '<input type="checkbox" data-job-practice-check="' + i + '" ' + (checked ? 'checked' : '') + ' style="width:19px;height:19px;margin-top:3px;accent-color:var(--purple);flex:0 0 auto">'
                + '<span style="display:block;flex:1">'
                + '<span class="level-chip">PRACTICAL ' + (i + 1) + '</span>'
                + '<b style="display:block;font-size:17px;margin:8px 0 5px">' + escape(topic) + '</b>'
                + '<span class="small" style="display:block;color:var(--ink)">' + escape(prompt) + '</span>'
                + (tests ? '<span class="small" style="display:block;margin-top:8px"><b>Check:</b> ' + escape(tests) + '</span>' : '')
                + '</span></label>';
        }).join('');
        return '<div class="panel lab-session job-practice-session">'
            + '<div class="panel-head"><div><span class="eyebrow">' + escape(route.job.toUpperCase()) + ' · PRACTICE PROBLEMS</span><h2>Job-focused practice checklist</h2><p class="muted">Complete these problems for your selected branch and target job. Tick a problem only after you actually solve or work through it.</p></div><span class="tag">' + done + '/' + labs.length + ' completed</span></div>'
            + '<div class="panel" style="margin-top:16px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbf9ff,#f1f9ff)">'
            + '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;align-items:center"><div><b>Practice progress</b><div class="small">These questions come from your current ' + escape(route.job) + ' learning route.</div></div><strong>' + pct + '%</strong></div>'
            + '<div class="skillbar" style="margin-top:10px"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div></div>'
            + '<div style="margin-top:18px">' + rows + '</div>'
            + '<div class="diagnosis-note" style="margin-top:18px"><b>Recommended flow:</b> Read the related study material → work through the example → solve the problem → tick it as completed → move to the next problem. Only real practice evidence should be counted.</div>'
            + '<div style="display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:18px">'
            + '<button class="secondary" data-action="resetJobPractice">Reset practice checklist</button>'
            + '<button class="secondary" data-view="studyMaterials">Back to study materials</button>'
            + '<button class="primary" data-view="labs">Back to practice labs</button>'
            + '</div></div>';
    }
    const previousLabView = labView;
    labView = function () {
        const topic = String(state.activeLab || state.activeStudyTopic || '');
        /* Preserve the original 30-question Python checklist exactly. */
        if (topic.toLowerCase() === 'python fundamentals')
            return previousLabView();
        const route = currentRoute();
        if (route && route.labs && route.labs.length)
            return jobPracticeChecklistView();
        return previousLabView();
    };
    /* Every Start Lab button on a career route opens the checkbox checklist. */
    const previousBind = bind;
    bind = function () {
        previousBind();
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-study-lab]').forEach(btn => {
            btn.onclick = () => {
                const t = btn.dataset.studyLab || '';
                state.activeLab = t;
                state.activeStudyTopic = t;
                const route = currentRoute();
                if (route && route.labs && route.labs.some(x => String(x.topic).toLowerCase() === String(t).toLowerCase()) && String(t).toLowerCase() !== 'python fundamentals') {
                    routePracticeState(route);
                    state.view = 'labSession';
                    state.screen = 'app';
                    markStudySessionStart();
                    render();
                    return;
                }
                /* Python and any legacy lab retain their original behaviour. */
                state.view = 'labSession';
                state.screen = 'app';
                markStudySessionStart();
                render();
            };
        });
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-job-practice-check]').forEach(box => {
            box.onchange = () => {
                const route = currentRoute();
                if (!route)
                    return;
                const data = routePracticeState(route);
                const i = Number(box.dataset.jobPracticeCheck);
                if (Number.isInteger(i) && i >= 0 && i < data.checks.length) {
                    data.checks[i] = !!box.checked;
                    state.jobPracticeChecks[data.key] = data.checks;
                    /* Keep lab status/evidence in sync with the number of completed problems. */
                    const lab = route.labs[i];
                    if (lab && box.checked)
                        state.labDone[studyKey(lab.topic)] = true;
                    else if (lab)
                        state.labDone[studyKey(lab.topic)] = false;
                    p360Persist();
                    render();
                }
            };
        });
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-action="resetJobPractice"]').forEach(btn => btn.onclick = () => {
            const route = currentRoute();
            if (!route)
                return;
            const data = routePracticeState(route);
            data.checks = data.checks.map(() => false);
            state.jobPracticeChecks[data.key] = data.checks;
            (route.labs || []).forEach(l => { state.labDone[studyKey(l.topic)] = false; });
            p360Persist();
            showToast(route.job + ' practice checklist reset.');
            render();
        });
    };
    const previousPersist = p360Persist;
    p360Persist = function () {
        previousPersist();
        if (!state.userEmail)
            return;
        try {
            const key = p360UserKey(state.userEmail), old = p360LoadUser(state.userEmail) || {};
            localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify({
                ...old, jobPracticeChecks: state.jobPracticeChecks || {}
            }));
        }
        catch (e) { }
    };
    const previousApplyUser = p360ApplyUser;
    p360ApplyUser = function (u) {
        previousApplyUser(u);
        state.jobPracticeChecks = (u && u.jobPracticeChecks && typeof u.jobPracticeChecks === 'object') ? u.jobPracticeChecks : {};
    };
})();
</script>


<script>
// ========================================================================
// PLACEMENT360 MODULE: JOB-SPECIFIC STUDY MATERIAL
// ========================================================================

/* V36: visible Back button for every sidebar feature */
(function () {
    function addFeatureBack() {
        const app = document.getElementById('app');
        if (!app)
            return;
        const content = app.querySelector('.content');
        if (!content)
            return;
        const active = app.querySelector('.sidebar .side-btn.active');
        const isDashboard = !!(active && /dashboard/i.test(active.textContent || ''));
        if (isDashboard)
            return;
        if (content.querySelector('.feature-back-button'))
            return;
        const bar = document.createElement('div');
        bar.className = 'feature-back-button';
        bar.style.cssText = 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin:0 0 14px;padding:0;position:relative;z-index:5';
        const btn = document.createElement('button');
        btn.type = 'button';
        btn.className = 'secondary';
        btn.style.cssText = 'padding:9px 14px;border-radius:12px;font-weight:850';
        btn.textContent = '← Back';
        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
            const top = app.querySelector('.topbar [data-action="back"]');
            if (top)
                top.click();
            else {
                const dash = app.querySelector('.sidebar .side-btn[data-view="dashboard"]');
                if (dash)
                    dash.click();
            }
        });
        bar.appendChild(btn);
        content.insertBefore(bar, content.firstChild);
    }
    addFeatureBack();
    const app = document.getElementById('app');
    if (app) {
        new MutationObserver(function () { addFeatureBack(); }).observe(app, {
            childList: true, subtree: true
        });
    }
})();
</script>


<script>
// ========================================================================
// PLACEMENT360 MODULE: FINAL DIAGNOSTIC NEXT-BUTTON FIX
// ========================================================================

/* V36: RELEASE ASSESSMENT MEDIA + FAST DIAGNOSTIC RESULT
   Stops active camera/microphone/screen-share capture and exits fullscreen when a
   diagnostic or assessment ends. Browser site permission settings themselves are
   controlled by the browser; this ends the active capture session immediately.
*/
(function () {
    const priorStopStreamsV36 = stopStreams;
    stopStreams = function () {
        try {
            priorStopStreamsV36();
        }
        catch (e) { }
        const a = state.assessment || {};
        [
            'stream', 'cameraStream', 'micStream', 'displayStream'
        ].forEach(key => {
            const stream = a[key];
            try {
                stream?.getTracks?.().forEach(track => { track.enabled = false; track.stop(); });
            }
            catch (e) { }
            a[key] = null;
        });
        try {
            a.micTrack?.stop?.();
        }
        catch (e) { }
        a.micTrack = null;
        a.micAvailable = false;
        a.mic = false;
        a.camera = false;
        a.screen = false;
        a.recording = false;
        try {
            a.voiceRecorder?.stop?.();
        }
        catch (e) { }
        a.voiceRecorder = null;
        if (a.voiceTimer) {
            clearTimeout(a.voiceTimer);
            a.voiceTimer = null;
        }
        if (a.audioTimer) {
            clearInterval(a.audioTimer);
            a.audioTimer = null;
        }
        if (a.frameMonitor) {
            clearInterval(a.frameMonitor);
            a.frameMonitor = null;
        }
        try {
            a.audioMonitor?.ctx?.close?.();
        }
        catch (e) { }
        a.audioMonitor = null;
        const video = document.getElementById('camVideo');
        if (video) {
            try {
                video.pause?.();
            }
            catch (e) { }
            try {
                video.srcObject = null;
            }
            catch (e) { }
        }
        a.fullscreen = false;
        document.body.classList.remove('p360-exam-mode');
        try {
            if (document.fullscreenElement && document.exitFullscreen)
                document.exitFullscreen().catch(() => { });
        }
        catch (e) { }
    };
    /* Make diagnostic completion release media before any result-analysis UI appears. */
    const priorFinishDiagnosticV36 = finishAssessment;
    finishAssessment = function () {
        try {
            stopStreams();
        }
        catch (e) { }
        return priorFinishDiagnosticV36();
    };
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
